// Compatibility discovery never evaluates downloaded source or calls candidate functions.
function compatTokens(source){
 const keywords=new Set('function return const let var if else for of in while do switch case break continue throw try catch finally new this typeof instanceof void delete class extends static get set async await yield true false null undefined'.split(' '));
 const ids=new Map();
 const literals=source.replace(/\\u([0-9a-f]{4})|\\x([0-9a-f]{2})/gi,(_,u,x)=>String.fromCharCode(parseInt(u||x,16)));
 const tokens=literals.match(/"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|'(?:\\.|[^'\\])*'|`(?:\\.|[^`\\])*`|\/\/[^\n]*|\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*|0x[0-9a-f]+|\d+(?:\.\d+)?|[^\s]/gi)||[];
 return tokens.filter(t=>!t.startsWith('//')&&!t.startsWith('/*')).map(t=>{
  if(t[0]==='"'||t[0]==="'")return 'S'+t.slice(1,-1);
  if(/^[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*$/.test(t)&&!keywords.has(t)){if(!ids.has(t))ids.set(t,ids.size);return 'I'+ids.get(t);}
  return t;
 }).join('|');
}
function compatSignature(source){
 const normalized=compatTokens(source);let a=2166136261,b=5381;
 for(let i=0;i<normalized.length;i++){const c=normalized.charCodeAt(i);a=Math.imul(a^c,16777619);b=Math.imul(b,33)^c;}
 return normalized.length+':'+(a>>>0).toString(16)+':'+(b>>>0).toString(16);
}
function compatContext(fn,sources){
 const name=fn.name;if(!/^[\w$]+$/.test(name))return [];
 const escaped=name.replace(/[$]/g,'\\$'),re=new RegExp('function\\s+'+escaped+'\\s*\\(','g');
 const result=[];
 for(const source of sources){re.lastIndex=0;let match;while((match=re.exec(source))){result.push(compatSignature(source.slice(match.index,match.index+4096)));if(result.length>8)return [];}}
 return result;
}
const COMPAT_FIELDS={
 ar:['Bow','Boomerang','ShooterHat','IllusionHat'],as:['Player','Scaffold','Trap','Wolf'],
 bS:['slots','activeIndex','activeProgress'],bW:['cursor','hatType','shieldType','shieldDurability'],
 bd:['scene','paused','nickname'],bq:['dayHour'],c9:['health','maxHealth','ping','alive'],
 cP:['localEntityId','entries'],cQ:['players'],cR:['targetId'],ch:['toasts'],dT:['objects','terrain','worldWidth','worldHeight']
};
function compatContract(key,value){
 if(COMPAT_FIELDS[key])return value&&typeof value==='object'&&COMPAT_FIELDS[key].every(k=>k in value)&&
   (!['ar','as'].includes(key)||COMPAT_FIELDS[key].every(k=>Number.isInteger(value[k])));
 if(key==='eC')return typeof value==='function'&&['bind','onEntityAdded','onEntityRemoved','create'].every(k=>typeof value.prototype?.[k]==='function');
 if(key==='eL')return value&&typeof value.createSkeleton==='function';
 return typeof value==='function';
}
function compatResolve(exports,sources,baseline){
 const entries=Object.entries(exports),resolved={},missing=[];
 for(const [key,rule] of Object.entries(baseline)){
  const variants=rule.variants||[rule];
  const candidates=entries.filter(([,value])=>{
   if(!compatContract(key,value))return false;
   if(variants.some(v=>!v.signature))return true;
   const signature=compatSignature(Function.prototype.toString.call(value));
   return variants.some(v=>v.signature===signature);
  });
  let selected=candidates;
  if(selected.length!==1&&variants.some(v=>v.context))selected=candidates.filter(([,fn])=>{
   const contexts=compatContext(fn,sources),signature=compatSignature(Function.prototype.toString.call(fn));
   return variants.some(v=>v.signature===signature&&contexts.includes(v.context));
  });
  if(selected.length===1)resolved[key]=selected[0][0];else missing.push(key+(selected.length?' (ambiguous)':' (missing)'));
 }
 return {resolved,missing};
}
async function compatReadSources(root){
 const queue=[root.href],seen=new Set(),sources=[];let total=0;
 while(queue.length&&seen.size<12){
  const href=queue.shift();if(seen.has(href))continue;seen.add(href);
  const controller=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),4000);
  try{
   const response=await fetch(href,{signal:controller.signal,credentials:'same-origin'});
   if(!response.ok)continue;
   const reader=response.body?.getReader();if(!reader)continue;
   const decoder=new TextDecoder();let source='',bytes=0;
   while(true){const part=await reader.read();if(part.done)break;bytes+=part.value.byteLength;total+=part.value.byteLength;
    if(bytes>6000000||total>16000000){await reader.cancel();throw Error('Compatibility source budget exceeded');}source+=decoder.decode(part.value,{stream:true});
   }
   source+=decoder.decode();sources.push(source);
   for(const match of source.matchAll(/["']((?:\.\/|\/assets\/)[\w./-]+\.js)["']/g)){
    const next=new URL(match[1],href);if(next.origin===root.origin&&next.pathname.startsWith('/assets/')&&!seen.has(next.href))queue.push(next.href);
   }
  }catch(error){console.debug('[KiroHack] Compatibility source:',href,error.message);}finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
  if(total>16000000)break;
 }
 return {sources,files:[...seen]};
}
function compatSchema(table,index,depth=0){
 if(depth>16||!Array.isArray(table[1]?.[index]))throw Error('Unsupported protocol schema');
 return table[1][index].map(field=>[field[0],field[1],field[2],field[4],field[2]===9?compatSchema(table,field[3],depth+1):null]);
}
function compatSchemaMatches(actual,expected,allowExtra=false){
 if(!Array.isArray(actual)||!Array.isArray(expected)||(!allowExtra&&actual.length!==expected.length))return false;
 if(new Set(actual.map(f=>f[1])).size!==actual.length)return false;
 return expected.every(wanted=>{
  const found=actual.find(f=>f[1]===wanted[1]);
  return found&&found.slice(0,4).every((v,i)=>v===wanted[i])&&
   (wanted[4]===null?found[4]===null:compatSchemaMatches(found[4],wanted[4],allowExtra));
 });
}
async function compatPackets(sources,expected){
 for(const source of sources){
  let attempts=0;
  for(const match of source.matchAll(/["']([A-Za-z0-9+/=]{1000,200000})["']/g)){
   if(++attempts>4)break;
   try{
    const bytes=Uint8Array.from(atob(match[1]),c=>c.charCodeAt(0));
    const reader=new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw')).getReader();
    let text='',size=0;const decoder=new TextDecoder();
    while(true){const chunk=await reader.read();if(chunk.done)break;size+=chunk.value.length;if(size>2000000){await reader.cancel();throw Error('Protocol too large');}text+=decoder.decode(chunk.value,{stream:true});}
    text+=decoder.decode();const table=JSON.parse(text);if(table[0]!==1||!Array.isArray(table[4]?.[0]))continue;
    const names=Object.fromEntries(table[4][0]),indices=new Map(table[3]);
    const matched=Object.entries(expected).filter(([name,schema])=>{try{return Number.isInteger(names[name])&&compatSchemaMatches(compatSchema(table,indices.get(names[name])),JSON.parse(schema),!name.startsWith('Input'));}catch{return false;}});
    if(matched.length)return Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(matched.map(([name])=>[name,names[name]])));
   }catch{}
  }
 }
 return null;
}

function compatInventoryBridge(key,packets){
 const names={c2:'InputSwapItemAndCursor',c3:'InputSwapShieldAndCursor',c4:'InputSwapHatAndCursor',c5:'InputDropFromCursor'};
 const name=names[key];if(!name||!Number.isInteger(packets?.[name]))return null;
 return function(...args){
  const registry=window.__t7Registry__,local=window.__t7LocalEntity__;
  const session=automationSessionReady(registry,local,performance.now());
  if(!session?.conn||session.conn.connected===false)throw Error('Equipment session is not ready');
  if((key==='c3'||key==='c4')&&!GAME.c7('inventory'))throw Error('Inventory is not open');
  const payload={};
  if(key==='c2'){
   if(!Number.isInteger(args[0])||args[0]<0||args[0]>4096)throw Error('Invalid inventory slot');
   payload.index=args[0];payload.half=args[1]===true;
  }
  session.conn.send(packets[name],payload);
 };
}

// Runs in the page realm, even when the userscript manager isolates its own window.
function installFpsPageBridge(node){
 const nativeRequest=window.requestAnimationFrame,nativeCancel=window.cancelAnimationFrame;
 const pending=new Map(),known=new WeakMap(),target=node.dataset.callback.replace(/\s+/g,'');let serial=0,disposed=false,active=false;
 const enabled=()=>!disposed&&!document.hidden&&node.isConnected&&node.dataset.enabled==='1'&&Date.now()-Number(node.dataset.heartbeat)<1500;
 function matches(callback){if(typeof callback!=='function')return false;if(!known.has(callback))known.set(callback,Function.prototype.toString.call(callback).replace(/\s+/g,'')===target);return known.get(callback);}
 function restoreCancel(){if(!active&&!pending.size&&window.cancelAnimationFrame===cancel)window.cancelAnimationFrame=nativeCancel;}
 function finish(id,callback,time){pending.delete(id);restoreCancel();callback(time);}
 function request(callback){
  if(!enabled()){probe();return nativeRequest.call(window,callback);}
  if(!matches(callback))return nativeRequest.call(window,callback);
  const id=--serial,entry={callback,timer:null,native:null};pending.set(id,entry);
  entry.timer=setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!pending.has(id))return;
   if(!enabled()){probe();entry.native=nativeRequest.call(window,time=>finish(id,callback,time));return;}
   finish(id,callback,performance.now());
  },4);
  return id;
 }
 function cancel(id){const entry=pending.get(id);if(!entry)return nativeCancel.call(window,id);clearTimeout(entry.timer);if(entry.native!==null)nativeCancel.call(window,entry.native);pending.delete(id);restoreCancel();}
 function probe(){
  const wanted=enabled();
  if(wanted&&!active){
   if(window.requestAnimationFrame!==nativeRequest||(window.cancelAnimationFrame!==nativeCancel&&window.cancelAnimationFrame!==cancel)){node.dataset.ready='0';return;}
   window.requestAnimationFrame=request;window.cancelAnimationFrame=cancel;active=true;
  }else if(!wanted&&active){active=false;if(window.requestAnimationFrame===request)window.requestAnimationFrame=nativeRequest;restoreCancel();}
  node.dataset.ready=disposed?'0':active?(window.requestAnimationFrame===request&&window.cancelAnimationFrame===cancel?'1':'0'):'1';
 }
 function stop(){if(disposed)return;disposed=true;probe();node.removeEventListener('kiro-fps-stop',stop);node.removeEventListener('kiro-fps-probe',probe);}
 node.addEventListener('kiro-fps-stop',stop);node.addEventListener('kiro-fps-probe',probe);probe();
}
const fpsSettings=Object.values(clientModule).find(v=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&v.values&&typeof v.values==='object'&&'frameRateLimit'in v.values);
const fpsCallbackSource=moduleURL.pathname==='/assets/main-VdJ18zLo.js'?'M0=>this[M3M(0x1a4e)](M0)':null;
const fpsBridge=document.createElement('script');fpsBridge.id='kiro-fps-page-bridge';fpsBridge.dataset.callback=fpsCallbackSource||'';fpsBridge.dataset.enabled='0';
if(fpsCallbackSource&&fpsSettings){
 fpsBridge.textContent='('+installFpsPageBridge.toString()+')(document.currentScript);';
 document.head.append(fpsBridge);
}
const fpsBridgeSupported=fpsBridge.dataset.ready==='1';
let fpsBridgeRetries=0,fpsBridgeRetryAt=0,fpsBridgeFailed=false;
function reconnectFpsBridge(){
 fpsBridge.dispatchEvent(new Event('kiro-fps-stop'));
 const script=document.createElement('script');
 script.textContent='('+installFpsPageBridge.toString()+')(document.getElementById("kiro-fps-page-bridge"));';
 document.head.append(script);script.remove();
}
function syncFpsBridge(){
 fpsBridge.dataset.enabled=CFG.freeFps&&fpsSettings?.values.frameRateLimit===0&&GAME.be.scene==='gameplay'&&!GAME.be.paused&&!document.hidden?'1':'0';
 fpsBridge.dataset.heartbeat=String(Date.now());
 fpsBridge.dispatchEvent(new Event('kiro-fps-probe'));
 if(!CFG.freeFps){fpsBridgeRetries=0;return;}
 if(fpsBridge.dataset.enabled!=='1'||fpsBridge.dataset.ready==='1'||!fpsBridgeSupported||fpsBridgeFailed)return;
 if(Date.now()<fpsBridgeRetryAt)return;
 if(fpsBridgeRetries<3){fpsBridgeRetries++;fpsBridgeRetryAt=Date.now()+2000;reconnectFpsBridge();return;}
 fpsBridgeFailed=true;CFG.freeFps=false;fpsBridge.dataset.enabled='0';
 const input=document.getElementById('t7freefps'),card=input?.closest('.t7card');
 if(input){input.checked=false;input.disabled=true;}
 if(card){card.classList.add('t7unavailable');for(const button of card.querySelectorAll('button'))button.disabled=true;
  if(!card.querySelector('.t7updatebadge')){const badge=document.createElement('span');badge.className='t7updatebadge';badge.textContent='?';badge.tabIndex=0;badge.title=uiLanguage==='en'?'Requires update':'Требует обновления';badge.setAttribute('aria-label',badge.title);card.querySelector('.t7cardheader').append(badge);}}

}
const fpsBridgeTimer=setInterval(syncFpsBridge,100);
cleanups.push(()=>{CFG.freeFps=false;clearInterval(fpsBridgeTimer);fpsBridge.dataset.enabled='0';fpsBridge.dispatchEvent(new Event('kiro-fps-stop'));fpsBridge.remove();});

function syncLocalPlayer() {
    const projection = typeof GAME.cB==='function'?GAME.cB():null;
    const id = GAME.d4.localEntityId;
    window.__pP__ = GAME.cO;
    window.__rU__ = GAME.dT;
    window.__localId__ = id == null ? null : String(id);
    window.__t7Projection__ = projection ?? null;
    const position = id == null ? null : projection?.entityPos?.(String(id));
    window.__t7LocalEntity__ = position
        ? {id:String(id), rx:position.x, ry:position.y} : null;
    return window.__t7LocalEntity__;
}
function localTracerOrigin() {
    const local = window.__t7LocalEntity__;
    if (!local || !Number.isFinite(local.rx) || !Number.isFinite(local.ry)) return null;
    const point = w2s(local.rx, local.ry);
    return Number.isFinite(point.sx) && Number.isFinite(point.sy) ? point : null;
}

window.__T7_FULL_LOCAL__ = true;

// Capture the active registry through the getter used by camera-follow.
const registryPrototype = GAME.eC?.prototype;
const localDescriptor = registryPrototype && Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(registryPrototype, 'local');
if (localDescriptor?.get && localDescriptor.configurable) {
    const originalLocal = localDescriptor.get;
    const patchedDescriptor = {
        ...localDescriptor,
        get() {
            const entity = originalLocal.call(this);
            if (entity?.id != null && String(entity.id) === String(GAME.d4.localEntityId))
                window.__t7Registry__ = this;
            return entity;
        }
    };
    Object.defineProperty(registryPrototype,'local',patchedDescriptor);
    cleanups.push(()=>{if(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(registryPrototype,'local')?.get===patchedDescriptor.get)Object.defineProperty(registryPrototype,'local',localDescriptor);});
}

// OWNER HELPERS BEGIN
function idOf(value) {
    if (typeof value === 'number' && (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value <= 0)) return null;
    if (!['string', 'number', 'bigint'].includes(typeof value)) return null;
    const id = String(value);
    return id && id !== '0' ? id : null;
}
function cleanName(value) {
    return typeof value === 'string'
        ? value.replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g, ' ').trim() : '';
}
// Decoded Leaderboard packets update GAME.d4.entries. Retain names within one
// registry/local-ID session; never interpret minimap friend/flags as an owner ID.
const OWNER_CACHE={registry:null,localId:null,names:new Map(),parents:new Map()};
function ownerCacheSession() {
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__,localId=idOf(GAME.d4.localEntityId);
    const active=registry && localId && idOf(registry.localId)===localId;
    const currentRegistry=active?registry:null,currentId=active?localId:null;
    if(OWNER_CACHE.registry!==currentRegistry || OWNER_CACHE.localId!==currentId){
        OWNER_CACHE.registry=currentRegistry;OWNER_CACHE.localId=currentId;
        OWNER_CACHE.names.clear();OWNER_CACHE.parents.clear();
    }
    return Boolean(active);
}
function rememberLeaderboard(entries) {
    if(!ownerCacheSession())return;
    for(const entry of entries || []){
        const id=idOf(entry?.id),name=cleanName(entry?.name)||cleanName(entry?.rawName);
        if(id && name)OWNER_CACHE.names.set(id,name);
    }
}
function parentFromEntity(entity) {
    const data=entity.state?.get?.('ChildComponentRepl');
    if(data && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(data,'parent_id')){
        const field=data.parent_id;
        return {known:true,id:idOf(field && typeof field==='object' && 'value' in field ? field.value : field)};
    }
    if(entity.parentId!=null)return {known:true,id:idOf(entity.parentId)};
    return {known:false,id:null};
}
function syncOwnerCache() {
    if(!ownerCacheSession())return;
    rememberLeaderboard(GAME.d4.entries);
    // Live player state can supply a newer name than an older leaderboard snapshot.
    for(const [raw,player] of Object.entries(GAME.cO.players || {})){
        const id=idOf(raw),name=cleanName(player?.name);
        if(id && name)OWNER_CACHE.names.set(id,name);
    }
    for(const entity of OWNER_CACHE.registry.entities.values()){
        const id=idOf(entity.id);if(!id)continue;
        const parent=parentFromEntity(entity);
        if(parent.known){
            if(parent.id)OWNER_CACHE.parents.set(id,parent.id);
            else OWNER_CACHE.parents.delete(id);
        }
    }
}
function playerNames() {
    syncOwnerCache();
    return OWNER_CACHE.names;
}
function ownerOf(entity,registry,localId,names) {
    if(!ownerCacheSession())return {id:null,name:null};
    const entityId=idOf(entity.id),parent=parentFromEntity(entity);
    let id=parent.known?parent.id:(entityId?OWNER_CACHE.parents.get(entityId):null);
    if(!id && !parent.known && registry?.localChildren?.has?.(entity.id))id=localId;
    if(entityId && parent.known){
        if(id)OWNER_CACHE.parents.set(entityId,id);else OWNER_CACHE.parents.delete(entityId);
    }
    return id?{id,name:names.get(id)||null}:{id:null,name:null};
}
// Watch the decoded store instead of installing another binary WebSocket decoder.
if(typeof GAME.w==='function')cleanups.push(GAME.w(
    ()=>GAME.d4.entries,
    entries=>rememberLeaderboard(entries),
    {deep:true,flush:'sync'}
));

function ownerText(owner) {
    if (!owner?.id) return uiText('Владелец неизвестен');
    return owner.name
        ? `: ${owner.name}`
        : uiText(': ник недоступен');
}
// OWNER HELPERS END

function traceEnabled(kind){return TRACE_SELECT.types.has(tracerFamily(kind));}

function targetKind(value) {
    const name=constructionTypeName(value);
    if(name && (traceEnabled(name)||name==='ForeignHeartStone'))return name;
    return null;
}
function tracerFamily(kind){return kind==='ForeignHeartStone'?'Heartstone':kind;}
function constructionTypeName(value){
    const raw=apValue(value);
    if(Number.isInteger(raw)&&raw>0){
        const name=GAME.at?.[raw];
        if(typeof name==='string')return name;
        return Object.keys(GAME.at||{}).find(key=>GAME.at[key]===raw)||null;
    }
    if(typeof raw==='string'&&typeof GAME.at?.[raw]==='number')return raw;
    return null;
}
function futureBuildingType(entity){
    // Decode any confirmed construction target, not just tracer categories.
    // No prediction from inventory selection, cursor preview or previous entities.
    const scaffold=entity.state?.get?.('ScaffoldComponentRepl');
    return scaffold?constructionTypeName(scaffold.ty):null;
}
const TRACER_MOB_LABELS={Bear:'Медведь',WhiteBear:'Белый медведь',Wolf:'Волк',Cow:'Корова',GoldenCow:'Золотая корова',Chicken:'Курица',Pig:'Свинья',Rabbit:'Кролик',Bat:'Летучая мышь',Penguin:'Пингвин',Boar:'Кабан',Deer:'Олень',Turkey:'Индейка',Tortoise:'Черепаха',Skeleton:'Скелет',SnowMan:'Снеговик',Zombie:'Зомби',RedSpider:'Красный паук',Spider:'Паук',SpiderMother:'Паучиха',Hedgehog:'Ёж',Buffalo:'Буйвол',Werewolf:'Оборотень',Slime:'Слизень',HermitCrab:'Рак-отшельник'};
function tracerActualKind(entity){
    if(typeof entity?.typeName==='string'&&entity.typeName)return entity.typeName;
    const kind=constructionTypeName(entity?.type);
    if(kind)return kind;
    // An explicit unknown type must not inherit the label of a shared visual prefab.
    if(entity?.type!=null)return null;
    const prefab=entity?.prefab?.type;
    return typeof prefab==='string'?prefab:constructionTypeName(prefab);
}
function tracerLabel(kind){
    if(TRACE_MOBS.includes(kind))return TRACER_MOB_LABELS[kind]||UI_RU[kind]||kind;
    return TRACE_RESOURCES.find(x=>x.type===kind)?.label||BUILDING_ALERT_CATALOG.find(x=>x.type===kind)?.label||kind;
}
function tracerEntityKind(entity){
    const kind=tracerActualKind(entity);
    if(kind==='Scaffold'){
        const future=futureBuildingType(entity);
        return future?{kind:future,building:true}:null;
    }
    // A known unselected prefab must never fall through to another numeric type.
    if(kind)return traceEnabled(kind)?{kind,building:false}:null;
    if(entity.type!=null||entity.typeName||entity.prefab?.category==='character')return null;
    const future=futureBuildingType(entity);
    return future?{kind:future,building:true}:null;
}
function tracerEntityPosition(entity){
    // Child replication can create a registry entry before its position arrives.
    // Zero is a valid coordinate, but a default root position is not evidence.
    const transform=entity.state?.get?.('TransformComponentRepl');
    if(apValue(transform?.position)==null)return null;
    return Number.isFinite(entity.rx)&&Number.isFinite(entity.ry)
        ?{x:entity.rx,y:entity.ry}:null;
}
function groundStackCount(entity){
    const data=entity.state?.get?.('DropComponentRepl');
    const count=apValue(data?.item_count);
    return Number.isSafeInteger(count)&&count>0?count:null;
}
function drawGroundItemCounts(ctx,width,height){
    if(!CFG.groundItemCounts||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return;
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    if(!registry||registry.localId==null||String(registry.localId)!==window.__localId__)return;
    ctx.save();
    try{
        ctx.font='bold 13px Arial';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
        ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.85)';ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
        for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
            const count=groundStackCount(entity);if(count===null)continue;
            const position=tracerEntityPosition(entity);if(!position)continue;
            const point=w2s(position.x,position.y);
            if(!Number.isFinite(point.sx)||!Number.isFinite(point.sy)||point.sx<0||point.sx>width||point.sy<0||point.sy>height)continue;
            const text=String(count),y=point.sy;
            ctx.strokeText(text,point.sx,y);ctx.fillText(text,point.sx,y);
        }
    }finally{ctx.restore();}
}
const BUILD_TIMERS={registry:null,records:new Map()};
function clearBuildTimers(){for(const r of BUILD_TIMERS.records.values())r.off?.();BUILD_TIMERS.records.clear();BUILD_TIMERS.registry=null;}
cleanups.push(clearBuildTimers);
function updateBuildDeadline(record,now){
    const data=record.entity.state?.get?.('ScaffoldComponentRepl');
    const seconds=apValue(data?.timeout);
    if(typeof seconds==='number'&&Number.isFinite(seconds)&&seconds>=0)record.deadline=now+seconds*1000;
}
function buildSecondsLeft(record,now){return Number.isFinite(record.deadline)?Math.max(0,Math.ceil((record.deadline-now)/1000)):null;}
function drawBuildTimers(ctx,width,height){
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    if(!CFG.buildTimers||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!registry||registry.localId==null||String(registry.localId)!==window.__localId__){if(BUILD_TIMERS.registry)clearBuildTimers();return;}
    if(BUILD_TIMERS.registry!==registry){clearBuildTimers();BUILD_TIMERS.registry=registry;}
    const now=performance.now(),live=new Set();
    ctx.save();
    try{
      ctx.font='10px Arial';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.8)';ctx.fillStyle='#d6efdf';
      for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
        const kind=entity.prefab?.type;
        if(kind&&kind!=='Scaffold')continue;
        const data=entity.state?.get?.('ScaffoldComponentRepl');if(!data)continue;
        live.add(entity);let record=BUILD_TIMERS.records.get(entity);
        if(!record){record={entity,deadline:null,off:null,message:data};BUILD_TIMERS.records.set(entity,record);updateBuildDeadline(record,now);
          record.off=entity.state?.subscribe?.('ScaffoldComponentRepl',()=>{record.message=entity.state.get('ScaffoldComponentRepl');updateBuildDeadline(record,performance.now());});
        }else if(record.message!==data){record.message=data;updateBuildDeadline(record,now);}
        const seconds=buildSecondsLeft(record,now),point=tracerEntityPosition(entity);if(seconds===null||!point)continue;
        const screen=w2s(point.x,point.y),x=screen.sx,y=screen.sy+28;
        if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y)||x<14||x>width-14||y<8||y>height-8)continue;
        const label=seconds+(uiLanguage==='en'?' s':' с');ctx.strokeText(label,x,y);ctx.fillText(label,x,y);
      }
    }finally{ctx.restore();}
    for(const [entity,record] of BUILD_TIMERS.records)if(!live.has(entity)){record.off?.();BUILD_TIMERS.records.delete(entity);}
}
function collectTracerTargets() {
    const targets=[],names=CFG.owners?playerNames():new Map();
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    const active=registry&&registry.localId!=null&&String(registry.localId)===window.__localId__;
    const map=GAME.dT;
    const enabled=traceEnabled;
    if(active){
        for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
            if(!entity)continue;
            const info=tracerEntityKind(entity),point=tracerEntityPosition(entity);
            if(!info||!point||(info.building?!TRACE_SELECT.scaffolds:!enabled(info.kind)))continue;
            targets.push({...info,...point,id:idOf(entity.id),source:'entity',
                owner:CFG.owners&&!TRACE_MOBS.includes(info.kind)&&!TRACE_RESOURCES.some(x=>x.type===info.kind)
                    ?ownerOf(entity,registry,idOf(window.__localId__),names):{id:null,name:null}});
        }
    }
    if(!(map.worldWidth>0&&map.worldHeight>0))return targets;
    const matched=new Set();
    // Minimap coordinates are quantized to 512 steps in each axis.
    const toleranceX=map.worldWidth/512+0.05,toleranceY=map.worldHeight/512+0.05;
    for(const item of map.objects||[]){
        // These flags are rendered as special/player markers by the game, not typed buildings.
        if((Number(item.flags)&0x43)||Number(item.friend)===0x10)continue;
        const kind=targetKind(item.ty);
        if(!kind||!enabled(kind))continue;
        const nx=Number.isFinite(item.nx)?item.nx:item.x/512;
        const ny=Number.isFinite(item.ny)?item.ny:item.y/512;
        if(!Number.isFinite(nx)||!Number.isFinite(ny)||nx<0||ny<0||nx>1||ny>1)continue;
        const x=nx*map.worldWidth,y=ny*map.worldHeight,id=idOf(item.id??item.entityId);
        let duplicate=id?targets.find(t=>t.id===id):null;
        if(!id){
            // Match only one marker to one entity. Never transfer ownership by proximity.
            const candidates=targets.filter(t=>t.source==='entity'&&!matched.has(t)&&
                tracerFamily(t.kind)===tracerFamily(kind)&&
                Math.abs(t.x-x)<=toleranceX&&Math.abs(t.y-y)<=toleranceY);
            candidates.sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-x,a.y-y)-Math.hypot(b.x-x,b.y-y));
            duplicate=candidates[0];
        }
        if(duplicate){matched.add(duplicate);continue;}
        targets.push({kind,x,y,id,building:false,source:'map',owner:
            id&&CFG.owners&&!TRACE_MOBS.includes(kind)&&!TRACE_RESOURCES.some(x=>x.type===kind)
                ?ownerOf({id},active?registry:null,idOf(window.__localId__),names):{id:null,name:null}});
    }
    return targets;
}

// Capture existing and future cameras through the shared Three camera prototype.
// No changes to Object.prototype, Object.freeze, Reflect.set, Proxy or WebSocket.
const cameraStates=new Map();
const runtimeURLs=[...new Set([
    ...[...document.querySelectorAll('script[src],link[rel="modulepreload"][href]')].map(el=>el.src||el.href),
    ...performance.getEntriesByType('resource').map(entry=>entry.name)
].map(url=>new URL(url,location.href)).filter(url=>url.origin===location.origin&&
    /^\/assets\/three-runtime-[\w-]+\.js$/.test(url.pathname)).map(url=>url.href))];

const three=runtimeURLs.length===1?await import(runtimeURLs[0]).catch(()=>({})):{};
if(disposed)return;
const cameraExports=Object.values(three).filter(v=>typeof v==='function'&&v.prototype?.updateProjectionMatrix&&/isOrthographicCamera/.test(Function.prototype.toString.call(v)));
const Camera=cameraExports.length===1?cameraExports[0]:null;
const cp=Camera?.prototype||{};
const projectionOriginal=cp.updateProjectionMatrix;
function cameraProjection(){
    if(disposed)return projectionOriginal.apply(this,arguments);
    window.__camera__=this;
    let state=cameraStates.get(this);
    const current=[this.left,this.right,this.top,this.bottom];
    if(!state){state={base:current,last:null};cameraStates.set(this,state);}
    if(!state.last||current.some((n,i)=>Math.abs(n-state.last[i])>1e-7))state.base=current;
    const [l,r,t,b]=state.base,cx=(l+r)/2,cy=(t+b)/2;
    const factor=CFG.zoomEnabled?CFG.zoom:1;const hw=(r-l)*factor/2,hh=(t-b)*factor/2;
    this.left=cx-hw;this.right=cx+hw;this.top=cy+hh;this.bottom=cy-hh;
    state.last=[this.left,this.right,this.top,this.bottom];
    return projectionOriginal.apply(this,arguments);
}
if(Camera)cp.updateProjectionMatrix=cameraProjection;
cleanups.push(()=>{if(cp.updateProjectionMatrix===cameraProjection)cp.updateProjectionMatrix=projectionOriginal;});
for(const method of ['updateMatrixWorld','updateWorldMatrix']){
    const own=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(cp,method),original=cp[method];
    if(typeof original!=='function')continue;
    const patched=function(){
        if(!disposed){window.__camera__=this;cameraProjection.call(this);}
        return original.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    Object.defineProperty(cp,method,{value:patched,writable:true,configurable:true});
    cleanups.push(()=>{if(cp[method]===patched){if(own)Object.defineProperty(cp,method,own);else delete cp[method];}});
}
cleanups.push(()=>{for(const [cam,state] of cameraStates){
    [cam.left,cam.right,cam.top,cam.bottom]=state.base;projectionOriginal.call(cam);
}cameraStates.clear();});

let mapLastSend=0,mapPending=false;
function _mapTick(){
    if(!CFG.mapEnabled||mapPending||Date.now()-mapLastSend<2000||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return;
    const local=window.__t7LocalEntity__,server=CFG.mapServer.trim();
    const name=local&&cleanName(GAME.cO.players?.[local.id]?.name);
    if(!local||!name||!server)return;
    if(!Number.isFinite(local.rx)||!Number.isFinite(local.ry))return;
    mapLastSend=Date.now();mapPending=true;
    fetch('https://dynast-map-6551.111vs111ss.workers.dev/api/update',{
        method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
        signal:AbortSignal.any([lifecycle.signal,AbortSignal.timeout(8000)]),
        body:JSON.stringify({server,playerName:name,x:local.rx,y:local.ry,timestamp:mapLastSend})
    }).then(r=>{if(!r.ok)throw Error('HTTP '+r.status);})
      .catch(e=>{if(!disposed)console.warn('[T7] Map update failed:',e.message);})
      .finally(()=>{mapPending=false;});
}

// Entity-addressed pickup through the verified InputInteract uint64 schema.
const AP = {registry:null, watched:new Map(), drops:new Map(), timers:new Map(), offAdded:null, status:'Выключен'};
function apValue(value) {
    return value && typeof value==='object' && 'value' in value ? value.value : value;
}
function apNear(entity) {
    const local=window.__t7LocalEntity__;
    return local && Number.isFinite(entity.rx) && Number.isFinite(entity.ry) &&
        Math.hypot(entity.rx-local.rx,entity.ry-local.ry)<=CFG.apRadius;
}
function apSchedule(drop,now) {
    if(!CFG.autoPickup || drop.started || now<(drop.retryAt||0) || !apNear(drop.entity))return;
    if(CFG.apItemType!=='' && String(drop.itemType)!==CFG.apItemType)return;
    drop.started=true;
    AP.timers.set(drop.id,{readyAt:now+CFG.apDelay,nextAt:now+CFG.apDelay,sent:0,total:CFG.apPackets,expiresAt:Infinity});
}
function apObserve(entity) {
    const id=String(entity.id), data=entity.state?.get?.('DropComponentRepl');
    if(!data){AP.drops.delete(id);AP.timers.delete(id);return;}
    const itemType=apValue(data.item_type);
    if(itemType===undefined || itemType===null)return;
    const hash=apValue(data.lock_hash);
    const locked=typeof hash==='string'?hash.length>0:null;
    const now=performance.now(), previous=AP.drops.get(id);
    const drop=previous || {id,entity,started:false,firstSeenAt:now,unlockedAt:null};
    if(previous?.isLocked===true && locked===false)drop.unlockedAt=now;
    drop.entity=entity;drop.itemType=itemType;drop.isLocked=locked;
    AP.drops.set(id,drop);
    // Start on appearance/update even while locked; do not wait for unlockedAt.
    apSchedule(drop,now);
}
function apWatch(entity) {
    if(!entity?.state?.subscribe || entity.id==null)return;
    const id=String(entity.id), existing=AP.watched.get(id);
    if(existing?.entity===entity)return;
    existing?.off?.();AP.drops.delete(id);AP.timers.delete(id);
    const record={entity,off:null};AP.watched.set(id,record);
    record.off=entity.state.subscribe('DropComponentRepl',()=>apObserve(entity));
    apObserve(entity);
}
function apResetRegistry() {
    AP.offAdded?.();AP.offAdded=null;
    for(const watched of AP.watched.values())watched.off?.();
    AP.watched.clear();AP.drops.clear();AP.timers.clear();AP.registry=null;
}
function apBindRegistry() {
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    const valid=registry && registry.localId!=null && String(registry.localId)===String(GAME.d4.localEntityId);
    if(!valid){if(AP.registry)apResetRegistry();return false;}
    if(AP.registry!==registry){
        apResetRegistry();AP.registry=registry;
        AP.offAdded=registry.onEntityAdded(apWatch);
        for(const entity of registry.entities.values())apWatch(entity);
    }
    for(const [id,record] of AP.watched){
        if(registry.entities.get(id)!==record.entity){
            record.off?.();AP.watched.delete(id);AP.drops.delete(id);AP.timers.delete(id);
        }
    }
    return true;
}
cleanups.push(apResetRegistry);
function apSetEnabled(enabled) {
    CFG.autoPickup=Boolean(enabled);AP.timers.clear();
    for(const drop of AP.drops.values())drop.started=false;
    AP.status=enabled?'Ожидание данных дропа':'Выключен';
    const toggle=document.getElementById('t7auto');if(toggle)toggle.checked=CFG.autoPickup;
}
function pickupEntityId(value){
    if(typeof value==='number'&&!Number.isSafeInteger(value))return null;
    if(!['string','number','bigint'].includes(typeof value)||!/^\d+$/.test(String(value)))return null;
    const id=BigInt(value);return id>0n&&id<=18446744073709551615n?id.toString():null;
}
function sendPickup(entityId){
    const id=pickupEntityId(entityId),registry=AP.registry;
    if(!id||!registry||registry!==window.__t7Registry__||String(registry.localId)!==String(GAME.d4.localEntityId)||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return false;
    const entity=registry.entities.get(id);
    if(!entity?.state?.get?.('DropComponentRepl')||!apNear(entity))return false;
    const conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry);
    if(!conn||conn.connected!==true||typeof conn.send!=='function')return false;
    // Verified in main-D55LcHW0.js: InputInteract=230, field 1=id (uint64).
    // Decimal string avoids rounding IDs above Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER.
    if(!Number.isInteger(compatPacketTypes?.InputInteract))return false;conn.send(compatPacketTypes.InputInteract,{id});return true;
}
window.__T7_LOCAL_API__.sendPickup=sendPickup;
function _apTick() {
    if(GEAR.tx)return;
    if(!apBindRegistry()){if(CFG.autoPickup)AP.status='Ожидание реестра сущностей';return;}
    if(!CFG.autoPickup||GAME.be?.scene!=='gameplay')return;
    const now=performance.now();
    for(const drop of AP.drops.values())apSchedule(drop,now);
    for(const [id,timer] of AP.timers){
        const drop=AP.drops.get(id);
        if(!drop){AP.timers.delete(id);continue;}
        if(now<timer.nextAt||!apNear(drop.entity))continue;
        try{if(!sendPickup(id))continue;}catch(error){AP.status='Ошибка подбора: '+error.message;timer.nextAt=now+250;continue;}
        timer.sent++;timer.nextAt=now;
        AP.status='Дроп: вызовы '+timer.sent+'/'+timer.total;
        if(timer.sent>=timer.total)AP.timers.delete(id);
        // Each drop gets at most one send per animation frame, independent of targetId.
    }
}

function applyZoom(){const cam=window.__camera__;if(Camera&&cam)cameraProjection.call(cam);}
function w2s(wx, wy) {
    const projection = window.__t7Projection__;
    const canvas = document.getElementById('three-canvas');
    if (!projection || typeof projection.worldToScreen !== 'function' || !canvas)
        return {sx:NaN, sy:NaN};
    const point = projection.worldToScreen(wx, wy);
    const viewport = projection.viewport();
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!(viewport.width > 0 && viewport.height > 0 && rect.width > 0 && rect.height > 0))
        return {sx:NaN, sy:NaN};
    return {
        sx:rect.left + point.sx * rect.width / viewport.width,
        sy:rect.top + point.sy * rect.height / viewport.height
    };
}
// Local daylight override; retains server time for restoration.
const DAYLIGHT={store:null,descriptor:null,getter:null,hour:null};
function setDaylight(enabled){
    if(!enabled){
        if(!DAYLIGHT.store)return;
        const store=DAYLIGHT.store;
        if(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(store,'dayHour')?.get===DAYLIGHT.getter)
            Object.defineProperty(store,'dayHour',{...DAYLIGHT.descriptor,value:DAYLIGHT.hour});
        store.dayTimeRevision++;
        DAYLIGHT.store=null;return;
    }
    if(DAYLIGHT.store)return;
    const store=client.bq;
    const descriptor=store&&Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(store,'dayHour');
    if(!descriptor||!('value'in descriptor)||!descriptor.configurable||!descriptor.writable||
        !Number.isFinite(store.dayHour)||!Number.isFinite(store.dayTimeRevision))
        throw Error('Настройка освещения несовместима с этой сборкой');
    DAYLIGHT.hour=store.dayHour;DAYLIGHT.store=store;DAYLIGHT.descriptor=descriptor;
    DAYLIGHT.getter=()=>12;
    Object.defineProperty(store,'dayHour',{configurable:true,enumerable:descriptor.enumerable,
        get:DAYLIGHT.getter,set(value){DAYLIGHT.hour=value;}});
    store.dayTimeRevision++;
}
cleanups.push(()=>setDaylight(false));

const NIGHT_TIMER={hour:null,lastDraw:0};
const NIGHT_FLOW={key:null,hour:null,at:0,rate:null};
function flowingNightHour(hour,now){
    if(!Number.isFinite(hour))return {hour,estimated:false};
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    const url=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry)?.ws?.url;
    if(!url)return {hour,estimated:false};
    const key='t7-clock-flow:'+url.split('/ws/')[0];
    if(NIGHT_FLOW.key!==key){
        Object.assign(NIGHT_FLOW,{key,hour:null,at:0,rate:null});
        try{
            const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'null');
            if(saved&&now-saved.at>=0&&now-saved.at<300000&&Number.isFinite(saved.hour)){
                NIGHT_FLOW.hour=saved.hour;NIGHT_FLOW.at=saved.at;
                if(Number.isFinite(saved.rate)&&saved.rate>0)NIGHT_FLOW.rate=saved.rate;
            }
        }catch{}
    }
    if(NIGHT_FLOW.hour===null||NIGHT_FLOW.hour!==hour){
        const elapsed=(now-NIGHT_FLOW.at)/1000;
        const delta=NIGHT_FLOW.hour===null?0:(hour-NIGHT_FLOW.hour+24)%24;
        if(delta>0&&delta<12&&elapsed>=0.5&&elapsed<300){
            const measured=delta/elapsed;
            NIGHT_FLOW.rate=measured;
        }else if(NIGHT_FLOW.hour!==null&&(delta>=12||elapsed>=300))NIGHT_FLOW.rate=null;
        NIGHT_FLOW.hour=hour;NIGHT_FLOW.at=now;
        try{localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify({hour,at:now,rate:NIGHT_FLOW.rate}));}catch{}
    }
    const elapsed=Math.max(0,(now-NIGHT_FLOW.at)/1000);
    if(!NIGHT_FLOW.rate||elapsed>300)return {hour,estimated:false};
    return {hour:(hour+elapsed*NIGHT_FLOW.rate)%24,estimated:elapsed>0.2};
}
const nightTimerNode=document.createElement('div');
nightTimerNode.id='t7-night-timer';nightTimerNode.hidden=true;
nightTimerNode.style.cssText='position:fixed;z-index:99998;pointer-events:none;transform:translateX(-50%);color:#fff;font:bold 11px Arial;text-shadow:0 1px 3px #000,1px 0 2px #000;white-space:nowrap;';
(document.body||document.documentElement).appendChild(nightTimerNode);
cleanups.push(()=>nightTimerNode.remove());
function nightCountdown(hour){
    if(!Number.isFinite(hour))return 'Ожидание игровых часов…';
    hour=((hour%24)+24)%24;
    if(hour>=23||hour<5)return 'Полная ночь';
    const totalSeconds=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((23-hour)*3600-1e-8));
    const hours=Math.floor(totalSeconds/3600),minutes=Math.floor(totalSeconds%3600/60),seconds=totalSeconds%60;
    return 'До ночи: '+hours+' ч '+String(minutes).padStart(2,'0')+' мин '+String(seconds).padStart(2,'0')+' сек (игровых)';
}
function updateNightTimer(){
    const now=performance.now(),registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    if(GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!registry){nightTimerNode.hidden=true;NIGHT_TIMER.hour=null;return;}
    const hour=DAYLIGHT.store?DAYLIGHT.hour:client.bq?.dayHour;
    NIGHT_TIMER.hour=hour;
    if(now-NIGHT_TIMER.lastDraw<150)return;NIGHT_TIMER.lastDraw=now;
    const clock=document.querySelector('.clock__bg')?.parentElement;
    const rect=clock?.getBoundingClientRect();
    nightTimerNode.hidden=!CFG.nightTimer||!rect||rect.width<=0||rect.height<=0;
    if(nightTimerNode.hidden)return;
    nightTimerNode.style.left=(rect.left+rect.width/2)+'px';
    nightTimerNode.style.top=(rect.bottom+4)+'px';
    const flowing=flowingNightHour(NIGHT_TIMER.hour,Date.now());
    nightTimerNode.textContent=(flowing.estimated?'≈ ':'')+nightCountdown(flowing.hour);
}

const nightTimerInterval=setInterval(()=>{
    try{updateNightTimer();}catch(error){nightTimerNode.textContent='Таймер: данные временно недоступны';}
},200);
cleanups.push(()=>clearInterval(nightTimerInterval));

const INVIS_LOG={events:[],started:performance.now()};
function invisLog(event,record,message){
    if(!CFG.invisTracker)return;
    const entity=record.entity,visibility=entity.get?.('visibility');
    const snapshot={ms:Math.round(performance.now()-INVIS_LOG.started),event,name:record.name,
        visibility:visibility?{server:visibility.server,applied:visibility.applied,nightVision:visibility.nightVision}:null,
        previousHidden:record.hidden,hasLastSeen:!!record.lastSeen,hasMarker:!!record.marker,
        health:entity.state?.get?.('HealthComponentRepl'),
        visibilityMessage:entity.state?.get?.('VisibilityComponentRepl'),
        equipment:entity.state?.get?.('InventoryComponentRepl')};
    // Copy only the fields needed to identify invisibility; no entity IDs or network data.
    function fields(value,keys){
        if(!value||typeof value!=='object')return null;
        return Object.fromEntries(keys.filter(k=>k in value).map(k=>[k,value[k]]));
    }
    snapshot.health=fields(snapshot.health,['health','max_health','replicated_buffs','replicated_timeouts']);
    snapshot.equipment=fields(snapshot.equipment,['active_hat_type','active_item_type']);
    snapshot.visibilityMessage=fields(snapshot.visibilityMessage,['value','visibility','alpha']);
    if(message)snapshot.messageFields=Object.keys(message).filter(k=>!/id|name/i.test(k));
    try{INVIS_LOG.events.push(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(snapshot)));}catch{}
    if(INVIS_LOG.events.length>400)INVIS_LOG.events.splice(0,INVIS_LOG.events.length-400);
}
function saveInvisLog(){
    const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify({build:moduleURL.pathname,events:INVIS_LOG.events},null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),link=document.createElement('a');link.href=url;link.download='T7-invis-diagnostic.json';link.click();
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);
}
const INVIS={registry:null,players:new Map(),ttl:15000};
function clearInvisTracker(){for(const record of INVIS.players.values())record.unhook?.();INVIS.players.clear();INVIS.registry=null;}
cleanups.push(clearInvisTracker);
function observeInvisRecord(record,now){
    hookPotionUse(record);
    const entity=record.entity,player=GAME.cO.players?.[String(entity.id)];
        const name=cleanName(player?.name);if(name)record.name=name;
        const server=entity.get?.('visibility')?.server;
        const hidden=typeof server==='number'?server<0.5:null;
        const hatValue=apValue(entity.state?.get?.('InventoryComponentRepl')?.active_hat_type);
        const hat=typeof hatValue==='number'?hatValue:null;
        const camoType=gearType('DisguiseHat');
        const entered=record.hidden===false&&hidden===true;
        const putCamo=camoType!==null&&record.hat!==null&&record.hat!==camoType&&hat===camoType;
        const point=hidden===false?tracerEntityPosition(entity):null;
        const eventPoint=entered?record.lastSeen:(point||record.lastSeen);
        if((entered||putCamo)&&eventPoint){
            record.marker={x:eventPoint.x,y:eventPoint.y,at:now,expires:now+INVIS.ttl,
                reason:entered?'Ушёл в инвиз':'Надел камуфляж'};
        }
        if(point)record.lastSeen=point;
        if(hidden!==null)record.hidden=hidden;
        if(hat!==null)record.hat=hat;
}
function hookPotionUse(record){
    const animator=record.entity.get?.('animator');
    if(!animator||record.potionAnimator===animator)return;
    record.potionUnhook?.();
    record.potionAnimator=animator;
    const restores=[];
    for(const method of ['playAttack','playItemUse']){
        const original=animator[method];
        if(typeof original!=='function')continue;
        const own=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(animator,method);
        const patched=function(...args){
            // Capture the held item before the native handler can consume it.
            const entity=record.entity,type=gearType('InvisiblePotion');
            const drinking=type!==null&&entity.get?.('equipment')?.activeItemType===type;
            const point=drinking?tracerEntityPosition(entity):null;
            const result=original.apply(this,args);
            try{
                if(drinking&&CFG.invisTracker&&INVIS.players.get(String(entity.id))===record&&
                    this===animator&&!(typeof entity.isFriend==='function'&&entity.isFriend())){
                    const now=performance.now(),position=point||record.lastSeen;
                    if(position&&Number.isFinite(position.x)&&Number.isFinite(position.y)&&
                        (record.lastPotionUse===undefined||now-record.lastPotionUse>250)){
                        record.lastPotionUse=now;
                        record.marker={x:position.x,y:position.y,at:now,expires:now+INVIS.ttl,
                            reason:'Начал пить зелье инвиза'};
                        invisLog('invisibility-potion-use:'+method,record);
                    }
                }
            }catch{}
            return result;
        };
        animator[method]=patched;
        restores.push(()=>{if(animator[method]===patched){if(own)Object.defineProperty(animator,method,own);else delete animator[method];}});
    }
    record.potionUnhook=()=>{for(const restore of restores)restore();record.potionAnimator=null;};
}
function hookInvisRecord(record){
    const entity=record.entity,original=entity.applyComponentMessage;
    record.unhook=()=>record.potionUnhook?.();
    if(typeof original!=='function')return;
    const own=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(entity,'applyComponentMessage');
    const patched=function(...args){
        const result=original.apply(this,args);
        // Observe after the client's behaviours have processed this exact message,
        // before a subsequent removal or another message can hide the transition.
        if(CFG.invisTracker&&INVIS.players.get(String(this.id))===record){
            if(typeof this.isFriend==='function'&&this.isFriend())record.marker=null;
            else {
                if(args[0]!=='TransformComponentRepl')invisLog(String(args[0]),record,args[1]);
                observeInvisRecord(record,performance.now());
            }
        }
        return result;
    };
    entity.applyComponentMessage=patched;
    record.unhook=()=>{record.potionUnhook?.();if(entity.applyComponentMessage===patched){if(own)Object.defineProperty(entity,'applyComponentMessage',own);else delete entity.applyComponentMessage;}};
}
function markUnexplainedDisappearance(record,now){
    // No visibility transition was received. This is evidence of disappearance only.
    if(record.marker||!record.lastSeen||record.hidden!==false||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return;
    const health=apValue(record.entity.state?.get?.('HealthComponentRepl')?.health);
    if(typeof health==='number'&&health<=0)return;
    const point=record.lastSeen;
    if(!Number.isFinite(point.x)||!Number.isFinite(point.y))return;
    record.marker={x:point.x,y:point.y,at:now,expires:now+INVIS.ttl,reason:'Исчез — причина неизвестна'};
}
function updateInvisTracker(now){
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    if(!CFG.invisTracker||!registry||String(registry.localId)!==window.__localId__){clearInvisTracker();return;}
    if(INVIS.registry!==registry){clearInvisTracker();INVIS.registry=registry;}
    const live=new Set();
    for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
        const id=String(entity.id);
        if(entity.prefab?.type!=='Player'||id===window.__localId__)continue;
        const player=GAME.cO.players?.[id];
        const enemy=typeof entity.isFriend==='function'?!entity.isFriend():player?.friend===false;
        if(!enemy){INVIS.players.get(id)?.unhook?.();INVIS.players.delete(id);continue;}
        live.add(id);
        let record=INVIS.players.get(id);
        if(!record||record.entity!==entity){record?.unhook?.();record={entity,hidden:null,hat:null,lastSeen:null,name:'Игрок без ника',marker:null,unhook:null};INVIS.players.set(id,record);hookInvisRecord(record);invisLog('started-observing',record);}
        observeInvisRecord(record,now);
        if(record.marker&&now>=record.marker.expires)record.marker=null;
    }
    for(const [id,record] of INVIS.players){
        if(!live.has(id)){
            if(!record.loggedRemoval){
                invisLog('removed-from-registry',record);record.loggedRemoval=true;
                markUnexplainedDisappearance(record,now);
            }
            record.unhook?.();record.unhook=null;
            if(!record.marker||now>=record.marker.expires)INVIS.players.delete(id);
        }
    }
}
function drawInvisTracker(ctx,W,H){
    const now=performance.now();updateInvisTracker(now);
    if(!CFG.invisTracker)return;
    ctx.save();ctx.font='bold 11px Arial';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.shadowColor='#000';ctx.shadowBlur=3;
    for(const record of INVIS.players.values()){
        const p=record.marker;if(!p||now>=p.expires)continue;
        const q=w2s(p.x,p.y);
        if(!Number.isFinite(q.sx)||!Number.isFinite(q.sy)||q.sx<0||q.sx>W||q.sy<0||q.sy>H)continue;
        ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.strokeStyle='#ffb85c';ctx.fillStyle='#ffcf8b';ctx.lineWidth=2;
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.sx,q.sy,6,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillText(record.name+' · '+uiText(p.reason)+' · '+Math.ceil((p.expires-now)/1000)+(uiLanguage==='en'?' s':' с'),q.sx,Math.max(12,q.sy-12));
    }
    ctx.restore();
}






// Match whole words, not arbitrary substrings of ordinary words.

const CHAT_REMOTE={words:new Set(),loading:false,lastAttempt:0,status:'Не загружен',controller:null};
const CHAT_WORDS_URL='https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bars38/Russian_ban_words/refs/heads/master/words.txt';
const CHAT_REPEAT={enabled:false,text:'',channel:'public',effect:'none',milliseconds:200,step:0,next:0,session:null};
try{
 const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('kiro-chat-repeat-v1')||'null');
 if(saved){CHAT_REPEAT.text=String(saved.text||'').slice(0,120);CHAT_REPEAT.channel=saved.channel==='team'?'team':'public';CHAT_REPEAT.effect=['none','arrow','brackets','dots','sparkles','pulse','wave','scanner','orbit','lightning','typing','comet','radar','loading','equalizer','portal','heartbeat','spotlight','glitch','matrix','wings'].includes(saved.effect)?saved.effect:'none';}
}catch{}
function chatRepeatFrame(text,effect,step){
 text=Array.from(String(text).replace(/[\r\n\t]/g,' ').trim()).slice(0,120).join('');
 if(!text)return '';
 if(effect==='arrow')return ['▸ '+text,'▹ ▸ '+text,'▹ ▹ ▸ '+text][step%3];
 if(effect==='brackets'){const frames=[['[ ', ' ]'],['« ',' »'],['‹ ',' ›']];const f=frames[step%3];return f[0]+text+f[1];}
 if(effect==='dots')return text+[' ·',' ··',' ···'][step%3];
 if(effect==='sparkles'){const frames=['✦','✧','⋆','✧'];return frames[step%4]+' '+text+' '+frames[(step+2)%4];}
 if(effect==='pulse'){const frames=[['· ',' ·'],['• ',' •'],['● ',' ●'],['• ',' •']];const f=frames[step%4];return f[0]+text+f[1];}
 if(effect==='wave'){const frames=['∿','≈','≋','≈'];return frames[step%4]+' '+text+' '+frames[step%4];}
 if(effect==='scanner'){const frames=['▰▱▱▱','▱▰▱▱','▱▱▰▱','▱▱▱▰','▱▱▰▱','▱▰▱▱'];return '['+frames[step%6]+'] '+text;}
 if(effect==='orbit'){const frames=[['◐ ',' ◑'],['◓ ',' ◒'],['◑ ',' ◐'],['◒ ',' ◓']];const f=frames[step%4];return f[0]+text+f[1];}
 if(effect==='lightning'){const frames=[['ϟ ',' ϟ'],['ϟϟ ',' ϟ'],['ϟ ',' ϟϟ'],['ϟϟ ',' ϟϟ']];const f=frames[step%4];return f[0]+text+f[1];}
 if(effect==='comet'){const frames=['✦····','·✦···','··✦··','···✦·','····✦','···✧·','··✧··','·✧···'];return frames[step%8]+' '+text+' '+Array.from(frames[step%8]).reverse().join('');}
 if(effect==='radar'){const frames=['◴','◷','◶','◵'];return frames[step%4]+' ))) '+text+' ((( '+frames[(step+2)%4];}
 if(effect==='loading'){const count=step%7;return '['+'■'.repeat(count)+'□'.repeat(6-count)+'] '+text;}
 if(effect==='equalizer'){const frames=['▁▃▆█▅','▃▆█▅▂','▆█▅▂▁','█▅▂▁▃','▅▂▁▃▆','▂▁▃▆█'];const f=frames[step%6];return f+' '+text+' '+Array.from(f).reverse().join('');}
 if(effect==='portal'){const frames=[['〈 ',' 〉'],['❬〈 ',' 〉❭'],['❮❬〈 ',' 〉❭❯'],['❬〈 ',' 〉❭']];const f=frames[step%4];return f[0]+text+f[1];}
 if(effect==='heartbeat'){const frames=['─╱╲───','──╱╲──','───╱╲─','────╱╲','╲────╱','╱╲────'];return frames[step%6]+' ♥ '+text+' ♥';}
 if(effect==='spotlight'){const chars=Array.from(text);const at=step%chars.length;return chars.slice(0,at).join('')+'【'+chars[at]+'】'+chars.slice(at+1).join('');}
 if(effect==='glitch'){const frames=[['░▒ ',' ▒░'],['▓░ ',' ░▓'],['▒▓ ',' ▓▒'],['░▓ ',' ▓░'],['▓▒ ',' ▒▓']];const f=frames[step%5];return f[0]+text+f[1];}
 if(effect==='matrix'){const frames=['01001','10110','01101','11010','10011','00101'];return frames[step%6]+' ⟦'+text+'⟧ '+frames[(step+3)%6];}
 if(effect==='wings'){const frames=[['╰─ ',' ─╯'],['╰═ ',' ═╯'],['╚═ ',' ═╝'],['╭═ ',' ═╮'],['╭─ ',' ─╮'],['╰─ ',' ─╯']];const f=frames[step%6];return f[0]+text+f[1];}
 if(effect==='typing'){const chars=Array.from(text),count=step%(chars.length+4)+1;return chars.slice(0,Math.min(count,chars.length)).join('')+(step%2?'▏':'');}
 return text;
}
function chatRepeatTick(){
 const state=CHAT_REPEAT,now=performance.now();
 if(!state.enabled||disposed||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'){state.next=0;state.session=null;return;}
 const session=automationSessionReady(window.__t7Registry__,window.__t7LocalEntity__,now);
 if(!session){state.next=0;state.session=null;return;}
 if(state.session!==session.epoch){state.session=session.epoch;state.next=now+state.milliseconds;return;}
 if(now<state.next)return;
 state.next=now+state.milliseconds;
 const raw=state.text.trim().replace(/^\/team\s+/i,'');
 if(!raw||raw.startsWith('/'))return;
 const message=chatRepeatFrame(raw,state.effect,state.step);
 if(typeof client.chatSend!=='function')return;
 try{client.chatSend((state.channel==='team'?'/team ':'')+message);state.step++;}catch(error){console.debug('[KiroHack] Chat repeat:',error.message);}
}
const chatRepeatInterval=setInterval(chatRepeatTick,25);
cleanups.push(()=>{CHAT_REPEAT.enabled=false;clearInterval(chatRepeatInterval);});
const PACKET_LIMIT={conn:null,original:null,patched:null,descriptor:null,next:0};
function packetLimitStop(){const s=PACKET_LIMIT;if(s.conn?.transmitFrame===s.patched){if(s.descriptor)Object.defineProperty(s.conn,'transmitFrame',s.descriptor);else delete s.conn.transmitFrame;}Object.assign(s,{conn:null,original:null,patched:null,descriptor:null,next:0});}
function packetLimitTick(){
 const s=PACKET_LIMIT,conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(window.__t7Registry__);
 if(!CFG.packetLimit||disposed||!conn?.connected){packetLimitStop();return;}
 if(s.conn===conn)return;packetLimitStop();
 if(typeof conn.transmitFrame!=='function'||!conn.outbound)return;
 const original=conn.transmitFrame,descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(conn,'transmitFrame');
 const patched=function(...args){
  if(!CFG.packetLimit)return original.apply(this,args);
  const now=performance.now();if(now<s.next)return false;
  const sent=original.apply(this,args);
  if(sent)s.next=now+1000/Math.max(10,Math.min(150,Number(CFG.packetLimitRate)||100));
  return sent;
 };
 Object.assign(s,{conn,original,patched,descriptor,next:0});conn.transmitFrame=patched;
}
const packetLimitTimer=setInterval(packetLimitTick,50);
cleanups.push(()=>{clearInterval(packetLimitTimer);packetLimitStop();});

const CLEAR_PACKET={queue:null,original:null,patched:null,own:false,interval:null};
function clearPacketStop(){const r=CLEAR_PACKET;if(r.queue&&r.interval!==null&&r.queue.intervalMs===0)r.queue.intervalMs=r.interval;if(r.queue?.takeNext===r.patched){if(r.own)r.queue.takeNext=r.original;else delete r.queue.takeNext;}Object.assign(r,{queue:null,original:null,patched:null,own:false,interval:null});}
function clearPacketTick(){
 const conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(window.__t7Registry__),q=conn?.outbound;
 if(!CFG.clearPacket||disposed||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!conn?.connected){clearPacketStop();return;}
 if(CLEAR_PACKET.queue===q)return;clearPacketStop();
 if(!q||!Number.isFinite(q.intervalMs)||q.intervalMs<0||typeof q.takeNext!=='function'||!(q.latest instanceof Map)||!(q.priority instanceof Map)||!Array.isArray(q.reliable))return;
 const interval=q.intervalMs,original=q.takeNext,own=Object.hasOwn(q,'takeNext');let promoted=false;
 const patched=function(...args){
  // Never overtake a reliable attack edge with a newer movement state.
  const input=this.latest.get('input');
  if(CFG.clearPacket&&!promoted&&!this.priority.size&&input?.[1]===85&&!this.reliable.some(p=>p?.[1]===85)){
   this.latest.delete('input');promoted=true;return {packet:input,tier:'latest',channel:'input'};
  }
  promoted=false;return original.apply(this,args);
 };
 q.takeNext=patched;q.intervalMs=0;Object.assign(CLEAR_PACKET,{queue:q,original,patched,own,interval});
}
const clearPacketTimer=setInterval(clearPacketTick,100);
cleanups.push(()=>{clearInterval(clearPacketTimer);clearPacketStop();});

const RECONNECT={server:'',timer:null,epoch:0,attempts:0,next:0,connecting:false,started:0,scenes:null,abort:null,busy:false};
let autoLeaveBusy=false;
async function autoLeave(){
 if(disposed||autoLeaveBusy||!featureAvailable('t7autoleave'))return;
 const status=document.getElementById('t7autoleave');
 const show=text=>{if(status)status.textContent=text;};
 if(!nativeClient.b.state?.user){show(' — сначала войди в аккаунт');return;}
 autoLeaveBusy=true;show(' — выход…');
 try{
  reconnectStop(false);fastBuildStop();
  const ok=await nativeClient.b.logoutEverywhere();
  if(disposed)return;
  if(!ok){show(' — не удалось выйти');return;}
  packetLimitStop();nativeClient.bO();reconnectScenes()?.go('menu');
  show(' — выход выполнен');
 }catch(error){if(!disposed)show(' — ошибка выхода');console.warn('[KiroHack] Logout everywhere failed',error);}
 finally{autoLeaveBusy=false;}
}
function reconnectScenes(){
 for(const node of document.querySelectorAll('#ui-root,#app,#game-root,.app-scene-root')){
  const p=node.__vue_app__?._context?.provides??node.__vueParentComponent?.appContext?.provides;
  if(p?.scenes&&typeof p.scenes.go==='function')return p.scenes;
 }
 return null;
}
function reconnectStatus(ru,en){const el=document.getElementById('t7reconnectstatus');if(el)el.textContent=uiLanguage==='en'?en:ru;}
function reconnectStop(cancel=false){
 RECONNECT.epoch++;RECONNECT.abort?.abort();RECONNECT.abort=null;clearTimeout(RECONNECT.timer);RECONNECT.timer=null;
 if(cancel&&RECONNECT.connecting){nativeClient.bO();RECONNECT.scenes?.go('menu');}
 RECONNECT.connecting=false;CFG.reconnect=false;
 const el=document.getElementById('t7reconnect');if(el)el.checked=false;
}
async function reconnectStep(){
 if(!CFG.reconnect||disposed||RECONNECT.busy)return;
 const r=RECONNECT,epoch=r.epoch,abort=new AbortController();r.abort=abort;r.busy=true;r.connecting=true;
 let timeout=null;
 try{
  if(navigator.onLine===false)throw Error('offline');
  const assertActive=()=>{if(abort.signal.aborted||epoch!==r.epoch||!CFG.reconnect||disposed)throw new DOMException('Cancelled','AbortError');};
  timeout=setTimeout(()=>{abort.abort();nativeClient.bO();},150000);
  r.attempts++;reconnectStatus('Попытка '+r.attempts,'Attempt '+r.attempts);
  const servers=await nativeClient.bh({signal:abort.signal,refresh:true});assertActive();
  const server=servers.find(s=>s.label===r.server);if(!server)throw Error('Selected server unavailable');
  const account=nativeClient.b;
  const options={serverId:server.private?undefined:server.label,resourcePack:server.resourcePack,resourcePackPolicy:'always',connectionTimeoutMs:server.private?120000:30000};
  if(server.private){options.privateAuth={mode:nativeClient.bR(account.state?.user?.id||''),onUrl:async url=>{assertActive();if(!await account.authorizePrivateServer(url))throw Error('Private server authorization required');assertActive();}};}
  else options.acquireAuthTicket=async()=>{assertActive();const admission=await account.createGameAdmission(server.label,crypto.randomUUID(),abort.signal);assertActive();if(!admission?.ticket)throw Error('Admission not ready');return admission.ticket;};
  await nativeClient.bQ(server.websocketBaseUrl,server.mapArchiveUrl,options);assertActive();
  r.connecting=false;clearTimeout(timeout);timeout=null;r.scenes.go('gameplay');reconnectStop();reconnectStatus('Подключено','Connected');
 }catch(error){
  if(epoch===r.epoch&&CFG.reconnect&&!disposed){nativeClient.bO();reconnectStatus('Повтор подключения: '+r.server,'Retrying connection: '+r.server);}
 }finally{
  clearTimeout(timeout);r.busy=false;
  if(epoch===r.epoch){r.connecting=false;r.abort=null;if(CFG.reconnect&&!disposed)r.timer=setTimeout(()=>void reconnectStep(),Math.min(10000,2000*Math.max(1,r.attempts)));}
 }
}
function reconnectStart(){
 reconnectStop(true);const r=RECONNECT;if(r.busy){reconnectStatus('Дождись отмены предыдущей попытки','Wait for the previous attempt to stop');return;}r.scenes=reconnectScenes();
 const label=document.querySelector('.server-list__trigger')?.textContent?.trim()?.replace(/\s+\d+\s*\/\s*\d+\s*⌄?\s*$/,'').trim();
 try{r.server=label||localStorage.getItem('lastServer')||'';}catch{r.server=label||'';}
 if(!r.scenes||!r.server){reconnectStatus('Выбери сервер в меню игры и включи снова','Select a server in the game menu and enable again');return;}
 CFG.reconnect=true;const el=document.getElementById('t7reconnect');if(el)el.checked=true;
 r.attempts=0;r.next=Date.now()+1500;r.started=Date.now();nativeClient.bO();r.scenes.go('menu');r.timer=setTimeout(()=>void reconnectStep(),1500);
}
cleanups.push(()=>reconnectStop(true));

const LOCAL_CHAT={records:new Map(),models:new Map(),pending:new Set(),detector:null,busy:false,epoch:0,cache:new Map(),preparing:false,next:0,timer:null,controller:null,session:null};
const localChatSelector='.world-chat-message > span,.clan-chat-line__body,.chat-line__body,.chat-entry > p,.chat-message:not(.announcement-message) > p';
function localChatSession(){
 if(GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return null;
 return automationSessionReady(window.__t7Registry__,window.__t7LocalEntity__);
}
function localChatNearby(node,text){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,local=window.__t7LocalEntity__;if(!registry||!local)return false;
 const wrapper=node.closest('.world-overlay-position'),key=wrapper?.__vnode?.key;
 let id=typeof key==='string'&&key.startsWith('chat:')?key.slice(5):null;
 if(!id){const state=Object.values(clientModule).find(v=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&v.worldMessages&&v.teamMessages);const entries=Object.values(state?.worldMessages||{}).filter(m=>m.text===text);if(entries.length!==1)return false;id=String(entries[0].id);}
 const entity=registry.entities.get(id);if(entity?.prefab?.type!=='Player')return false;
 return Number.isFinite(entity.rx)&&Number.isFinite(entity.ry)&&Math.hypot(entity.rx-local.rx,entity.ry-local.ry)<=10;
}
function localChatStatus(ru,en){const el=document.getElementById('t7translationstatus');if(el)el.textContent=uiLanguage==='en'?en:ru;}
function localChatReset(){
 LOCAL_CHAT.epoch++;LOCAL_CHAT.session=null;
 clearInterval(LOCAL_CHAT.timer);LOCAL_CHAT.timer=null;
 LOCAL_CHAT.controller?.abort();LOCAL_CHAT.controller=null;
 LOCAL_CHAT.detector?.destroy();LOCAL_CHAT.detector=null;
 for(const p of LOCAL_CHAT.models.values())p.then(t=>t.destroy()).catch(()=>{});
 LOCAL_CHAT.models.clear();LOCAL_CHAT.cache.clear();LOCAL_CHAT.preparing=false;LOCAL_CHAT.busy=false;LOCAL_CHAT.next=0;
 for(const [node,r]of LOCAL_CHAT.records)if(node.isConnected&&node.textContent===r.shown)node.textContent=r.original;
 LOCAL_CHAT.records.clear();
}
async function localChatPrepare(){
 if(!CFG.chatTranslate||LOCAL_CHAT.preparing||disposed)return;
 const session=localChatSession();if(!session){localChatStatus('Войди на сервер','Join a server first');return;}LOCAL_CHAT.session=session;LOCAL_CHAT.preparing=true;
 const epoch=LOCAL_CHAT.epoch;LOCAL_CHAT.controller??=new AbortController();const signal=LOCAL_CHAT.controller.signal;
 if(LOCAL_CHAT.timer===null)LOCAL_CHAT.timer=setInterval(localChatTick,1000);
 try{
 if(!window.Translator||!window.LanguageDetector){localChatStatus('Локальный перевод недоступен в браузере','Local translation unavailable in this browser');return;}
 const jobs=[];
 if(!LOCAL_CHAT.detector)jobs.push(window.LanguageDetector.create({signal}).then(d=>{if(disposed||epoch!==LOCAL_CHAT.epoch||!CFG.chatTranslate)d.destroy();else LOCAL_CHAT.detector=d;}));
 const pairs=new Set(['en:ru','ru:en',...LOCAL_CHAT.pending]);
 for(const key of pairs){if(LOCAL_CHAT.models.has(key))continue;const [sourceLanguage,targetLanguage]=key.split(':');
 const promise=window.Translator.create({sourceLanguage,targetLanguage,signal});LOCAL_CHAT.models.set(key,promise);
 jobs.push(promise.then(t=>{LOCAL_CHAT.pending.delete(key);if(disposed||epoch!==LOCAL_CHAT.epoch||!CFG.chatTranslate)t.destroy();}).catch(error=>{if(epoch===LOCAL_CHAT.epoch)LOCAL_CHAT.models.delete(key);throw error;}));
 }
 localChatStatus('Подготовка локальных языков…','Preparing local languages…');
 const results=await Promise.allSettled(jobs);
 if(disposed||epoch!==LOCAL_CHAT.epoch||!CFG.chatTranslate)return;
 if(results.some(r=>r.status==='rejected'))localChatStatus('Язык недоступен. Повтори подготовку кнопкой','Language unavailable. Retry with the prepare button');
 else {LOCAL_CHAT.cache.clear();localChatStatus('Локальный перевод готов','Local translation ready');}
 }finally{if(epoch===LOCAL_CHAT.epoch)LOCAL_CHAT.preparing=false;}
}
async function localChatTranslateRaw(text,target){
 const epoch=LOCAL_CHAT.epoch,signal=LOCAL_CHAT.controller?.signal;
 const check=()=>{if(disposed||!CFG.chatTranslate||epoch!==LOCAL_CHAT.epoch||signal?.aborted||localChatSession()!==LOCAL_CHAT.session)throw new DOMException('Cancelled','AbortError');};check();
 const results=await LOCAL_CHAT.detector.detect(text,{signal});check();const best=results[0];
 if(!best||best.confidence<.4)return text;
 const source=best.detectedLanguage;if(source===target)return text;
 const key=source+':'+target;
 if(!LOCAL_CHAT.models.has(key)){
  const state=await window.Translator.availability({sourceLanguage:source,targetLanguage:target});
  check();if(state!=='available'){if(state!=='unavailable')LOCAL_CHAT.pending.add(key);localChatStatus('Нужен язык: '+source+'. Нажми «Подготовить языки»','Language needed: '+source+'. Click Prepare languages');return null;}
  LOCAL_CHAT.models.set(key,window.Translator.create({sourceLanguage:source,targetLanguage:target,signal}));
 }
 try{const model=await LOCAL_CHAT.models.get(key);check();return await model.translate(text,{signal});}catch(error){if(epoch===LOCAL_CHAT.epoch)LOCAL_CHAT.models.delete(key);throw error;}
}
async function localChatTranslate(text,target){
 const epoch=LOCAL_CHAT.epoch,key=target+'\n'+text,now=Date.now(),cached=LOCAL_CHAT.cache.get(key);
 if(cached&&cached.until>now)return cached.value;
 try{
  const value=await localChatTranslateRaw(text,target);
  if(epoch!==LOCAL_CHAT.epoch||!CFG.chatTranslate)return null;
  LOCAL_CHAT.cache.set(key,{value,until:Date.now()+(value===null?30000:300000)});
  while(LOCAL_CHAT.cache.size>128)LOCAL_CHAT.cache.delete(LOCAL_CHAT.cache.keys().next().value);
  return value;
 }catch(error){if(epoch!==LOCAL_CHAT.epoch||!CFG.chatTranslate)return null;LOCAL_CHAT.cache.set(key,{value:null,until:Date.now()+30000});LOCAL_CHAT.next=Date.now()+5000;throw error;}
}
async function localChatTick(){
 if(CFG.chatTranslate&&LOCAL_CHAT.session&&localChatSession()!==LOCAL_CHAT.session){localChatReset();CFG.chatTranslate=false;const box=document.getElementById('t7chattranslate');if(box)box.checked=false;return;}
 if(!CFG.chatTranslate||disposed||document.hidden||LOCAL_CHAT.preparing||Date.now()<LOCAL_CHAT.next||LOCAL_CHAT.busy||!LOCAL_CHAT.detector)return;
 LOCAL_CHAT.busy=true;const epoch=LOCAL_CHAT.epoch,target=uiLanguage;
 try{
  for(const [node,r]of LOCAL_CHAT.records){if(!node.isConnected)LOCAL_CHAT.records.delete(node);else if(!localChatNearby(node,r.original)){if(node.textContent===r.shown)node.textContent=r.original;LOCAL_CHAT.records.delete(node);}}
  let budget=1;
  for(const node of Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(localChatSelector)).slice(-40).reverse()){
   if(!CFG.chatTranslate||disposed||epoch!==LOCAL_CHAT.epoch||target!==uiLanguage)break;
   const current=node.textContent;let r=LOCAL_CHAT.records.get(node);
   if(!r||current!==r.shown){r={original:current,shown:current,lang:null};LOCAL_CHAT.records.set(node,r);}
   if(!localChatNearby(node,r.original))continue;
   if(r.lang===target||!r.original.trim()||r.original.length>500||!node.getClientRects().length)continue;
   const cached=LOCAL_CHAT.cache.get(target+'\n'+r.original);
   if(!cached||cached.until<=Date.now()){if(budget--<=0)break;}
   const result=await localChatTranslate(r.original,target);
   if(result===null)continue;
   if(CFG.chatTranslate&&!disposed&&epoch===LOCAL_CHAT.epoch&&target===uiLanguage&&localChatSession()===LOCAL_CHAT.session&&localChatNearby(node,r.original)&&node.isConnected&&node.textContent===r.shown){node.textContent=result;r.shown=result;r.lang=target;}
  }
 }catch{if(epoch===LOCAL_CHAT.epoch&&CFG.chatTranslate)localChatStatus('Не удалось перевести локально','Local translation failed');}finally{if(epoch===LOCAL_CHAT.epoch){LOCAL_CHAT.next=Math.max(LOCAL_CHAT.next,Date.now()+750);LOCAL_CHAT.busy=false;}}
}
cleanups.push(localChatReset);

function parseChatWordList(text){
 const entries=text.replace(/^\uFEFF/,'').split(/\r?\n/).map(w=>w.trim().normalize('NFKC').toLocaleLowerCase('ru').replace(/ё/g,'е')).filter(Boolean);
 if(entries.length<10||entries.length>50000||entries.some(w=>w.length>100||/[<>\x00-\x08]/.test(w)))throw Error('Неверный формат словаря');
 return new Set(entries);
}
function remoteChatStatus(){const node=document.getElementById('t7chatwordstatus');if(node)node.textContent=CHAT_REMOTE.status;}
try{const cached=localStorage.getItem('t7-chat-word-list-v1');if(cached){CHAT_REMOTE.words=parseChatWordList(cached);CHAT_REMOTE.status='Сохранённый список: '+CHAT_REMOTE.words.size+' слов';}}catch{}
async function refreshChatWords(){
 if(CHAT_REMOTE.loading||!CFG.chatSpacing||disposed)return;
 CHAT_REMOTE.loading=true;CHAT_REMOTE.lastAttempt=Date.now();CHAT_REMOTE.status='Загрузка списка…';remoteChatStatus();
 const controller=new AbortController();CHAT_REMOTE.controller=controller;const timeout=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),10000);
 try{
  const response=await fetch(CHAT_WORDS_URL,{signal:controller.signal,credentials:'omit',cache:'no-cache'});
  if(!response.ok)throw Error('HTTP '+response.status);
  const text=await response.text();if(text.length>2000000)throw Error('Слишком большой список');
  const words=parseChatWordList(text);if(disposed)return;
  CHAT_REMOTE.words=words;try{localStorage.setItem('t7-chat-word-list-v1',text);}catch{}
  CHAT_REMOTE.status='Список загружен: '+words.size+' слов';
 }catch{if(!disposed)CHAT_REMOTE.status=CHAT_REMOTE.words.size?'GitHub недоступен — используется сохранённый список':'GitHub недоступен — используется встроенный словарь';}
 finally{clearTimeout(timeout);CHAT_REMOTE.loading=false;CHAT_REMOTE.controller=null;remoteChatStatus();}
}
const chatWordsInterval=setInterval(()=>{if(CFG.chatSpacing&&Date.now()-CHAT_REMOTE.lastAttempt>=600000)refreshChatWords();},30000);
cleanups.push(()=>{clearInterval(chatWordsInterval);CHAT_REMOTE.controller?.abort();});
const CHAT_PROFANITY=[
 /^(?:бля|бляд(?:ь|и|ью|ей|ям|ями|ях|ский|ская|ское|ские|ство|овать)|блять)$/u,
 /^(?:на|по|ни|до|за|о|а)?ху(?:й|я|ю|ем|е|и|ёв|ев|ями|ями|ях|йн[яиею]|йней|йню|йня|евый|евая|евое|евые|ево|еть|ел|ела|ели|еешь|еет|еют)$/u,
 /^(?:(?:о|а)ху(?:енн(?:о|ый|ая|ое|ые)|ительн(?:о|ый|ая|ое|ые))|нахуй|похуй|нихуя|дохуя)$/u,
 /^(?:пизд(?:а|ы|е|у|ой|ою|ец|ецы|еца|ецу|еж|еть|ит|ишь|ят|ил|ила|или|ежа|юк|юки|юков|атый|атая|атое|ато)|пиздец)$/u,
 /^(?:от|на|за|вы|рас|раз|про|до)?пизд(?:ить|ел|ела|ели|ил|ила|или|ит|ишь|ят|еть|уй|уйте|овали|овать|ец)$/u,
 /^(?:(?:вы|за|на|до|от|раз|рас|под|про|пере|с)?[её]б(?:ать|аться|ал|ала|ало|али|ался|алась|ались|аный|аная|аное|аные|ан(?:ый|ая|ое|ые)|анн(?:ый|ая|ое|ые)|анут(?:ый|ая|ое|ые)|анул|анула|анули|ни|ните|ну|нуть|нет|нут|ешь|ет|ут|ите|ись|итесь|ля|ли|ло|лом|учий|учая|учее|учие)|[её]пт|[её]пта|[её]бт|[её]бля)$/u,
 /^(?:сука|суки|суке|суку|сукой|сукою|сукам|суками|суках|сучка|сучки|сучку|сучке|сучкой|сучара|сучары)$/u,
 /^(?:мудак|мудака|мудаку|мудаком|мудаки|мудаков|мудакам|мудаками|мудила|мудилы|мудил|мудилу|мудиле)$/u,
 /^(?:пидор|пидора|пидору|пидором|пидоры|пидоров|пидорам|пидорами|пидорас|пидораса|пидорасу|пидорасом|пидорасы|пидорасов|пидар|пидары|пидаров|педик|педики|педиков)$/u,
 /^(?:залупа|залупы|залупу|залупе|залупой|шлюха|шлюхи|шлюху|шлюхе|шлюх|шлюхой|говно|говна|говну|говном|говнюк|говнюки|гандон|гандоны|гандона|гандонов|гондон|гондоны|гондона|гондонов)$/u
];

// Word families supplement the exact forms above; matching stays at word boundaries.
const CHAT_PROFANITY_FAMILIES=[
 /^(?:(?:без|бес|до|за|на|недо|об|от|пере|по|под|подъ|при|про|раз|разъ|рас|с|съ|у|вы|въ|из|изъ)?(?:еба|ебну|ебни|ебен|ебуч|ебущ|ебл|ебыва|ебош|ебаш|ебыр|еблив))[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:еб|ебу|ебут|ебет|ебешь|ебете|ебем|ебись|ебитесь|ебусь|ебутся|ебется|ебешься|ебетесь|ебемся|еб|ебт|епт|епта|епть)$/u,
 /^(?:(?:до|за|на|недо|от|пере|по|под|при|про|раз|рас|у|вы|в)?пизд)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:(?:на|не|ни|по|до|за|о|а|с)?(?:хуй|хуе|хуя|хую)|хуйн)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:хули|хуле|хуль|нахер|нахрен|похер|похрен)$/u,
 /^(?:бляд|блять|блят|блядин|блядск|блядств)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:пидор|пидар|пидерас|педераст|педик|педрил)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:мудак|мудач|мудил|мудозвон|мудоеб|мудобл|мудень)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:сучар|сучон|сучен|сучищ|сучк)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:залуп|шлюх|шалав|говн|гавн|гандон|гондон|долбоеб|долбаеб|дебилоид)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:дроч|задроч|надроч|передроч|подроч|отдроч|выдроч|обдроч|сдроч|ананист|онанист)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:жоп|жопо|жополиз|жопошник|пердеж|пердун|пердеть|срака|срань|сраный|сраная|сраное|сраные|срать|срал|срала|срали|обосра|засра|насра|просра|высра)[а-я]*$/u,
 /^(?:fuck|fucking|fucked|fucker|fuckers|motherfucker|motherfuckers|motherfucking|shit|shits|shitty|bullshit|bitch|bitches|bitching|asshole|assholes|bastard|bastards|cunt|cunts|dickhead|dickheads|wanker|wankers)$/u
];
function normalizedChatWord(word){
 return word.normalize('NFKC').toLocaleLowerCase('ru').replace(/ё/g,'е').replace(/([а-яa-z])\1{2,}/gu,'$1');
}
function isChatProfanity(word){
 const normalized=normalizedChatWord(word);
 return CHAT_REMOTE.words.has(word.normalize('NFKC').toLocaleLowerCase('ru').replace(/ё/g,'е'))||CHAT_REMOTE.words.has(normalized)||CHAT_PROFANITY.some(pattern=>pattern.test(normalized))||CHAT_PROFANITY_FAMILIES.some(pattern=>pattern.test(normalized));
}
function spaceChatWords(text){
 if(!CFG.chatSpacing||typeof text!=='string'||(/^\//.test(text)&&!/^\/team\s/i.test(text)))return text;
 return text.replace(/[\p{L}\p{M}]+/gu,word=>isChatProfanity(word)?Array.from(word).join(' '):word);
}
function prepareSpacedChat(input){
 if(!CFG.chatSpacing||!input?.matches?.('input.chat-input__field,textarea.chat-input__field'))return;
 const value=input.value,next=spaceChatWords(value);if(next===value)return;
 const prototype=input.tagName==='TEXTAREA'?HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype:HTMLInputElement.prototype;
 Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(prototype,'value').set.call(input,next);
 input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));
}
function chatSpacingKey(e){if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.isComposing&&!e.shiftKey)prepareSpacedChat(e.target);}
function chatSpacingSubmit(e){const input=e.target?.querySelector?.('.chat-input__field');if(input)prepareSpacedChat(input);}
window.addEventListener('keydown',chatSpacingKey,true);window.addEventListener('submit',chatSpacingSubmit,true);
cleanups.push(()=>{window.removeEventListener('keydown',chatSpacingKey,true);window.removeEventListener('submit',chatSpacingSubmit,true);});
