const selectedBuildings=new Set(['Heartstone']);
// The client has its own heartstone alert; honour the same picture selection for it.
function filterUnselectedHeartstoneAlert(){
 if(!CFG.buildingAlerts||selectedBuildings.has('Heartstone')||!Array.isArray(client.ch?.toasts))return;
 const toasts=client.ch.toasts;
 if(toasts.some(t=>t.header==='notify.heartstone_destroyed'))client.ch.toasts=toasts.filter(t=>t.header!=='notify.heartstone_destroyed');
}
if(typeof GAME.w==='function')cleanups.push(GAME.w(()=>client.ch?.toasts,filterUnselectedHeartstoneAlert,{deep:true,flush:'sync'}));
const BUILD_ALERT={registry:null,localId:null,previous:null,off:null,serial:0,timers:new Set(),ownIds:new Set()};
function stopBuildingAlerts(){BUILD_ALERT.off?.();BUILD_ALERT.off=null;BUILD_ALERT.registry=null;BUILD_ALERT.localId=null;BUILD_ALERT.previous=null;}
function buildingAlertDiff(previous,next){
 const result=new Map();if(!previous)return result;
 for(const [id,type] of previous)if(!next.has(id)&&selectedBuildings.has(type))result.set(type,(result.get(type)||0)+1);
 return result;
}
const buildingAlertLayer=document.createElement('div');buildingAlertLayer.id='t7buildingalertlayer';
buildingAlertLayer.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:50%;top:90px;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:6px;max-width:90vw;pointer-events:none;z-index:2147483646';
document.body.appendChild(buildingAlertLayer);
function positionBuildingAlerts(){
 const native=document.querySelector('.notifiers');
 const box=native?.getBoundingClientRect();
 if(box&&box.width>0&&box.height>0){
  buildingAlertLayer.style.left=(box.left+box.width/2)+'px';
  buildingAlertLayer.style.top=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerHeight-50,box.bottom+6))+'px';
 }else{buildingAlertLayer.style.left='50%';buildingAlertLayer.style.top='90px';}
}
cleanups.push(()=>buildingAlertLayer.remove());
function showBuildingAlert(type,count){
 const label=BUILDING_ALERT_CATALOG.find(b=>b.type===type)?.label||type;
 const text=uiText('(Хак) '+(type==='Heartstone'&&count===1?'Вам сломали камень душ':'Вам сломали: '+label+(count>1?' ×'+count:'')));
 const id='t7-building-'+(++BUILD_ALERT.serial),message=document.createElement('div');
 message.id=id;message.textContent=text;
 message.style.cssText='color:#ff4040;font:bold 14px Arial,sans-serif;text-align:center;text-shadow:0 1px 3px #000,1px 0 2px #000;overflow-wrap:anywhere';
 buildingAlertLayer.prepend(message);BUILD_ALERT.ownIds.add(id);positionBuildingAlerts();
 const timer=setTimeout(()=>{BUILD_ALERT.timers.delete(timer);BUILD_ALERT.ownIds.delete(id);message.remove();},8000);BUILD_ALERT.timers.add(timer);
}
function ownedBuildingSnapshot(registry){
 const result=new Map();
 for(const [id,type] of registry.localChildren?.typeById||[]){const name=GAME.at?.[type];if(typeof name==='string')result.set(String(id),name);}
 return result;
}
function sampleBuildingAlerts(){
 const registry=BUILD_ALERT.registry;if(!registry||!CFG.buildingAlerts||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||String(registry.localId)!==BUILD_ALERT.localId)return;
 const next=ownedBuildingSnapshot(registry),previous=BUILD_ALERT.previous;BUILD_ALERT.previous=next;
 const removed=buildingAlertDiff(previous,next);if(!removed.size)return;
 const localId=BUILD_ALERT.localId;
 queueMicrotask(()=>{
  if(!CFG.buildingAlerts||BUILD_ALERT.registry!==registry||BUILD_ALERT.localId!==localId||String(registry.localId)!==localId||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return;
  const current=ownedBuildingSnapshot(registry),confirmed=new Map();
  for(const [id,type] of previous)if(!next.has(id)&&!current.has(id)&&selectedBuildings.has(type))confirmed.set(type,(confirmed.get(type)||0)+1);
  for(const [type,count] of confirmed)showBuildingAlert(type,count);
 });
}
function updateBuildingAlerts(){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,localId=window.__localId__;
 if(!CFG.buildingAlerts||!registry||!localId||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||String(registry.localId)!==localId){stopBuildingAlerts();return;}
 if(BUILD_ALERT.registry!==registry||BUILD_ALERT.localId!==localId){stopBuildingAlerts();BUILD_ALERT.registry=registry;BUILD_ALERT.localId=localId;sampleBuildingAlerts();BUILD_ALERT.off=registry.onLocalChildrenChanged?.(sampleBuildingAlerts);}
 sampleBuildingAlerts();
}
const buildingDialog=document.createElement('dialog');buildingDialog.id='t7buildingchoices';buildingDialog.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;margin:auto;width:min(560px,94vw);max-height:85vh;overflow:auto;background:#191d22;color:#e4e6e9;border:1px solid #48554e;border-radius:12px;padding:16px;font:13px Arial;z-index:2147483647;scrollbar-width:thin;scrollbar-color:#59616a transparent';

const buildingTitle=document.createElement('strong');buildingTitle.textContent='Уведомления о постройках';const buildingClose=document.createElement('button');buildingClose.textContent='Закрыть';buildingClose.onclick=()=>buildingDialog.close();buildingTop.append(buildingTitle,buildingClose);

function renderBuildingChoices(){
 buildingGrid.replaceChildren();const query=buildingSearch.value.trim().toLowerCase();
 for(const item of BUILDING_ALERT_CATALOG){
  if(query&&!uiText(item.label).toLowerCase().includes(query)&&!item.type.toLowerCase().includes(query))continue;
  const label=document.createElement('button');label.type='button';label.className='choice';label.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(selectedBuildings.has(item.type)));
  label.onclick=()=>{if(selectedBuildings.has(item.type))selectedBuildings.delete(item.type);else selectedBuildings.add(item.type);label.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(selectedBuildings.has(item.type)));filterUnselectedHeartstoneAlert();};
  const picture=document.createElement('img');picture.width=44;picture.height=44;picture.loading='lazy';picture.alt=item.label;picture.style.objectFit='contain';
  const asset='assets/spriteassets/entitiesicons/'+item.type.toLowerCase()+'.webp';picture.src=safeMenuAsset(asset);
  const text=document.createElement('span');text.textContent=item.label;label.append(picture,text);buildingGrid.appendChild(label);
 }
}
chooseAll.onclick=()=>{for(const item of BUILDING_ALERT_CATALOG)selectedBuildings.add(item.type);renderBuildingChoices();};chooseNone.onclick=()=>{selectedBuildings.clear();filterUnselectedHeartstoneAlert();renderBuildingChoices();};buildingSearch.oninput=renderBuildingChoices;
function openBuildingAlerts(){renderBuildingChoices();if(!buildingDialog.open)buildingDialog.showModal();}
const buildingAlertInterval=setInterval(()=>{try{updateBuildingAlerts();if(buildingAlertLayer.childElementCount)positionBuildingAlerts();}catch{}},250);
cleanups.push(()=>{clearInterval(buildingAlertInterval);stopBuildingAlerts();buildingDialog.remove();for(const timer of BUILD_ALERT.timers)clearTimeout(timer);if(client.ch?.toasts)client.ch.toasts=client.ch.toasts.filter(t=>!BUILD_ALERT.ownIds.has(t.id));BUILD_ALERT.timers.clear();BUILD_ALERT.ownIds.clear();});

const ALL_CHUNKS={binding:null};
function stopAllChunks(){
 const b=ALL_CHUNKS.binding;if(!b)return;
 b.active=false;
 if(b.map.updateViewport===b.patched){if(b.own)Object.defineProperty(b.map,'updateViewport',b.own);else delete b.map.updateViewport;}
 if(b.last&&!b.map.disposed)b.original.call(b.map,b.last);
 ALL_CHUNKS.binding=null;
}
function updateAllChunks(){
 const map=window.__t7Registry__?.services?.tilemap;
 if(!CFG.allChunks||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!map){stopAllChunks();return;}
 if(ALL_CHUNKS.binding?.map===map)return;
 stopAllChunks();
 if(typeof map.updateViewport!=='function'||!Array.isArray(map.chunks))return;
 const original=map.updateViewport,own=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(map,'updateViewport');
 const b={map,original,own,last:map.viewport?{...map.viewport}:null,active:true,patched:null};
 b.patched=function(viewport){
  b.calls=(b.calls||0)+1;b.last={...viewport};
  return original.call(this,b.active&&CFG.allChunks?{left:-Infinity,right:Infinity,bottom:-Infinity,top:Infinity}:viewport);
 };
 map.updateViewport=b.patched;ALL_CHUNKS.binding=b;
 if(b.last)b.patched.call(map,b.last);
}
const allChunksInterval=setInterval(()=>{try{updateAllChunks();}catch{stopAllChunks();}},250);
cleanups.push(()=>{clearInterval(allChunksInterval);stopAllChunks();});
const TRAP_MEMORY={registry:null,removed:new Set(),records:new Map(),watched:new Map(),conn:null,off:null};
function clearTrapMemory(){
 for(const off of TRAP_MEMORY.watched.values())off();TRAP_MEMORY.watched.clear();
 TRAP_MEMORY.off?.();TRAP_MEMORY.off=null;TRAP_MEMORY.conn=null;TRAP_MEMORY.registry=null;TRAP_MEMORY.records.clear();TRAP_MEMORY.removed.clear();
}
cleanups.push(clearTrapMemory);
function trapEnemy(entity,registry){
 const parent=parentFromEntity(entity);
 if(parent.id===window.__localId__)return false;
 if(registry.localChildren?.has?.(entity.id))return false;
 return typeof entity.isFriend==='function'&&!entity.isFriend();
}
function forgetTrap(id){TRAP_MEMORY.records.delete(id);TRAP_MEMORY.removed.add(id);}
function trapRemovalConfirmed(record,now){
 if(record.built)return false;
 const remaining=Number.isFinite(record.finishAt)?record.finishAt-now:null;
 return record.removalAt!==undefined&&now-record.removalAt>=350&&
  record.lastDamage!==undefined&&record.removalAt-record.lastDamage>=0&&record.removalAt-record.lastDamage<1500&&
  (remaining===null||remaining>250);
}
function observeTrap(entity,now){
 const registry=TRAP_MEMORY.registry;if(!registry)return;
 const id=String(entity.id),future=futureBuildingType(entity),built=entity.prefab?.type==='Trap';
 if(future!=='Trap'&&!built)return;
 if(TRAP_MEMORY.removed.has(id))return;
 let record=TRAP_MEMORY.records.get(id);
 const point=tracerEntityPosition(entity);
 if(!record&&built&&point){
  const possible=[...TRAP_MEMORY.records.values()].filter(r=>Math.hypot(r.x-point.x,r.y-point.y)<0.25&&(!parentFromEntity(entity).id||r.owner===parentFromEntity(entity).id));
  if(possible.length===1){record=possible[0];TRAP_MEMORY.records.delete(record.id);record.id=id;TRAP_MEMORY.records.set(id,record);}
 }
 if(!record){
  if(future!=='Trap'||!point||!trapEnemy(entity,registry))return;
  record={id,x:point.x,y:point.y,owner:parentFromEntity(entity).id,built:false,seen:now};TRAP_MEMORY.records.set(id,record);
 }
 if(parentFromEntity(entity).id===window.__localId__||registry.localChildren?.has?.(entity.id)||entity.isFriend?.()===true){TRAP_MEMORY.records.delete(id);return;}
 record.seen=now;if(point){record.x=point.x;record.y=point.y;}
 const trap=entity.state?.get?.('TrapComponentRepl');
 const hp=apValue(entity.state?.get?.('HealthComponentRepl')?.health);
 if(typeof hp==='number'){if(typeof record.hp==='number'&&hp<record.hp)record.lastDamage=now;record.hp=hp;}
 const scaffold=entity.state?.get?.('ScaffoldComponentRepl');
 if(scaffold&&scaffold!==record.scaffoldMessage){
  record.scaffoldMessage=scaffold;const remaining=apValue(scaffold.timeout);
  if(typeof remaining==='number'&&Number.isFinite(remaining))record.finishAt=now+Math.max(0,remaining)*1000;
 }
 if((trap&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(trap,'trapped_id'))||(typeof hp==='number'&&hp<=0)){forgetTrap(id);return;}
 if(built){record.built=true;delete record.removalAt;}
}
function trapShapeRadius(def){
 const shape=def?.physics?.shape;
 if(shape?.shape==='circle'&&Number.isFinite(shape.radius))return Math.max(0.1,shape.radius);
 if(shape?.shape==='cuboid'&&Number.isFinite(shape.width)&&Number.isFinite(shape.height))return Math.hypot(shape.width,shape.height)/2;
 return 0.7;
}
function trapIsActor(type,def){
 return type==='Player'||TRACE_MOBS.includes(type)||def?.tags?.includes('characters')||Boolean(def?.transport);
}
function trapActorObstructs(point,registry,defs){
 const target=defs[String(GAME.at?.[point.type]||'woodwall').toLowerCase()];
 const shape=target?.physics?.shape,margin=.04;
 function near(x,y,body){
  if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y))return false;
  let dx=Math.abs(x-point.x),dy=Math.abs(y-point.y);
  // Probe buildings have angle 0. Use their actual rectangle, not a large
  // circumscribed circle plus a generic exclusion zone around the character.
  if(shape?.shape==='cuboid'&&Number.isFinite(shape.width)&&Number.isFinite(shape.height)){
   dx=Math.max(0,dx-shape.width/2);dy=Math.max(0,dy-shape.height/2);
   return Math.hypot(dx,dy)<=body+margin;
  }
  return Math.hypot(dx,dy)<=trapShapeRadius(target)+body+margin;
 }
 const local=window.__t7LocalEntity__;
 if(local&&near(local.rx,local.ry,trapShapeRadius(defs.player)))return true;
 for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
  const type=entity.prefab?.type||entity.typeName||GAME.at?.[entity.type],def=defs[String(type||'').toLowerCase()];
  if(!trapIsActor(type,def))continue;
  const body=trapShapeRadius(def),position=tracerEntityPosition(entity);
  if(position&&near(position.x,position.y,body))return true;
  const raw=entity.state?.get?.('TransformComponentRepl')?.position;
  if(raw?.length>=2&&near(raw[0],raw[1],body))return true;
 }
 return false;
}

function trapKnownObstacle(point,registry,defs){
 if(trapActorObstructs(point,registry,defs))return true;
 const targetRadius=trapShapeRadius(defs[String(GAME.at?.[point.type]||'').toLowerCase()]);
 for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
  if(String(entity.id)===String(GAME.d4.localEntityId))continue;
  const visibility=entity.get?.('visibility')?.server;
  if(typeof visibility==='number'&&visibility<0.5)continue;
  const type=futureBuildingType(entity)||entity.prefab?.type;
  if(entity.prefab?.category==='projectile'||type==='Item')continue;
  const p=tracerEntityPosition(entity);if(!p)continue;
  const def=defs[String(type||'').toLowerCase()];
  if(trapIsActor(type,def))continue;
  if(Math.hypot(p.x-point.x,p.y-point.y)<=targetRadius+trapShapeRadius(def)+0.15)return true;
 }
 const map=GAME.dT;
 for(const item of map.terrain||[]){
  const type=GAME.at?.[item.ty];if(!RESOURCE_STYLE[type])continue;
  const x=item.nx*map.worldWidth,y=item.ny*map.worldHeight;
  if(Number.isFinite(x)&&Number.isFinite(y)&&Math.hypot(x-point.x,y-point.y)<=targetRadius+trapShapeRadius(defs[type.toLowerCase()])+0.15)return true;
 }
 // Conservatively exclude custom map collider bounds, including rotated objects.
 const parsed=registry.services?.tilemap?.parsedMap;
 for(const object of parsed?.objects||[]){
  if(String(object.type).toLowerCase()!=='collider')continue;
  const x=Number(object.x)/100,y=(parsed.height*parsed.tileHeight-Number(object.y))/100;
  const width=Number(object.width),height=Number(object.height);
  if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y)||!Number.isFinite(width)||!Number.isFinite(height))continue;
  const radius=Math.hypot(width,height)/100;
  if(Math.hypot(x-point.x,y-point.y)<=targetRadius+radius+0.2)return true;
 }
 return false;
}
const TRAP_DETECT={registry:null,epoch:null,conn:null,off:null,pending:new Map(),passed:new Map(),marks:new Map(),checked:new Map(),suspects:new Map(),next:0,serial:1000000,origin:null,points:[]};
function clearTrapDetector(){
 TRAP_DETECT.off?.();Object.assign(TRAP_DETECT,{registry:null,epoch:null,conn:null,off:null,next:0,origin:null,points:[]});
 for(const name of ['pending','passed','marks','checked','suspects'])TRAP_DETECT[name].clear();
}
cleanups.push(clearTrapDetector);
function trapBuildState(){return Object.values(clientModule).find(v=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&'buildType'in v&&'cursorReady'in v&&'valid'in v);}
function trapScanRadius(defs){
 const value=Number(defs?.player?.build_radius);
 return Number.isFinite(value)?Math.max(.1,value):4;
}
function trapProbeInZone(point,defs){
 const local=window.__t7LocalEntity__;if(!local)return false;
 const radius=trapScanRadius(defs);
 if(Math.hypot(point.x-local.rx,point.y-local.ry)>radius-.1)return false;
 const entity=TRAP_DETECT.registry?.entities?.get?.(String(GAME.d4.localEntityId));
 const raw=entity?.state?.get?.('TransformComponentRepl')?.position;
 return !(raw?.length>=2&&Number.isFinite(raw[0])&&Number.isFinite(raw[1]))||Math.hypot(point.x-raw[0],point.y-raw[1])<=radius-.1;
}
function trapAutoReply(packet){
 if(packet.name!=='BuildResponse')return;
 const state=TRAP_DETECT,data=packet.payload||{},id=Number(data.id),pending=state.pending.get(id),now=performance.now();
 if(!pending||!(data.test_pass===true||data.testPass===true))return;
 state.pending.delete(id);
 if(!CFG.trapDetector||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||trapBuildState()?.buildType>0||now-pending.time>2000||pending.obstructed)return;
 const config=hatsConfig(),defs=config?.entities?.entities??config?.entities;if(!defs)return;
 if(!trapProbeInZone(pending,defs)||trapActorObstructs(pending,state.registry,defs)){
  state.suspects.delete(pending.key);state.marks.delete(pending.key);return;
 }
 if(data.flag===true){
  state.suspects.delete(pending.key);
  state.passed.set(pending.key,{x:pending.x,y:pending.y,time:now});
  for(const [key,mark] of state.marks)if(Math.hypot(mark.x-pending.x,mark.y-pending.y)<.8)state.marks.delete(key);
 }else{
  const control=[...state.passed.values()].some(p=>now-p.time<1500&&Math.hypot(p.x-pending.x,p.y-pending.y)<2);
  if(!control||trapKnownObstacle(pending,state.registry,defs)){state.suspects.delete(pending.key);state.marks.delete(pending.key);return;}
  const reason=Number(data.reason??0),previous=state.suspects.get(pending.key);
  const count=previous&&now-previous.time<3000&&previous.reason===reason?previous.count+1:1;
  state.suspects.set(pending.key,{count,reason,time:now});
  if(count>=2)state.marks.set(pending.key,{x:pending.x,y:pending.y,type:pending.type,expires:now+15000});
  else state.checked.set(pending.key,now-3000);

 }
}
function trapAutoTick(registry,session,now){
 const state=TRAP_DETECT;
 if(state.registry!==registry||state.epoch!==session.epoch||state.conn!==session.conn){
  clearTrapDetector();Object.assign(state,{registry,epoch:session.epoch,conn:session.conn});
  state.off=session.conn.onAny?.(trapAutoReply)||null;
 }
 if(trapBuildState()?.buildType>0){state.pending.clear();return;}
 const local=window.__t7LocalEntity__,config=hatsConfig(),defs=config?.entities?.entities??config?.entities;
 const type=GAME.at?.WoodWall,builds=config?.build?.build??config?.build;
 if(!local||!defs||!builds?.woodwall||!Number.isInteger(type)||!session.ready||session.conn.connected!==true)return;
 for(const [id,p] of state.pending){
  if(now-p.time>2000)state.pending.delete(id);
  else if(!trapProbeInZone(p,defs)||trapActorObstructs(p,registry,defs)){
   p.obstructed=true;state.suspects.delete(p.key);state.marks.delete(p.key);
  }
 }
 for(const [key,p] of state.passed)if(now-p.time>8000)state.passed.delete(key);
 for(const [key,p] of state.suspects)if(now-p.time>3000)state.suspects.delete(key);
 for(const [key,time] of state.checked)if(now-time>30000)state.checked.delete(key);
 // Ordinary send coalesces all test probes under one 'build-position' key.
 // Reliable sending preserves distinct request IDs; keep at most four in flight.
 const reliable=typeof session.conn.sendReliable==='function',capacity=reliable?4:1;
 if(now<state.next||state.pending.size>=capacity)return;
 const radius=trapScanRadius(defs);
 if(!state.origin||state.origin.radius!==radius||Math.hypot(local.rx-state.origin.x,local.ry-state.origin.y)>.32){
  state.origin={x:local.rx,y:local.ry,radius};state.points=[];
  const step=1.28,count=Math.ceil(radius/step);
  // Match the game's Tile placement lattice. A changed server radius is read live.
  for(let iy=-count;iy<=count;iy++)for(let ix=-count;ix<=count;ix++){
   const x=Math.floor(local.rx/step)*step+.64+ix*step,y=Math.floor(local.ry/step)*step+.64+iy*step;
   if(Math.hypot(x-local.rx,y-local.ry)<=radius)state.points.push({x,y,key:x.toFixed(2)+':'+y.toFixed(2)});
  }
 }
 // Oldest/unvisited points first in all directions; no movement-direction bias.
 state.points.sort((a,b)=>(state.checked.get(a.key)??-Infinity)-(state.checked.get(b.key)??-Infinity)||
  Math.hypot(a.x-local.rx,a.y-local.ry)-Math.hypot(b.x-local.rx,b.y-local.ry));
 const map=GAME.dT,busy=new Set([...state.pending.values()].map(p=>p.key));
 for(const point of state.points){
  const candidate={...point,type};
  if(busy.has(point.key)||point.x<=0||point.y<=0||point.x>=map.worldWidth||point.y>=map.worldHeight||
   Math.hypot(point.x-local.rx,point.y-local.ry)>radius||trapKnownObstacle(candidate,registry,defs))continue;
  const id=++state.serial,request={...candidate,id,time:now};
  state.pending.set(id,request);state.checked.set(point.key,now);state.next=now+50;
  try{
   const payload={id,build_type:type,x:point.x,y:point.y,angle:0,test_pass:true};
   if(!Number.isInteger(compatPacketTypes?.InputBuildRequest))return;if(reliable)session.conn.sendReliable(compatPacketTypes.InputBuildRequest,payload);else session.conn.send(compatPacketTypes.InputBuildRequest,payload);
  }catch{state.pending.delete(id);state.next=now+1000;}
  break;
 }
}
function drawTrapDetector(ctx){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,session=registry&&AUTOMATION.sessions.get(registry),now=performance.now();
 if(!CFG.trapDetector||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!registry||!session){clearTrapDetector();return;}
 trapAutoTick(registry,session,now);
 const config=hatsConfig(),defs=config?.entities?.entities??config?.entities;
 ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='#fff';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.setLineDash([4,3]);
 for(const [key,mark] of TRAP_DETECT.marks){
  if(now>=mark.expires||(defs&&trapActorObstructs(mark,registry,defs))){TRAP_DETECT.marks.delete(key);continue;}
  const a=w2s(mark.x-.55,mark.y-.55),b=w2s(mark.x+.55,mark.y+.55);
  if(a&&b&&[a.sx,a.sy,b.sx,b.sy].every(Number.isFinite))ctx.strokeRect(Math.min(a.sx,b.sx),Math.min(a.sy,b.sy),Math.abs(a.sx-b.sx),Math.abs(a.sy-b.sy));
 }
 ctx.restore();
}

function drawTrapMemory(ctx){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,now=performance.now();
 if(!CFG.trapMemory||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!registry){clearTrapMemory();return;}
 if(TRAP_MEMORY.registry!==registry){clearTrapMemory();TRAP_MEMORY.registry=registry;}
 const conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry);
 if(conn!==TRAP_MEMORY.conn){TRAP_MEMORY.off?.();TRAP_MEMORY.conn=conn;TRAP_MEMORY.off=conn?.onAny?.(packet=>{
  const now=performance.now();
  if(packet.name==='RPCDamageReceive'){
   const damaged=TRAP_MEMORY.records.get(String(apValue(packet.payload?.to)));if(damaged)damaged.lastDamage=now;return;
  }
  const id=String(apValue(packet.payload?.id)),record=TRAP_MEMORY.records.get(id);
  if(!record)return;
  if(['RPCObjectDeath','RPCObjectSimulateDeath'].includes(packet.name)){forgetTrap(id);return;}
  if(packet.name==='RPCObjectWillBeRemoved'&&record.built){forgetTrap(id);return;}
  if(['RPCObjectWillBeRemoved','ReplicateRemove'].includes(packet.name)&&!record.built)record.removalAt=now;

 });}
 const live=new Set();
 for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
  if(entity.prefab?.type!=='Trap'&&futureBuildingType(entity)!=='Trap')continue;
  live.add(entity);
  if(!TRAP_MEMORY.watched.has(entity)){
   const off=[];
   for(const component of ['TrapComponentRepl','ScaffoldComponentRepl','HealthComponentRepl','ChildComponentRepl']){
    const unsubscribe=entity.state?.subscribe?.(component,()=>observeTrap(entity,performance.now()));if(typeof unsubscribe==='function')off.push(unsubscribe);
   }
   TRAP_MEMORY.watched.set(entity,()=>off.forEach(f=>f()));
  }
  observeTrap(entity,now);
 }
 for(const [entity,off] of TRAP_MEMORY.watched)if(!live.has(entity)){off();TRAP_MEMORY.watched.delete(entity);}
 ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle='#fff';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.shadowColor='#000';ctx.shadowBlur=2;
 for(const [id,r] of TRAP_MEMORY.records){
  if(trapRemovalConfirmed(r,now)){forgetTrap(id);continue;}
  // Preserve completed hidden traps and ordinary visibility unloads.
  const a=w2s(r.x-0.55,r.y-0.55),b=w2s(r.x+0.55,r.y+0.55);
  if(!a||!b||![a.sx,a.sy,b.sx,b.sy].every(Number.isFinite))continue;
  ctx.strokeRect(Math.min(a.sx,b.sx),Math.min(a.sy,b.sy),Math.abs(b.sx-a.sx),Math.abs(b.sy-a.sy));
 }
 ctx.restore();
}
const LOST_ITEMS={conn:null,off:null,last:[],alive:false,localId:null,pending:null,ghosts:new Map(),revision:0,lastInventory:0,nextDraw:0,history:[],position:null,teleport:null,inventoryBefore:null,healthSample:null,stoneRecovery:null};
const lostItemsLayer=document.createElement('div');lostItemsLayer.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;pointer-events:none;z-index:2147483644';lostItemsLayer.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');lostItemsLayer.id='kiro-lost-items';(document.body||document.documentElement).append(lostItemsLayer);
function lostItemsReset(){
 const s=LOST_ITEMS;s.off?.();Object.assign(s,{conn:null,off:null,last:[],alive:false,localId:null,pending:null,revision:0,lastInventory:0,nextDraw:0,history:[],position:null,teleport:null,inventoryBefore:null,healthSample:null,stoneRecovery:null});s.ghosts.clear();lostItemsLayer.replaceChildren();
}
cleanups.push(()=>{lostItemsReset();lostItemsLayer.remove();});
function lostItemsSnapshot(){return GAME.bS.slots.filter(slot=>Number.isInteger(slot.index)&&slot.index>=0&&slot.index<45&&slot.type>0&&slot.count>0).map(slot=>({index:slot.index,type:Number(slot.type),count:Number(slot.count)}));}
function lostItemsDeath(now){
 const s=LOST_ITEMS;if(!s.alive)return;
 s.ghosts.clear();lostItemsLayer.replaceChildren();s.pending={before:(s.history.find(entry=>entry.time>=now-500)?.slots||s.last).map(slot=>({...slot})),revision:s.revision,started:now,respawnAt:null,sawDead:false,expires:now+60000};s.alive=false;s.teleport=null;s.inventoryBefore=null;s.stoneRecovery=null;
}
function lostItemsCompare(before,after){
 const totals=new Map(),slots=new Map(after.map(slot=>[slot.index,slot])),result=[];
 for(const slot of before)totals.set(slot.type,(totals.get(slot.type)||0)+slot.count);
 for(const slot of after)totals.set(slot.type,Math.max(0,(totals.get(slot.type)||0)-slot.count));
 for(const slot of before){
  const current=slots.get(slot.index);if(current&&current.type!==slot.type)continue;
  const remaining=current?.count||0,count=Math.min(Math.max(0,slot.count-remaining),totals.get(slot.type)||0);
  if(count>0){result.push({...slot,count,remaining});totals.set(slot.type,totals.get(slot.type)-count);}
 }
 return result;
}
function lostItemsSlotIndex(element){
 const raw=element.getAttribute('data-inventory-index')??(element.getAttribute('data-touch-slot-kind')==='inventory'?element.getAttribute('data-touch-slot-index'):null);
 if(raw!==null&&/^\d+$/.test(raw)&&Number(raw)<45)return Number(raw);
 const label=element.getAttribute('aria-label')||'',match=/^Inventory slot (\d+)$/.exec(label);if(match)return Number(match[1])-1;
 for(let component=element.__vueParentComponent,depth=0;component&&depth<3;component=component.parent,depth++){
  const p=component.props,index=p?.slot?.index??p?.index??p?.slotIndex;if(Number.isInteger(index)&&index>=0&&index<45)return index;
 }
 return -1;
}
function lostItemsDraw(now){
 const s=LOST_ITEMS;if(now<s.nextDraw)return;s.nextDraw=now+100;lostItemsLayer.replaceChildren();
 if(document.hidden||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!s.ghosts.size)return;
 const seen=new Set();
 for(const element of document.querySelectorAll('[data-inventory-index],[data-touch-slot-kind="inventory"],[aria-label^="Inventory slot "]')){
  const index=lostItemsSlotIndex(element),ghost=s.ghosts.get(index);if(!ghost||!element.getClientRects().length)continue;
  const rect=element.getBoundingClientRect();if(rect.width<10||rect.height<10)continue;
  const key=index+':'+rect.left+':'+rect.top;if(seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);
  const box=document.createElement('div');box.style.cssText='position:absolute;box-sizing:border-box;pointer-events:none;border:1px solid #ff5555;background:#d5222228;border-radius:5px;overflow:hidden';Object.assign(box.style,{left:rect.left+'px',top:rect.top+'px',width:rect.width+'px',height:rect.height+'px'});
  const img=document.createElement('img');img.style.cssText='position:absolute;inset:12%;width:76%;height:76%;object-fit:contain;opacity:.95;filter:sepia(.65) saturate(1.8) hue-rotate(315deg) drop-shadow(0 0 2px #ff3030)';
  const name=gearName(ghost.type);img.alt=name;let url;try{url=nativeClient.z(ghost.type);}catch{}
  if(url){img.src=url;box.append(img);}else{const label=document.createElement('span');label.textContent=name;label.style.cssText='color:#ff5757;font:10px Arial;overflow-wrap:anywhere';box.append(label);}
  const count=document.createElement('span');count.textContent='−'+String(ghost.count);count.style.cssText='position:absolute;right:3px;bottom:2px;color:#ff6666;font:bold 12px Arial;text-shadow:0 1px 2px #000';box.append(count);lostItemsLayer.append(box);
 }
}
function lostItemsTick(){
 const s=LOST_ITEMS,now=performance.now(),registry=window.__t7Registry__,conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry);
 if(!CFG.lostItems||!conn||conn.connected===false||!['gameplay','spectating','gameover'].includes(GAME.be.scene)){if(s.conn||s.ghosts.size)lostItemsReset();return;}
 if(s.conn!==conn){
  lostItemsReset();s.conn=conn;
  s.off=conn.onAny?.(packet=>{
   if(!CFG.lostItems||s.conn!==conn)return;
   if(packet.name==='ReplicateInventory'){
    s.revision++;s.lastInventory=performance.now();
    if((packet.payload?.index?.length||0)>=45)s.inventoryBefore={time:s.lastInventory,slots:lostItemsSnapshot()};
   }
   if(packet.name==='RPCEntityTeleported'&&idOf(packet.payload?.id)===s.localId)s.teleport={time:performance.now(),slots:s.last.map(slot=>({...slot}))};
   if(packet.name==='PlayerDespawn'){const snapshot=lostItemsSnapshot();if(snapshot.length){s.last=snapshot;s.history=[];}lostItemsDeath(performance.now());if(s.pending)s.pending.sawDead=true;}
   if(packet.name==='RPCObjectDeath'&&idOf(packet.payload?.id)===s.localId)lostItemsDeath(performance.now());
   // A simulated death keeps the local entity alive (including return to a soul stone).
   if(packet.name==='RPCObjectSimulateDeath'&&s.localId!==null&&idOf(packet.payload?.id)===s.localId){
    lostItemsDeath(performance.now());
    if(s.pending){s.pending.sawDead=true;s.pending.simulated=true;}
   }
  })||null;
 }
 const hp=Number(GAME.c9.health),maxHp=Number(GAME.c9.maxHealth),previousHealth=s.healthSample;
 if(previousHealth&&now-previousHealth.time<1500&&maxHp>0&&hp-previousHealth.hp>=maxHp*.1&&Math.abs(hp-maxHp*.3)<=Math.max(1,maxHp*.01))s.stoneRecovery={time:now,slots:s.last.map(slot=>({...slot}))};
 s.healthSample={time:now,hp};
 const point=window.__t7LocalEntity__;
 if(point&&Number.isFinite(point.rx)&&Number.isFinite(point.ry)){
  const previous=s.position;
  if(previous&&now-previous.time<250&&Math.hypot(point.rx-previous.x,point.ry-previous.y)>8)s.teleport={time:now,slots:s.last.map(slot=>({...slot}))};
  s.position={time:now,x:point.rx,y:point.ry};
 }
 const transfer=s.stoneRecovery||s.teleport;
 if(!s.pending&&s.alive&&transfer&&s.inventoryBefore&&Math.abs(transfer.time-s.inventoryBefore.time)<1500&&now-Math.max(transfer.time,s.inventoryBefore.time)<1500){
  const before=s.inventoryBefore.time<=transfer.time?s.inventoryBefore.slots:transfer.slots;
  lostItemsDeath(now);
  if(s.pending){s.pending.before=before;s.pending.sawDead=true;s.pending.simulated=true;}
  s.teleport=null;s.inventoryBefore=null;s.stoneRecovery=null;
 }
 const alive=GAME.c9.health>0&&GAME.c9.alive!==false&&GAME.be.scene==='gameplay',current=lostItemsSnapshot();
 if(s.alive&&!alive)lostItemsDeath(now);
 if(s.pending){
  if(!alive){s.pending.sawDead=true;s.pending.respawnAt=null;}
  else if(s.pending.respawnAt===null)s.pending.respawnAt=now;
  if(now>=s.pending.expires)s.pending=null;
  else if(alive&&s.pending.sawDead&&now-s.pending.respawnAt>=(s.pending.simulated?700:350)&& (now-s.lastInventory>=100||now-s.pending.respawnAt>=1500)){
   for(const slot of lostItemsCompare(s.pending.before,current))s.ghosts.set(slot.index,{...slot,expires:s.pending.expires});s.pending=null;s.teleport=null;s.inventoryBefore=null;s.stoneRecovery=null;s.healthSample=null;s.position=null;s.history=[];
  }
 }
 if(alive&&!s.pending){s.last=current;s.history.push({time:now,slots:current});while(s.history.length>1&&s.history[0].time<now-600)s.history.shift();s.localId=idOf(GAME.d4.localEntityId);s.alive=true;}
 for(const [index,ghost]of s.ghosts){
  const slot=current.find(slot=>slot.index===index);
  if(now>=ghost.expires||(slot&&(slot.type!==ghost.type||slot.count>(ghost.remaining||0))))s.ghosts.delete(index);
 }
 lostItemsDraw(now);
}

function drawBarrelRadius(ctx){
 if(!CFG.barrelRadius||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return;
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,config=hatsConfig(),defs=config?.entities?.entities??config?.entities;
 if(!registry?.entities||!defs)return;
 ctx.save();
 try{
  ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,111,66,.85)';ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,75,35,.065)';
  for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
   const type=entity.prefab?.type||entity.typeName||GAME.at?.[entity.type];
   if(!/barrel/i.test(String(type||'')))continue;
   const radius=Number(defs[String(type).toLowerCase()]?.explosion?.radius);
   if(!Number.isFinite(radius)||radius<=0)continue;
   const visibility=entity.get?.('visibility')?.server;if(typeof visibility==='number'&&visibility<.5)continue;
   const p=tracerEntityPosition(entity);if(!p)continue;
   const points=[];
   for(let i=0;i<48;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/24,q=w2s(p.x+Math.cos(a)*radius,p.y+Math.sin(a)*radius);if(!Number.isFinite(q.sx)||!Number.isFinite(q.sy))break;points.push(q);}
   if(points.length!==48)continue;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(points[0].sx,points[0].sy);for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(points[i].sx,points[i].sy);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
  }
 }finally{ctx.restore();}
}

const TOWER_RD={conn:null,off:null,attacks:new Map()};
function towerRdReset(){TOWER_RD.off?.();TOWER_RD.off=null;TOWER_RD.conn=null;TOWER_RD.attacks.clear();}
cleanups.push(towerRdReset);
function towerRdDefinition(entity){
 const config=hatsConfig(),defs=config?.entities?.entities??config?.entities,items=config?.items?.items??config?.items;
 const type=entity?.prefab?.type||entity?.typeName||GAME.at?.[entity?.type];
 const def=defs?.[String(type||'').toLowerCase()],weapon=items?.[String(def?.behavior?.weapon||'').toLowerCase()],action=weapon?.item_action;
 if(!def?.tower||!action||action.tag!=='Range')return null;
 const radius=Number(action.distance),timeout=Number(def.tower.timeout);
 if(!(radius>0&&Number.isFinite(radius)))return null;
 return {radius,timeout:Number.isFinite(timeout)&&timeout>0?timeout:Number(action.post_timeout)||0};
}
function drawTowerRd(ctx){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry);
 if(!CFG.towerRd||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!conn||conn.connected===false){if(TOWER_RD.conn)towerRdReset();return;}
 if(TOWER_RD.conn!==conn){towerRdReset();TOWER_RD.conn=conn;TOWER_RD.off=conn.onAny?.(packet=>{
  if(!CFG.towerRd||packet.name!=='RPCPlayerAttack')return;
  const id=idOf(packet.payload?.id),entity=registry.entities.get(id),def=towerRdDefinition(entity);if(!def)return;
  const pre=Math.max(0,Number(packet.payload?.pre_timeout)||0),post=Math.max(0,Number(packet.payload?.post_timeout)||0),duration=Math.max(def.timeout,post)*1000;
  if(duration>0)TOWER_RD.attacks.set(id,{entity,start:performance.now()+pre*1000,duration});
 })||null;}
 const now=performance.now(),live=new Set();ctx.save();
 try{ctx.lineWidth=1.3;ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,190,70,.8)';ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,170,40,.025)';
 for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
  const def=towerRdDefinition(entity);if(!def)continue;
  const id=idOf(entity.id);live.add(id);const pos=tracerEntityPosition(entity);if(!pos)continue;
  const visibility=entity.get?.('visibility')?.server;if(typeof visibility==='number'&&visibility<.5)continue;
  const points=[];for(let i=0;i<48;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/24,p=w2s(pos.x+Math.cos(a)*def.radius,pos.y+Math.sin(a)*def.radius);if(!Number.isFinite(p.sx)||!Number.isFinite(p.sy))break;points.push(p);}
  if(points.length===48){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(points[0].sx,points[0].sy);for(let i=1;i<48;i++)ctx.lineTo(points[i].sx,points[i].sy);ctx.closePath();ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,170,40,.025)';ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();}
  const record=TOWER_RD.attacks.get(id);if(!record||record.entity!==entity)continue;
  const progress=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,(now-record.start)/record.duration));if(progress>=1){TOWER_RD.attacks.delete(id);continue;}
  const p=w2s(pos.x,pos.y);if(!Number.isFinite(p.sx)||!Number.isFinite(p.sy))continue;
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.7)';ctx.fillRect(p.sx-24,p.sy+22,48,5);ctx.fillStyle='#ffc05a';ctx.fillRect(p.sx-23,p.sy+23,46*progress,3);
  ctx.font='10px Arial';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText('≈'+(Math.max(0,record.start+record.duration-now)/1000).toFixed(1)+' '+(uiLanguage==='en'?'s':'с'),p.sx,p.sy+40);
 }
 for(const id of TOWER_RD.attacks.keys())if(!live.has(id))TOWER_RD.attacks.delete(id);
 }finally{ctx.restore();}
}

const RESOURCE_STYLE={'BlackFern':{'color':"#6464e6",'size':0x3},'Cactus':{'color':'#a1fc56','size':0x2},'CopperStone':{'color':"#b87511",'size':0x3},'DeadTree':{'color':"#ae4ac2",'size':0x2},'DesertBushSpawner':{'color':'#646400','size':0x2},'GoldStone':{'color':'#f5ff40','size':0x3},'IceSpawner':{'color':'#323232','size':0x2},'SnowGoldStone':{'color':'#e9f069','size':0x3},'SnowStone':{'color':"#999999",'size':0x3},'SnowTree':{'color':"#2e630b",'size':0x2},'SnowTree2':{'color':'#2e630b','size':0x2},'Stone':{'color':"#545454",'size':0x3},'TimeriteStone':{'color':'#8a1e39','size':0x3},'Tree':{'color':'#15e642','size':0x2}};

const resourcePanel=document.createElement('div');
resourcePanel.style.cssText='position:fixed;right:16px;top:160px;width:220px;z-index:2147483645;background:transparent;border:1px solid #555;color:white;font:12px Arial;resize:none;overflow:hidden;pointer-events:auto';
const resourceTitle=document.createElement('div');resourceTitle.textContent='Карта ресурсов · зелёная точка — ты';resourceTitle.style.cssText='padding:8px;pointer-events:none';
const resourceCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');resourceCanvas.width=512;resourceCanvas.height=512;resourceCanvas.style.cssText='display:block;width:220px;height:220px;pointer-events:auto;cursor:grab;touch-action:none';
// Only this canvas background is translucent; resource and player marks stay opaque.
const resourceContext=resourceCanvas.getContext('2d');
if(resourceContext){
 const nativeFillRect=resourceContext.fillRect;
 resourceContext.fillRect=function(x,y,w,h){
  const background=x===0&&y===0&&w===resourceCanvas.width&&h===resourceCanvas.height&&
   ['#000000','#090b0b','#262626'].includes(String(this.fillStyle).toLowerCase());
  if(!background)return nativeFillRect.call(this,x,y,w,h);
  const alpha=this.globalAlpha;
  try{this.globalAlpha=alpha*0.78;return nativeFillRect.call(this,x,y,w,h);}
  finally{this.globalAlpha=alpha;}
 };
 cleanups.push(()=>{resourceContext.fillRect=nativeFillRect;});
}
const resourceNote=document.createElement('div');resourceNote.style.cssText='padding:7px;color:#bbb';resourceNote.textContent='Вся игровая карта · зелёная точка — ты';
resourcePanel.append(resourceCanvas);resourcePanel.hidden=true;(document.body||document.documentElement).appendChild(resourcePanel);


cleanups.push(()=>resourcePanel.remove());


let mapGesture=null,mapReplaying=false;
function mapUnderlying(x,y){
 const previous=resourcePanel.style.visibility;resourcePanel.style.visibility='hidden';
 try{return document.elementFromPoint(x,y);}finally{resourcePanel.style.visibility=previous;}
}
function replayMapClick(g,e){
 const target=g.target;if(!target?.isConnected)return;
 const common={bubbles:true,cancelable:true,composed:true,view:window,
  clientX:g.x,clientY:g.y,screenX:g.screenX,screenY:g.screenY,
  button:g.button,ctrlKey:e.ctrlKey,shiftKey:e.shiftKey,altKey:e.altKey,metaKey:e.metaKey};
 const pointer={pointerId:g.id,pointerType:g.pointerType,isPrimary:true,width:1,height:1};
 mapReplaying=true;
 try{
  target.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerdown',{...common,...pointer,buttons:g.buttons,pressure:0.5}));
  target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown',{...common,buttons:g.buttons,detail:1}));
  target.dispatchEvent(new PointerEvent('pointerup',{...common,...pointer,buttons:0,pressure:0}));
  target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mouseup',{...common,buttons:0,detail:1}));
  target.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(g.button===0?'click':'auxclick',{...common,buttons:0,detail:1}));
 }finally{mapReplaying=false;}
}
function mapGestureEvent(e){
 if(mapReplaying)return;
 const onMap=e.composedPath().includes(resourcePanel);
 if(!onMap&&!mapGesture)return;
 if(mapGesture&&e.pointerId!==undefined&&e.pointerId!==mapGesture.id)return;
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
 if(e.type==='pointerdown'){
  if(mapGesture)return;
  const rect=resourcePanel.getBoundingClientRect();
  mapGesture={id:e.pointerId,pointerType:e.pointerType,button:e.button,buttons:e.buttons,
   x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,screenX:e.screenX,screenY:e.screenY,left:rect.left,top:rect.top,
   dragging:false,target:mapUnderlying(e.clientX,e.clientY)};
  resourceCanvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
 }else if(e.type==='pointermove'&&mapGesture){
  const g=mapGesture,dx=e.clientX-g.x,dy=e.clientY-g.y;
  if(g.button===0&&Math.hypot(dx,dy)>=5)g.dragging=true;
  if(g.dragging){
   resourceCanvas.style.cursor='grabbing';resourcePanel.style.right='auto';
   resourcePanel.style.left=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerWidth-resourcePanel.offsetWidth,g.left+dx))+'px';
   resourcePanel.style.top=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerHeight-resourcePanel.offsetHeight,g.top+dy))+'px';
  }
 }else if(e.type==='pointerup'&&mapGesture){
  const g=mapGesture;cancelMapGesture();if(!g.dragging)replayMapClick(g,e);
 }else if(e.type==='pointercancel')cancelMapGesture();
}
function cancelMapGesture(){
 const g=mapGesture;mapGesture=null;resourceCanvas.style.cursor='grab';
 if(g)try{resourceCanvas.releasePointerCapture(g.id);}catch{}
}
const mapGestureEvents=['pointerdown','pointermove','pointerup','pointercancel','mousedown','mouseup','click','auxclick','dblclick','contextmenu'];
for(const type of mapGestureEvents)window.addEventListener(type,mapGestureEvent,{capture:true,passive:false});
window.addEventListener('blur',cancelMapGesture);
cleanups.push(()=>{cancelMapGesture();for(const type of mapGestureEvents)window.removeEventListener(type,mapGestureEvent,true);window.removeEventListener('blur',cancelMapGesture);});

// User-provided reward: 3 leaderboard points per destroyed resource.
// Identity remains a temporal correlation, not a server-reported attribution.
const RESOURCE_SCORES={conn:null,off:null,scores:null,time:0,awards:[]};
function resetResourceScores(){
 RESOURCE_SCORES.off?.();Object.assign(RESOURCE_SCORES,{conn:null,off:null,scores:null,time:0,awards:[]});
}
function resourceScoreSnapshot(entries,now){
 const state=RESOURCE_SCORES,next=new Map();
 for(const entry of entries||[]){const id=idOf(entry.id),score=Number(entry.score),name=cleanName(entry.rawName)||cleanName(entry.name);if(id&&Number.isFinite(score))next.set(id,{score,name});}
 if(state.scores&&now>state.time&&now-state.time<=10000){
  for(const [id,row]of next){const old=state.scores.get(id);if(old&&row.name&&row.score-old.score===3)state.awards.push({id,name:row.name,gain:row.score-old.score,from:state.time,to:now});}
 }
 state.scores=next.size?next:null;state.time=now;
 state.awards=state.awards.filter(row=>now-row.to<15000).slice(-512);
}
function syncResourceScores(registry,now){
 const state=RESOURCE_SCORES,conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry)||null;
 if(conn!==state.conn){
  resetResourceScores();for(const marker of MAP_REMOVALS.markers.values())marker.name=null;
  state.conn=conn;
  if(conn){
   resourceScoreSnapshot(GAME.d4.entries,now);
   state.off=conn.onAny?.(packet=>{
    if(!CFG.resourceMap||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||state.conn!==conn)return;
    if(packet.name==='Leaderboard'){
     const p=packet.payload||{};resourceScoreSnapshot((p.ids||[]).map((id,i)=>({id,score:p.scores?.[i],name:p.names?.[i]})),performance.now());
    }else if(packet.name==='MapData'){state.scores=null;state.awards=[];for(const marker of MAP_REMOVALS.markers.values())marker.name=null;}
   })||null;
  }
 }
 state.awards=state.awards.filter(row=>now-row.to<15000);
 const markers=[...MAP_REMOVALS.markers.values()].filter(marker=>now<marker.expires);
 const matches=markers.map(marker=>state.awards.filter(award=>marker.time>=award.from-1000&&marker.time<=award.to+1000));
 for(let i=0;i<markers.length;i++){
  const marker=markers[i];marker.name=null;
  if(now-marker.time<1500)continue;
  // Multiple updates from one player are not multiple candidate players.
  // Every leaderboard packet advances the baseline; only its exact +3 delta qualifies.
  const candidates=new Map(matches[i].map(award=>[award.id,award]));
  if(candidates.size!==1)continue;
  const award=[...candidates.values()][0];
  if(matches.filter(list=>list.some(other=>other.id===award.id)).length===1)marker.name=award.name;
 }
}


const MAP_REMOVALS={registry:null,width:0,height:0,previous:null,markers:new Map()};
function resetMapRemovals(){resetResourceScores();MAP_REMOVALS.registry=null;MAP_REMOVALS.previous=null;MAP_REMOVALS.markers.clear();}
cleanups.push(resetMapRemovals);
function trackMapRemovals(registry,map,now){
 const next=new Map();
 for(const t of map.terrain||[])if(t.id!=null&&RESOURCE_STYLE[GAME.at?.[t.ty]]&&Number.isFinite(t.nx)&&Number.isFinite(t.ny))next.set(String(t.id),{nx:t.nx,ny:t.ny});
 if(MAP_REMOVALS.registry!==registry||MAP_REMOVALS.width!==map.worldWidth||MAP_REMOVALS.height!==map.worldHeight){
  resetMapRemovals();MAP_REMOVALS.registry=registry;MAP_REMOVALS.width=map.worldWidth;MAP_REMOVALS.height=map.worldHeight;
 }
 // A complete map clear can be a reload. Do not report it as mass harvesting.
 if(next.size===0){resetResourceScores();MAP_REMOVALS.previous=null;MAP_REMOVALS.markers.clear();return;}
 if(MAP_REMOVALS.previous)for(const [id,p] of MAP_REMOVALS.previous){
  if(!next.has(id))MAP_REMOVALS.markers.set(id,{...p,time:now,expires:now+10000,name:null});
 }
 MAP_REMOVALS.previous=next;
 for(const [id,m] of MAP_REMOVALS.markers)if(now>=m.expires||next.has(id))MAP_REMOVALS.markers.delete(id);
}
function drawMapRemovals(ctx,now){
 ctx.save();
 for(const m of MAP_REMOVALS.markers.values()){
  if(now>=m.expires)continue;
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(m.nx*512,(1-m.ny)*512,4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle='#ff3030';ctx.fill();ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeStyle='#fff';ctx.stroke();
  if(m.name){
   ctx.font='bold 15px Arial';ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.textBaseline='bottom';
   const x=Math.max(2,Math.min(510-Math.min(508,ctx.measureText(m.name).width),m.nx*512+7)),y=Math.max(16,(1-m.ny)*512-6);
   ctx.strokeStyle='#000';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeText(m.name,x,y,508);ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillText(m.name,x,y,508);
  }
 }
 ctx.restore();
}


function updateResourceMap(){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,map=GAME.dT;
 resourcePanel.hidden=!CFG.resourceMap||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!registry;
 if(resourcePanel.hidden){resetMapRemovals();return;}
 trackMapRemovals(registry,map,performance.now());
 syncResourceScores(registry,performance.now());
 const ctx=resourceCanvas.getContext('2d');if(!ctx)return;
 // The game's own full-map renderer preserves resource colours, positions and icons.
 const render=client.dU;
 if(typeof render==='function'&&Function.prototype.toString.call(render).includes('getContext')){
  render(resourceCanvas,512,false,false);
 }else{
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,512,512);ctx.fillStyle='#090b0b';ctx.fillRect(0,0,512,512);
  for(const t of map.terrain||[]){
   const style=RESOURCE_STYLE[GAME.at?.[t.ty]];
   if(!style||!Number.isFinite(t.nx)||!Number.isFinite(t.ny))continue;
   ctx.fillStyle=style.color;ctx.fillRect(t.nx*512-style.size/2,(1-t.ny)*512-style.size/2,style.size,style.size);
  }
 }

 drawMapRemovals(ctx,performance.now());
 resourceNote.textContent=map.worldWidth>0?'Зелёная — ты · красная — ресурс исчез (10 с).':'Ожидание данных игровой карты…';
 if(!(map.worldWidth>0&&map.worldHeight>0))return;
 const entity=window.__t7LocalEntity__;
 const position=entity?tracerEntityPosition(entity):null;
 const mapLocal=(map.objects||[]).find(t=>t.friend===16);
 const nx=position?position.x/map.worldWidth:mapLocal?.nx??(mapLocal?mapLocal.x/512:NaN);
 const ny=position?position.y/map.worldHeight:mapLocal?.ny??(mapLocal?mapLocal.y/512:NaN);
 if(!Number.isFinite(nx)||!Number.isFinite(ny)||nx<0||ny<0||nx>1||ny>1)return;
 ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(nx*512,(1-ny)*512,5,0,Math.PI*2);
 ctx.shadowColor='#33ff33';ctx.shadowBlur=8;ctx.fillStyle='#33ff33';ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
}
const resourceInterval=setInterval(()=>{try{updateResourceMap();}catch(error){resourceNote.textContent='Карта: данные временно недоступны';}},100);
cleanups.push(()=>clearInterval(resourceInterval));


const HEALTH_TRACK={registry:null,records:new Map(),offAdded:null};
function healthNumber(value){const n=apValue(value);return typeof n==='number'&&Number.isFinite(n)?n:null;}
function updateHealthRecord(record){
    const entity=record.entity,data=entity.state?.get?.('HealthComponentRepl');
    if(data){
        const hp=healthNumber(data.health),max=healthNumber(data.max_health);
        if(hp!==null)record.hp=hp;
        if(max!==null)record.max=max;
    }
    const position=entity.state?.get?.('TransformComponentRepl');
    if(apValue(position?.position)!=null)record.positionReady=true;
}
function clearHealthTracking(){
    HEALTH_TRACK.offAdded?.();HEALTH_TRACK.offAdded=null;
    for(const record of HEALTH_TRACK.records.values())for(const off of record.off)off?.();
    HEALTH_TRACK.records.clear();HEALTH_TRACK.registry=null;
}
cleanups.push(clearHealthTracking);
function watchHealthEntity(entity){
    if(!entity?.state||entity.id==null)return;
    const id=String(entity.id),old=HEALTH_TRACK.records.get(id);
    if(old?.entity===entity)return;
    if(old)for(const off of old.off)off?.();
    const record={entity,hp:null,max:null,positionReady:false,off:[]};
    HEALTH_TRACK.records.set(id,record);
    if(typeof entity.state.subscribe==='function'){
        for(const component of ['HealthComponentRepl','TransformComponentRepl']){
            const off=entity.state.subscribe(component,()=>updateHealthRecord(record));
            if(typeof off==='function')record.off.push(off);
        }
    }
    updateHealthRecord(record);
}
function syncHealthTracking(){
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    if(!registry||String(registry.localId)!==window.__localId__){clearHealthTracking();return;}
    if(HEALTH_TRACK.registry!==registry){
        clearHealthTracking();HEALTH_TRACK.registry=registry;
        HEALTH_TRACK.offAdded=registry.onEntityAdded?.(watchHealthEntity);
    }
    for(const entity of registry.entities.values())watchHealthEntity(entity);
    for(const [id,record] of HEALTH_TRACK.records){
        if(registry.entities.get(id)!==record.entity){
            for(const off of record.off)off?.();HEALTH_TRACK.records.delete(id);
        }
    }
}
function entityHealth(entity){
    const record=HEALTH_TRACK.records.get(String(entity.id));
    if(!record||record.entity!==entity)return null;
    updateHealthRecord(record);
    const state=entity.get?.('health')?.state;
    // Buildings/resources can retain their maximum in sprite/layer state,
    // without the character health-bar behaviour. 0.1 is its uninitialized default.
    let max=record.max??healthNumber(state?.maxHealth);
    if(max===null){
        for(const key of ['stateSprite','layerState']){
            const candidate=healthNumber(entity.get?.(key)?.maxHealth);
            if(candidate!==null&&candidate>0&&candidate!==0.1){max=candidate;break;}
        }
    }
    if(max!==null)record.max=max;
    const hp=record.hp??healthNumber(state?.health);
    if(hp===null||max===null||max<=0||hp<=0||hp>=max)return null;
    return {hp,max,ratio:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,hp/max))};
}
function drawHealthBars(ctx,W,H){
    syncHealthTracking();
    if(!CFG.healthBars)return;
    const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
    if(!registry||String(registry.localId)!==window.__localId__)return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.lineWidth=1;
    for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
        if(!entity)continue;
        const health=entityHealth(entity),record=HEALTH_TRACK.records.get(String(entity.id));
        const position=record?.positionReady&&Number.isFinite(entity.rx)&&Number.isFinite(entity.ry)?{x:entity.rx,y:entity.ry}:null;
        if(!health||!position)continue;
        const offset=Number.isFinite(entity.prefab?.healthOffset)?Math.max(0.35,entity.prefab.healthOffset):1;
        const point=w2s(position.x,position.y-Math.min(offset,0.65));
        if(!Number.isFinite(point.sx)||!Number.isFinite(point.sy)||point.sx<0||point.sx>W||point.sy<0||point.sy>H-12)continue;
        const width=36,height=5,x=Math.round(point.sx-width/2),y=Math.round(point.sy+6);
        ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.85)';ctx.fillRect(x-1,y-1,width+2,height+2);
        ctx.fillStyle='#452b2e';ctx.fillRect(x,y,width,height);
        ctx.fillStyle=health.ratio>0.5?'#53dc79':health.ratio>0.25?'#f4c95d':'#f06464';
        ctx.fillRect(x,y,Math.max(1,Math.round(width*health.ratio)),height);
    }
    ctx.restore();
}

function drawOwnerLabel(ctx, owner, sx, sy, W, H) {
    const maxWidth = Math.max(1, W-16);
    let text = ownerText(owner);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.font = 'bold 11px Arial';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
    if (ctx.measureText(text).width > maxWidth) {
        while (text.length && ctx.measureText(text+'…').width > maxWidth) text = text.slice(0,-1);
        text += '…';
    }
    const half = Math.min(maxWidth, ctx.measureText(text).width)/2;
    const x = Math.max(8+half, Math.min(W-8-half, sx));
    // At the bottom edge, place owner above the type label rather than over it.
    const y = Math.max(2, sy+22 <= H ? sy+8 : sy-34);
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#000';
    ctx.fillStyle = owner?.id ? '#fff' : '#bbb';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.strokeText(text, x, y, maxWidth);
    ctx.fillText(text, x, y, maxWidth);
    ctx.restore();
}

// Shared equipment transaction: inventory -> equipment -> inventory.
// Uses verified exported handlers and replicated cursor/equipment state.
const AUTO={fastBoosts:false,traps:false,boosts:false,hats:false,shield:false,delay:41,bobReleaseMs:2000,lowHp:30,enemyRadius:3,threatRadius:10,threatLeadMs:4000,closeBoomRadius:5,shieldPreemptRadius:2,shieldQuietMs:550,shieldMinHoldMs:850};
const GEAR={tx:null,halted:false,status:'Выключено',held:false,registry:null,baseHat:null,observedHat:null,manualHatRule:null,autoHatExpected:null,bobUntil:0,bobWeapon:null,baseShield:null,shots:new Map(),closeBoomPlayers:new Set(),rpcThreats:new Map(),ignoredRpcOwners:new Map(),rpcEntities:new Set(),rpcOriginals:new Map(),rpcPatched:new Map(),lastThreat:0,lastSlots:{hat:null,shield:null},wasThreatened:false,shieldHoldUntil:0,lastThreatId:null};
// RPCPlayerAttack is emitted when a remote player starts the attack animation,
// before the projectile entity is replicated.  The current protocol assigns 93
// to this RPC; the string check keeps the hook tolerant of a readable adapter.
const RPC_PLAYER_ATTACK=93;
const reflectAttackWeapons=new Set([
    'FireballWeapon',
    "ThrowingKnife","DarkThrowingKnife","CopperThrowingKnife","BronzeThrowingKnife","DaemonKnife","Kunai","Lollipop",
    'Bow','GoldenBow','ChitinBow','PhantomBow','PolarBow','PremiumBow',
    'Boomerang','DarkBoomerang',
    'StickyPotion','ImprovedStickyPotion','ChristmasPotion','PolarBombPotion',
    'GrenadeToxic','GrenadeSmoke','GrenadeIce','GrenadeFire','GrenadeSticky'
]);
const reflectBlockedWeapons=new Set(['SantaWeapon','ChristmasWand']);
function gearRpcNumber(payload,key){
    const direct=payload?.[key];
    if(typeof direct==='number'&&Number.isFinite(direct))return direct;
    if(typeof direct==='string'&&direct.trim()!==''){
        const value=Number(direct);if(Number.isFinite(value))return value;
    }
    if(direct&&typeof direct==='object'){
        const value=Number(direct.value??direct.number??direct.val);
        if(Number.isFinite(value))return value;
    }
    if(!(payload instanceof Uint8Array))return undefined;
    const fields={pre_timeout:2,post_timeout:3,attack_type:4,item_type:5};
    const wanted=fields[key];if(wanted==null)return undefined;
    let p=0;
    const readVar=()=>{let n=0,shift=0;while(p<payload.length&&shift<56){const b=payload[p++];n+=Number(b&127)*2**shift;if(!(b&128))return n;shift+=7;}return undefined;};
    while(p<payload.length){
        const tag=readVar();if(!Number.isFinite(tag))break;
        const field=tag>>>3,wire=tag&7;
        if(field===wanted){
            if(wire===0)return readVar();
            if(wire===1&&p+8<=payload.length){const value=new DataView(payload.buffer,payload.byteOffset+p,8).getFloat64(0,true);p+=8;return value;}
            if(wire===5&&p+4<=payload.length){const value=new DataView(payload.buffer,payload.byteOffset+p,4).getFloat32(0,true);p+=4;return value;}
        }
        if(wire===0)readVar();
        else if(wire===1)p+=8;
        else if(wire===2){const len=readVar();p+=Number(len)||0;}
        else if(wire===5)p+=4;
        else break;
    }
    return undefined;
}
function gearRpcAttackItem(payload,entity){
    const item=gearRpcNumber(payload,'item_type');
    // Never infer the attack from an equipped item: it may already have changed.
    return Number.isInteger(item)?gearName(item):'';
}
function gearDetachAttackRpcs(){
    for(const entity of GEAR.rpcEntities){
        const original=GEAR.rpcOriginals.get(entity),patched=GEAR.rpcPatched.get(entity);
        if(original&&patched&&entity.processRPC===patched)entity.processRPC=original;
    }
    GEAR.rpcEntities.clear();GEAR.rpcOriginals.clear();GEAR.rpcPatched.clear();GEAR.rpcThreats.clear();GEAR.ignoredRpcOwners.clear();
}
function gearAttachAttackRpc(entity){
    if(!entity||entity.prefab?.type!=='Player'||typeof entity.processRPC!=='function'||GEAR.rpcEntities.has(entity))return;
    const original=entity.processRPC;
    const patched=function(type,payload){
        if(AUTO.shield&&(type===RPC_PLAYER_ATTACK||type==='RPCPlayerAttack')){
            const weapon=gearRpcAttackItem(payload,entity);
            const now=performance.now();
            if(!reflectAttackWeapons.has(weapon)){
                GEAR.rpcThreats.delete(String(entity.id));
            }else{
                const threat=gearReflectWindup(weapon,payload,now);
                if(threat){threat.aim=gearReflectAim(entity);GEAR.rpcThreats.set(String(entity.id),threat);}
                else GEAR.rpcThreats.delete(String(entity.id));
                queueUrgentGearTick?.();
            }
        }
        return original.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    entity.processRPC=patched;GEAR.rpcEntities.add(entity);GEAR.rpcOriginals.set(entity,original);GEAR.rpcPatched.set(entity,patched);
}
function gearReflectAction(weapon){
 const config=Object.values(clientModule).find(v=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&v.items&&v.build&&v.craft);
 const items=config?.items?.items??config?.items;
 const item=items?.[String(weapon).toLowerCase()];
 return item?.item_action??item?.itemAction??null;
}
function gearReflectWindup(weapon,payload,now){
 const action=gearReflectAction(weapon);
 // Keep the explicit weapon allowlist; a config flag never enables Santa or towers.
 if(!reflectAttackWeapons.has(weapon))return null;
 if(action?.allow_redirect===false||action?.allow_redirect===0||action?.allow_redirect==='false')return null;
 const rpcPre=gearRpcNumber(payload,'pre_timeout');
 const pre=Number.isFinite(rpcPre)?rpcPre:Number(action?.pre_timeout);
 const preMs=Number.isFinite(pre)?Math.max(0,Math.min(3000,pre*1000)):0;
 const rawSpeed=Number(action?.bullet_speed),rawRange=Number(action?.distance);
 const speed=Number.isFinite(rawSpeed)&&rawSpeed>0?rawSpeed:null;
 const range=Number.isFinite(rawRange)&&rawRange>0?rawRange:null;
 const reach=Math.min(10,range??10);
 const flightMs=speed?reach/speed*1000:0;
 return {weapon,fireAt:now+preMs,speed,range,
  until:now+preMs+(speed?Math.min(10000,flightMs)+500:Math.max(900,gearReflectLeadMs()))};
}
function gearReflectAim(entity){
 const raw=entity.state?.get?.('TransformComponentRepl')?.rotation;
 const replicated=raw&&typeof raw==='object'&&'value'in raw?raw.value:raw;
 const angle=typeof replicated==='number'&&Number.isFinite(replicated)?replicated:entity.root?.rotation?.z;
 if(!Number.isFinite(angle)||!Number.isFinite(entity.rx)||!Number.isFinite(entity.ry))return null;
 return {x:entity.rx,y:entity.ry,ux:Math.cos(angle),uy:Math.sin(angle)};
}
function gearReflectAimedAt(aim,local){
 if(!aim)return false;
 const dx=local.rx-aim.x,dy=local.ry-aim.y;
 const forward=dx*aim.ux+dy*aim.uy;
 const sideways=Math.abs(dx*aim.uy-dy*aim.ux);
 // Allow near misses on either side, while requiring a forward shot.
 return Number.isFinite(forward)&&forward>0&&sideways<=2.2;
}
function gearEarlyRpcThreat(registry,local,now){
 let danger=false;
 for(const [id,until] of GEAR.ignoredRpcOwners)if(until<=now)GEAR.ignoredRpcOwners.delete(id);
 for(const [id,threat] of GEAR.rpcThreats){
  const entity=registry.entities.get(id);
  if(!entity||entity.prefab?.type!=='Player'||!reflectAttackWeapons.has(threat.weapon)||threat.until<=now||String(entity.id)===String(local.id)||entity.isFriend?.()){GEAR.rpcThreats.delete(id);continue;}
  const distance=Math.hypot(entity.rx-local.rx,entity.ry-local.ry);
  if(!Number.isFinite(distance)||distance>10){GEAR.rpcThreats.delete(id);continue;}
  if(threat.range!=null&&distance>threat.range)continue;
  // Follow aiming during windup, then keep the last known firing direction.
  if(now<=threat.fireAt)threat.aim=gearReflectAim(entity)??threat.aim;
  if(!gearReflectAimedAt(threat.aim,local))continue;
  // Do not truncate to whole seconds before converting to milliseconds.
  const arrival=threat.speed?threat.fireAt+distance/threat.speed*1000:null;
  if(arrival!=null&&(arrival-now>gearReflectLeadMs()||now>arrival+500))continue;
  danger=true;GEAR.lastThreatId=id;if(distance<=4)GEAR.closeThreatThisFrame=true;
 }
 return danger;
}
cleanups.push(gearDetachAttackRpcs);
// Track the latest real client input; restore only a held click consumed as cursor-drop.
const ATTACK_SYNC={type:null,payload:null,conn:null,pending:false,restored:false};
function rememberAttackInput(conn,type,payload){
    if(!payload||typeof payload.primary_action!=='boolean'||typeof payload.primary_action_changed!=='boolean'||
       !Number.isFinite(payload.move?.x)||!Number.isFinite(payload.move?.y)||
       !Number.isFinite(payload.look?.x)||!Number.isFinite(payload.look?.y))return;
    ATTACK_SYNC.conn=conn;ATTACK_SYNC.type=type;
    ATTACK_SYNC.payload={...payload,move:{...payload.move},look:{...payload.look}};
    if(payload.primary_action)gearLatchRangedAttack();
}
const EQUIP_CAPTURE={input:null,original:null,patched:null,own:null};
function restoreEquipmentCapture(){
 const state=EQUIP_CAPTURE;
 if(state.input?.capturePrimaryUntilRelease===state.patched){if(state.own)Object.defineProperty(state.input,'capturePrimaryUntilRelease',state.own);else delete state.input.capturePrimaryUntilRelease;}
 Object.assign(state,{input:null,original:null,patched:null,own:null});
}
function syncEquipmentCapture(){
 const input=followInputGetter?.(),state=EQUIP_CAPTURE;if(input===state.input)return;
 restoreEquipmentCapture();if(!input||typeof input.capturePrimaryUntilRelease!=='function')return;
 const original=input.capturePrimaryUntilRelease,own=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(input,'capturePrimaryUntilRelease');
 if(own&&!own.writable)return;
 const patched=function(...args){
  const tx=GEAR.tx,cursor=gearCursor();
  const automatic=tx&&['hat','shield'].includes(tx.kind)&&DROP_GUARD.owned&&cursor>0&&(cursor===tx.type||cursor===tx.old);
  if(automatic&&GEAR.held&&!document.hidden&&document.hasFocus()&&GAME.be.scene==='gameplay'&&!GAME.be.paused&&!GAME.c7('inventory')&&!(trapBuildState()?.buildType>0))return;
  return original.apply(this,args);
 };
 input.capturePrimaryUntilRelease=patched;Object.assign(state,{input,original,patched,own});
}
cleanups.push(restoreEquipmentCapture);
function preserveHeldEquipmentInput(){
 syncEquipmentCapture();
 if(!GEAR.held||!GEAR.tx||!['hat','shield'].includes(GEAR.tx.kind)||FAST_BOOSTS.held||document.hidden||!document.hasFocus()||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||GAME.be.paused||GAME.c7('inventory'))return;
 const focus=document.activeElement;if(focus?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(focus?.tagName||''))return;
 const input=followInputGetter?.();if(!input||input.touch)return;
 // Preserve only the physical primary-button hold. Movement and aim remain native.
 input.primary=true;ATTACK_SYNC.nativeInput=input;ATTACK_SYNC.restored=true;
}
function resumeConsumedAttack(){
    if(!ATTACK_SYNC.pending||GEAR.tx||gearCursor()!==0)return;
    ATTACK_SYNC.pending=false;
    if(!GEAR.held||document.hidden||!document.hasFocus()||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||GAME.be.paused||GAME.c7('inventory'))return;
    if(FAST_BOOSTS.held)return;
    const focus=document.activeElement;if(focus?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(focus?.tagName||''))return;
    const input=followInputGetter?.();
    if(input&&!input.touch){input.primary=true;ATTACK_SYNC.nativeInput=input;ATTACK_SYNC.restored=true;return;}
    const state=ATTACK_SYNC,guard=DROP_GUARD.active;
    if(!state.payload||guard?.conn!==state.conn)return;
    guard.conn.send(state.type,{...state.payload,primary_action:true,primary_action_changed:true});
    state.restored=true;
}
function releaseRestoredAttack(){
    ATTACK_SYNC.pending=false;
    if(ATTACK_SYNC.nativeInput){ATTACK_SYNC.nativeInput.primary=false;ATTACK_SYNC.nativeInput=null;ATTACK_SYNC.restored=false;return;}
    if(!ATTACK_SYNC.restored)return;
    ATTACK_SYNC.restored=false;
    if(ATTACK_SYNC.payload&&DROP_GUARD.active?.conn===ATTACK_SYNC.conn)
        ATTACK_SYNC.conn.send(ATTACK_SYNC.type,{...ATTACK_SYNC.payload,primary_action:false,primary_action_changed:true});
}
cleanups.push(releaseRestoredAttack);
// Cancel only cursor-drop messages owned by an automatic equipment swap.
// Bind captures the connection of newly created game sessions; never touch input events.
const DROP_GUARD={connections:new WeakMap(),active:null,owned:false,blocked:0};
// Each binding owns its listeners and readiness state, including reused registries.
const AUTOMATION={sessions:new WeakMap(),active:null,serial:0};
// The current module does not export its packet enum. These codes are verified
// against its embedded protocol; use this fallback only for the verified layout.
const automationPackets=compatPacketTypes;
function resetAutomationRuntime(){
    GEAR.tx=null;DROP_GUARD.owned=false;
    DROP_GUARD.active?.restore?.();DROP_GUARD.active=null;
    Object.assign(ATTACK_SYNC,{conn:null,type:null,payload:null,pending:false,restored:false});
    gearDetachAttackRpcs();GEAR.shots.clear();GEAR.closeBoomPlayers.clear();
    GEAR.rpcThreats.clear();GEAR.ignoredRpcOwners.clear();
    Object.assign(GEAR,{registry:null,session:null,halted:false,held:false,baseHat:null,baseShield:null,
        observedHat:null,observedShield:null,autoHatExpected:undefined,autoShieldExpected:undefined,
        manualHatRule:null,bobUntil:0,bobWeapon:null,chasedUntil:0,combat:null,equipLatencyMs:0,
        lastThreat:0,wasThreatened:false,shieldHoldUntil:0,nextSwapAt:0,lastHatRuleAt:0});
    gearHomes.clear();gearHomeRegistry=null;
    hatsReset();
    GEAR.lastSlots={hat:null,shield:null};
    smartTrapReset();SMART_TRAPS.registry=null;SMART_TRAPS.session=null;
    BOOST.previous.clear();BOOST.pending.clear();BOOST.registry=null;
}
function resetAutomationSession(session){
    session.epoch=++AUTOMATION.serial;session.ready=false;session.inventorySeen=false;session.firstInventoryAt=null;
    session.lastInventory=performance.now();session.localId=null;
    session.cursorRevision=0;session.hatRevision=0;session.shieldRevision=0;session.inventoryRevision=0;
    if(AUTOMATION.active===session)resetAutomationRuntime();
}
function observeAutomationSession(registry,conn){
    AUTOMATION.sessions.get(registry)?.close();
    const session={registry,conn,closed:false,offs:[]};
    session.close=()=>{
        if(session.closed)return;session.closed=true;
        for(const off of session.offs.splice(0)){try{off();}catch(error){console.warn('[KiroHack] session listener cleanup',error);}}
        if(AUTOMATION.sessions.get(registry)===session){
            AUTOMATION.sessions.delete(registry);DROP_GUARD.connections.delete(registry);
        }
        if(AUTOMATION.active===session){resetAutomationRuntime();AUTOMATION.active=null;}
    };
    resetAutomationSession(session);
    AUTOMATION.sessions.set(registry,session);
    DROP_GUARD.connections.set(registry,conn);
    if(AUTOMATION.active&&AUTOMATION.active!==session)AUTOMATION.active.close();
    AUTOMATION.active=session;resetAutomationRuntime();
    window.__t7Registry__=registry;window.__t7LocalEntity__=null;
    if(automationPackets&&typeof conn.on==='function'){
        for(const [name,field] of [['ReplicateInventory','inventoryRevision'],['ReplicateInventoryCursor','cursorRevision'],
            ['ReplicateInventoryHatSlot','hatRevision'],['ReplicateInventoryShieldSlot','shieldRevision']]){
            if(!Number.isInteger(automationPackets[name]))continue;
            const off=conn.on(automationPackets[name],()=>{
                if(session.closed)return;
                session[field]++;session.lastInventory=performance.now();
                if(name==='ReplicateInventory'){session.inventorySeen=true;session.firstInventoryAt??=performance.now();}
                queueUrgentGearTick();
            });
            if(typeof off==='function')session.offs.push(off);
        }
    }
    return session;
}
function automationSessionReady(registry,local,now=performance.now()){
    const session=AUTOMATION.sessions.get(registry);
    if(disposed||!session||session.closed||AUTOMATION.active!==session||session.conn.connected===false||
        GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!local||!(GAME.c9.health>0)||
        String(registry.localId)!==String(local.id)||String(GAME.d4.localEntityId)!==String(local.id)||
        !registry.entities.get(String(local.id))||!Number.isFinite(local.rx)||!Number.isFinite(local.ry))return null;
    if(!session.inventorySeen)return null;
    if(!session.ready){
        // Let the initial inventory/cursor/equipment messages finish before starting a swap.
        if(now-session.firstInventoryAt<200)return null;
        session.ready=true;session.localId=String(local.id);
    }
    if(session.localId!==String(local.id))return null;
    return session;
}
// Use the verified native movement getter; never leave synthetic keys held.
const followInputGetter=typeof nativeClient.c1==='function'?nativeClient.c1:null;
const FOLLOW={input:null,original:null,patched:null,target:null,registry:null};
function followPlayers(){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,projection=typeof GAME.cB==='function'?GAME.cB():null;
 if(!registry||String(registry.localId)!==String(GAME.d4.localEntityId))return [];
 const list=[];
 for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
  const id=String(entity.id);if(id===String(registry.localId)||entity.prefab?.type!=='Player')continue;
  const health=apValue(entity.state?.get?.('HealthComponentRepl')?.health);if(typeof health==='number'&&health<=0)continue;
  const pos=projection?.entityPos?.(id);if(!pos||!Number.isFinite(pos.x)||!Number.isFinite(pos.y))continue;
  list.push({id,x:pos.x,y:pos.y,name:cleanName(GAME.cO.players?.[id]?.name)||id});
 }return list;
}
function followVector(manual){
 const idle=()=>{FOLLOW.target=null;return manual;},focus=document.activeElement;
 if(!CFG.followPlayers||disposed||document.hidden||!document.hasFocus()||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||GAME.be.paused||!(GAME.c9.health>0)||focus?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(focus?.tagName||'')||Math.hypot(manual.x,manual.y)>0.01)return idle();
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,projection=GAME.cB(),local=projection?.entityPos?.(String(GAME.d4.localEntityId));
 if(!local||!Number.isFinite(local.x)||!Number.isFinite(local.y)||String(registry?.localId)!==String(GAME.d4.localEntityId))return idle();
 if(FOLLOW.registry!==registry){FOLLOW.registry=registry;FOLLOW.target=null;}
 const players=followPlayers();let target;
 if(CFG.followTarget)target=players.find(p=>p.id===CFG.followTarget);
 else {target=players.find(p=>p.id===FOLLOW.target);if(!target)target=players.sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-local.x,a.y-local.y)-Math.hypot(b.x-local.x,b.y-local.y))[0];}
 if(!target)return idle();FOLLOW.target=target.id;
 const dx=target.x-local.x,dy=target.y-local.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy),distance=Math.max(1,Math.min(6,Number(CFG.followDistance)||2));
 return d<=distance?{x:0,y:0}:{x:dx/d,y:dy/d};
}
function followDetach(){if(FOLLOW.input?.moveVec===FOLLOW.patched)FOLLOW.input.moveVec=FOLLOW.original;Object.assign(FOLLOW,{input:null,original:null,patched:null,target:null,registry:null});}
function followAttach(){
 const input=followInputGetter?.();if(input===FOLLOW.input)return;followDetach();
 if(!input||typeof input.moveVec!=='function'||!(input.keys instanceof Set)||typeof input.setVirtualMove!=='function')return;
 const original=input.moveVec,patched=function(...args){const manual=original.apply(this,args);try{return CFG.aimbot&&AIMBOT.dodge?{...AIMBOT.dodge}:followVector(manual);}catch{return manual;}};
 input.moveVec=patched;Object.assign(FOLLOW,{input,original,patched});
}
function followRefresh(){
 followAttach();const select=document.getElementById('t7followtarget');if(!select)return;
 const players=followPlayers(),english=uiLanguage==='en',options=[['',english?'Nearest player':'Ближайший игрок'],...players.map(p=>[p.id,p.name+' · '+p.id])];
 if(CFG.followTarget&&!players.some(p=>p.id===CFG.followTarget))options.push([CFG.followTarget,(english?'Unavailable':'Недоступен')+' · '+CFG.followTarget]);
 const stamp=JSON.stringify(options);if(select.dataset.options!==stamp){select.replaceChildren(...options.map(([value,label])=>{const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=value;option.textContent=label;return option;}));select.dataset.options=stamp;}select.value=CFG.followTarget;
}
const followTimer=setInterval(()=>{try{followRefresh();}catch{}},500);
cleanups.push(()=>{clearInterval(followTimer);followDetach();});

const HIT_PARTICLES={conn:null,registry:null,localId:null,off:null,items:[]};
function clearHitParticles(){HIT_PARTICLES.off?.();Object.assign(HIT_PARTICLES,{conn:null,registry:null,localId:null,off:null,items:[]});}
function hitParticlesPacket(packet){
 if(!CFG.hitParticles||disposed||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||packet.name!=='RPCDamageReceive')return;
 const registry=HIT_PARTICLES.registry,localId=String(GAME.d4.localEntityId);
 if(registry!==window.__t7Registry__||localId!==HIT_PARTICLES.localId)return;
 const from=idOf(apValue(packet.payload?.from)),to=idOf(apValue(packet.payload?.to));
 if(!from||!to||to===localId)return;
 let owner=from;const visited=new Set();
 for(let n=0;owner!==localId&&n<8;n++){
  if(visited.has(owner))return;visited.add(owner);
  const source=registry.entities.get(owner);if(!source)return;
  const parent=parentFromEntity(source);if(!parent.known||!parent.id)return;owner=parent.id;
 }
 if(owner!==localId)return;
 const entity=registry.entities.get(to);if(entity?.prefab?.type!=='Player')return;
 const pos=window.__t7Projection__?.entityPos?.(to);if(!pos||!Number.isFinite(pos.x)||!Number.isFinite(pos.y))return;
 const now=performance.now(),kind=["sparks","stars","hearts","rings","confetti","snow"].includes(CFG.hitParticleStyle)?CFG.hitParticleStyle:"sparks";
 for(let n=0;n<(kind==='rings'?10:24);n++){
  const angle=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,speed=42+Math.random()*99;
  HIT_PARTICLES.items.push({kind,spin:(Math.random()-.5)*6,x:pos.x,y:pos.y,at:now,life:600+Math.random()*450,vx:Math.cos(angle)*speed,vy:Math.sin(angle)*speed-32,size:(kind==='sparks'?3:6)+Math.random()*4,color:kind==='hearts'?['#ff689c','#ffacd1','#ff4e74'][n%3]:kind==='confetti'?['#ffce63','#69dcff','#be95ff','#80e5aa'][n%4]:n%3===0?'#ffffff':n%3===1?'#78deff':'#b0a1ff'});
 }
 if(HIT_PARTICLES.items.length>360)HIT_PARTICLES.items.splice(0,HIT_PARTICLES.items.length-360);
}
function paintHitParticle(ctx,p,x,y,progress){
 const size=p.size*(1-progress*.6),kind=p.kind||'sparks';
 ctx.save();try{
 ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate((p.spin||0)*progress);ctx.globalAlpha=(1-progress)**2;ctx.fillStyle=p.color;ctx.strokeStyle=p.color;ctx.lineWidth=1.3;ctx.beginPath();
 if(kind==='stars'){
  for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/5-Math.PI/2,r=i%2?size*.45:size;const px=Math.cos(a)*r,py=Math.sin(a)*r;if(i)ctx.lineTo(px,py);else ctx.moveTo(px,py);}ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 }else if(kind==='hearts'){
  ctx.moveTo(0,size);ctx.bezierCurveTo(-size*2,-size*.2,-size,-size*1.8,0,-size*.6);ctx.bezierCurveTo(size,-size*1.8,size*2,-size*.2,0,size);ctx.fill();
 }else if(kind==='rings'){
  ctx.arc(0,0,size*(1+progress*4),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
 }else if(kind==='confetti'){
  ctx.fillRect(-size,-size*.4,size*2,size*.8);
 }else if(kind==='snow'){
  for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/3,c=Math.cos(a),s=Math.sin(a);ctx.moveTo(0,0);ctx.lineTo(c*size,s*size);for(const sign of [-1,1]){ctx.moveTo(c*size*.6,s*size*.6);ctx.lineTo(c*size*.4-sign*s*size*.25,s*size*.4+sign*c*size*.25);}}ctx.stroke();
 }else{ctx.arc(0,0,size,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
 }finally{ctx.restore();}
}

function drawHitParticles(ctx){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,localId=String(GAME.d4.localEntityId);
 if(!CFG.hitParticles||disposed||document.hidden||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!registry||String(registry.localId)!==localId){clearHitParticles();return;}
 const conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry);
 if(conn!==HIT_PARTICLES.conn||registry!==HIT_PARTICLES.registry||localId!==HIT_PARTICLES.localId){
  clearHitParticles();Object.assign(HIT_PARTICLES,{conn,registry,localId});
  const off=conn?.onAny?.(hitParticlesPacket);if(typeof off==='function')HIT_PARTICLES.off=off;
 }
 const now=performance.now();HIT_PARTICLES.items=HIT_PARTICLES.items.filter(p=>now-p.at<p.life);
 ctx.save();try{ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';
 for(const p of HIT_PARTICLES.items){
  const age=now-p.at,t=age/1000,q=w2s(p.x,p.y);if(!Number.isFinite(q.sx)||!Number.isFinite(q.sy))continue;
  const scalePoint=w2s(p.x+1,p.y),scale=Number.isFinite(scalePoint.sx)?Math.min(60,Math.max(8,Math.abs(scalePoint.sx-q.sx))):20;
  const x=q.sx+p.vx*t,y=q.sy-scale*.55+p.vy*t+75*t*t;
  paintHitParticle(ctx,p,x,y,age/p.life);
 }
 }finally{ctx.restore();}
}
cleanups.push(clearHitParticles);

function fastBuildCancel(){fastBuildStop();FAST_BUILD.pointer=null;}
function fastBuildHold(type,code){
 fastBuildStop();
 const session=automationSessionReady(window.__t7Registry__,window.__t7LocalEntity__);
 if(!session||!CFG.fastBuild)return;
 const held={type,code,session,epoch:session.epoch};FAST_BUILD.held=held;
 const repeat=()=>{
  if(FAST_BUILD.held!==held)return;
  const focus=document.activeElement,current=automationSessionReady(window.__t7Registry__,window.__t7LocalEntity__);
  if(disposed||!CFG.fastBuild||document.hidden||!document.hasFocus()||GAME.be.paused||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||current!==held.session||current?.epoch!==held.epoch||document.querySelector('dialog[open]')||focus?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(focus?.tagName||'')){fastBuildStop();return;}
  try{fastBuildPlace(type);}catch{fastBuildStop();return;}
  if(FAST_BUILD.held===held)FAST_BUILD.timer=setTimeout(repeat,16);
 };
 repeat();
}
listen(window,'keyup',e=>{if(e.code===FAST_BUILD.held?.code)fastBuildStop();},true);
