function fastBuildPoint(pointer,mode){
 // Invert the current world projection, including zoom and canvas placement.
 const o=w2s(0,0),a=w2s(1,0),b=w2s(0,1);
 if(![o.sx,o.sy,a.sx,a.sy,b.sx,b.sy,pointer.x,pointer.y].every(Number.isFinite))return null;
 const ax=a.sx-o.sx,ay=a.sy-o.sy,bx=b.sx-o.sx,by=b.sy-o.sy,det=ax*by-ay*bx;if(Math.abs(det)<1e-8)return null;
 const dx=pointer.x-o.sx,dy=pointer.y-o.sy;let x=(dx*by-dy*bx)/det,y=(ax*dy-ay*dx)/det;
 const snap=v=>{const base=Math.floor(v),fraction=v-base;return Math.abs(fraction-.5)<=1e-7?(base%2===0?base:base+1):Math.round(v);};
 if(mode==='Tile'){x=Math.floor(x/1.28)*1.28+.64;y=Math.floor(y/1.28)*1.28+.64;}
 else if(mode==='SubTile'){x=snap(x/1.28)*1.28;y=snap(y/1.28)*1.28;}
 return Number.isFinite(x)&&Number.isFinite(y)?{x,y}:null;
}
function fastBuildPlace(typeName){
 if(!CFG.fastBuild||!fastBuildNative||!featureAvailable('t7fastbuild')||disposed||document.hidden||GAME.be.paused||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return;
 const focus=document.activeElement;if(focus?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(focus?.tagName||''))return;
 const pointer=FAST_BUILD.pointer;if(!pointer||document.elementFromPoint(pointer.x,pointer.y)?.id!=='three-canvas')return;
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,local=window.__t7LocalEntity__,session=automationSessionReady(registry,local);if(!session)return;
 const defs=nativeClient.eg(),def=defs.find(d=>GAME.at[d.entityType]===typeName);if(!def||!nativeClient.eh(def)?.available)return;
 const point=fastBuildPoint(pointer,def.buildMode);if(!point||!Number.isInteger(compatPacketTypes?.InputBuildRequest))return;
 const degrees=Number(CFG.blockAngle),angle=CFG.blockRotation&&Number.isFinite(degrees)?((degrees%360)+360)%360*Math.PI/180:0;
 session.conn.send(compatPacketTypes.InputBuildRequest,{id:0,build_type:def.entityType,x:point.x,y:point.y,angle,test_pass:false});
}
listen(window,'blur',fastBuildCancel);cleanups.push(fastBuildCancel);

 }badgeApply();

const VISUAL_SKIN={entity:null,hooks:[],catalog:[]};
function visualSkinCatalog(){
 const raw=hatsConfig()?.skin?.skins,items=raw&&typeof raw==='object'?Object.values(raw):[];

function visualSkinDetach(){

 VISUAL_SKIN.hooks=[];VISUAL_SKIN.entity=null;
}
function visualSkinTick(){
 const select=document.getElementById('t7visualskinselect'),skins=visualSkinCatalog();VISUAL_SKIN.catalog=skins;
 if(select){const signature=JSON.stringify(skins.map(s=>[s.id,s.name]));if(select.dataset.catalog!==signature){select.replaceChildren(...skins.map(s=>{const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=String(s.id);option.textContent=s.name+' · '+s.id;return option;}));select.dataset.catalog=signature;}select.value=String(CFG.visualSkinId);}
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,id=GAME.d4.localEntityId;
 const entity=GAME.be.scene==='gameplay'&&registry&&String(registry.localId)===String(id)?registry.entities.get(String(id)):null;
 if(disposed||!CFG.visualSkin||!entity||entity.prefab?.type!=='Player'){if(VISUAL_SKIN.entity)visualSkinDetach();return;}
 if(VISUAL_SKIN.entity!==entity){
  visualSkinDetach();
  const visual=entity.get?.('visual'),equipment=entity.get?.('equipment');
  if(typeof visual?.applySkinIndex!=='function')return;
  const replicated=Number(apValue(entity.state?.get?.('AppearanceComponentRepl')?.skin_idx));
  const real=Number.isInteger(replicated)?replicated:skins.find(s=>s.spine===visual.skinName)?.id;
  if(!Number.isInteger(real))return;
  VISUAL_SKIN.entity=entity;
  for(const [object,key]of [[visual,'applySkinIndex'],[equipment,'setSkinIndex']]){
   if(typeof object?.[key]!=='function')continue;
   const h={object,key,original:object[key],real,applied:null};
   h.patched=function(index){h.real=index;const chosen=CFG.visualSkin&&VISUAL_SKIN.entity===entity&&VISUAL_SKIN.catalog.some(s=>s.id===Number(CFG.visualSkinId))?Number(CFG.visualSkinId):index;h.applied=chosen;return h.original.call(this,chosen);};
   object[key]=h.patched;VISUAL_SKIN.hooks.push(h);
  }
 }
 const chosen=Number(CFG.visualSkinId);if(!skins.some(s=>s.id===chosen))return;
 for(const h of VISUAL_SKIN.hooks)if(h.applied!==chosen){h.original.call(h.object,chosen);h.applied=chosen;}
}

function mode063Fan(enemy,local){
 const points=[[.8,0],[-.8,0],[0,.8],[0,-.8],[.6,.6],[-.6,.6],[.6,-.6],[-.6,-.6]].map(([x,y])=>({x:enemy.x+x,y:enemy.y+y}));
 const dx=local.rx-enemy.x,dy=local.ry-enemy.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy),d=dist<.1?1:dist,ux=dx/d,uy=dy/d;
 for(let row=0;row<5;row++)for(let col=-3;col<=3;col++){const forward=Math.min(1.2+row,Math.max(1.2,dist-1.5));points.push({x:enemy.x+ux*forward-uy*col,y:enemy.y+uy*forward+ux*col});}
 return points;
}

function mode063Enemy(c){
 const targets=[];for(const trap of c.registry.entities.values()){
  if(trap.prefab?.type!=='Trap')continue;const id=idOf(apValue(trap.state?.get?.('TrapComponentRepl')?.trapped_id));if(!id||id===String(c.local.id))continue;
  const e=c.registry.entities.get(id);if(e?.prefab?.type!=='Player'||e.isFriend?.())continue;
  const p=window.__t7Projection__?.entityPos?.(id);if(!p||!Number.isFinite(p.x)||!Number.isFinite(p.y)||Math.hypot(p.x-c.local.rx,p.y-c.local.ry)>c.radius)continue;
  targets.push({id,key:String(trap.id)+':'+id,x:p.x,y:p.y});
 }return targets.sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-c.local.rx,a.y-c.local.ry)-Math.hypot(b.x-c.local.rx,b.y-c.local.ry))[0];
}

}

const ITEM_SWAP={record:null,session:null,equipment:{},hatHold:null};

 if(!Number.isInteger(index)||index<0||index>=45||!Number.isInteger(compatPacketTypes?.InputSelectInventoryMessage))return false;
 session.conn.send(compatPacketTypes.InputSelectInventoryMessage,{index});return true;
}

}

}

}

const AIMBOT={target:null,point:null,dodge:null,tracks:new Map(),session:null,input:null,props:null};
function aimbotStop(){
 const s=AIMBOT;if(s.input&&s.props)for(const key of ['mouseX','mouseY']){const p=s.props[key];if(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(s.input,key)?.get===p.get)Object.defineProperty(s.input,key,{...p.descriptor,value:p.value});}
 s.input=null;s.props=null;s.target=null;s.point=null;s.dodge=null;s.tracks.clear();s.session=null;
}
function aimIntercept(dx,dy,vx,vy,speed){
 const a=vx*vx+vy*vy-speed*speed,b=2*(dx*vx+dy*vy),c=dx*dx+dy*dy;
 if(!(speed>0))return 0;
 if(Math.abs(a)<1e-6)return b<0?-c/b:0;
 const disc=b*b-4*a*c;if(disc<0)return 0;
 return [(-b-Math.sqrt(disc))/(2*a),(-b+Math.sqrt(disc))/(2*a)].filter(t=>t>=0&&Number.isFinite(t)).sort((a,b)=>a-b)[0]??0;
}
function aimbotBind(input){
 const s=AIMBOT;if(s.input===input)return;
 if(s.input)aimbotStop();
 const props={};for(const key of ['mouseX','mouseY']){const descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(input,key);if(!descriptor?.configurable||!('value'in descriptor))return;props[key]={descriptor,value:descriptor.value};}
 for(const key of ['mouseX','mouseY']){const p=props[key];p.get=()=>{
  const point=s.point,projection=window.__t7Projection__;if(!CFG.aimbot||!point||!projection)return p.value;
  const q=projection.worldToScreen(point.x,point.y);const value=key==='mouseX'?q.sx:q.sy;return Number.isFinite(value)?value:p.value;
 };Object.defineProperty(input,key,{configurable:true,enumerable:p.descriptor.enumerable,get:p.get,set:value=>{p.value=value;}});}
 s.input=input;s.props=props;
}
function aimbotTick(){
 const s=AIMBOT,now=performance.now(),registry=window.__t7Registry__,local=window.__t7LocalEntity__,focus=document.activeElement;
 const session=automationSessionReady(registry,local,now);
 if(!CFG.aimbot||!session||disposed||document.hidden||!document.hasFocus()||GAME.be.paused||GAME.c7('inventory')||trapBuildState()?.buildType>0||gearCursor()!==0||focus?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(focus?.tagName||'')){aimbotStop();return;}
 if(s.session!==session){aimbotStop();s.session=session;}
 const input=followInputGetter?.();if(!input||input.touch){aimbotStop();return;}aimbotBind(input);s.point=null;s.dodge=null;
 const config=hatsConfig(),items=config?.items?.items??config?.items,action=items?.[gearActiveWeapon().toLowerCase()]?.item_action,range=Number(action?.distance),speed=Number(action?.bullet_speed);
 const candidates=[],bullets=[],seen=new Set();
 for(const e of registry.entities.values()){
  const id=String(e.id);if(id===String(local.id))continue;
  const player=e.prefab?.type==='Player',bullet=e.prefab?.category==='projectile';if(!player&&!bullet)continue;
  const p=tracerEntityPosition(e);if(!p)continue;seen.add(id);
  const old=s.tracks.get(id),dt=old?(now-old.time)/1000:0;let vx=0,vy=0;
  if(old&&old.entity===e&&dt>.005&&dt<.3){vx=(p.x-old.x)/dt;vy=(p.y-old.y)/dt;if(Math.hypot(vx,vy)>100){vx=0;vy=0;}else if(!bullet){vx=.7*vx+.3*old.vx;vy=.7*vy+.3*old.vy;}}
  s.tracks.set(id,{entity:e,x:p.x,y:p.y,time:now,vx,vy});
  if(typeof e.isFriend==='function'&&e.isFriend())continue;
  if(player){if(typeof e.isFriend!=='function')continue;const hp=Number(apValue(e.state?.get?.('HealthComponentRepl')?.health));if(Number.isFinite(hp)&&hp<=0)continue;
   const d=Math.hypot(p.x-local.rx,p.y-local.ry);if(!(range>0)||d>range||!(Number(action?.damage)>0))continue;
   const visibility=e.get?.('visibility')?.server;if(typeof visibility==='number'&&visibility<.5)continue;
   candidates.push({id,...p,vx,vy,d});
  }else{const owner=parentFromEntity(e);if(owner.known&&owner.id){const ownerId=String(owner.id),parent=registry.entities.get(ownerId);if(ownerId===String(local.id)||parent?.isFriend?.())continue;}
   bullets.push({x:p.x,y:p.y,vx,vy});}
 }
 for(const id of s.tracks.keys())if(!seen.has(id))s.tracks.delete(id);
 const target=candidates.find(p=>p.id===s.target)||candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d)[0];s.target=target?.id??null;
 if(target){const lag=Math.min(.35,Math.max(0,Number(GAME.c9.ping)||0)/2000)+Math.max(0,Number(action.pre_timeout)||0),dx=target.x-local.rx+target.vx*lag,dy=target.y-local.ry+target.vy*lag,t=Math.min(2,aimIntercept(dx,dy,target.vx,target.vy,speed));s.point={x:target.x+target.vx*(lag+t),y:target.y+target.vy*(lag+t)};}
 let imminent=null;
 for(const b of bullets){const v2=b.vx*b.vx+b.vy*b.vy;if(v2<1)continue;const dx=local.rx-b.x,dy=local.ry-b.y,t=(dx*b.vx+dy*b.vy)/v2;if(t<0||t>.65)continue;const miss=Math.hypot(dx-b.vx*t,dy-b.vy*t);if(miss>.9||imminent&&t>=imminent.t)continue;imminent={...b,t,dx,dy};}
 if(imminent){const b=imminent,len=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy),sign=(-b.vy*b.dx+b.vx*b.dy)>=0?1:-1;s.dodge={x:-b.vy/len*sign,y:b.vx/len*sign};}
}
listen(window,'blur',aimbotStop);cleanups.push(aimbotStop);

const AUTO_ATTACK={intent:false,input:null,releaseAt:0,nextAt:0,session:null,conn:null,off:null};
function autoAttackRelease(){if(AUTO_ATTACK.input){AUTO_ATTACK.input.primary=false;AUTO_ATTACK.input=null;}AUTO_ATTACK.releaseAt=0;}
function autoAttackStop(){AUTO_ATTACK.intent=false;autoAttackRelease();AUTO_ATTACK.off?.();AUTO_ATTACK.off=null;AUTO_ATTACK.conn=null;AUTO_ATTACK.session=null;}
function autoAttackTarget(local,aim,action,registry){
 const length=Math.hypot(aim.x-local.rx,aim.y-local.ry);if(length<.001)return false;
 const ux=(aim.x-local.rx)/length,uy=(aim.y-local.ry)/length,range=Number(action.distance),melee=String(action.tag).toLowerCase()==='melee';
 if(!(range>0))return false;
 const half=Math.max(0,Math.min(180,Number(action.attack_angle)||90))*Math.PI/360;
 for(const e of registry.entities.values()){
  if(e.prefab?.type!=='Player'||String(e.id)===String(local.id)||typeof e.isFriend!=='function'||e.isFriend())continue;
  const health=Number(apValue(e.state?.get?.('HealthComponentRepl')?.health));if(Number.isFinite(health)&&health<=0)continue;
  const p=window.__t7Projection__?.entityPos?.(String(e.id));if(!p||!Number.isFinite(p.x)||!Number.isFinite(p.y))continue;
  const dx=p.x-local.rx,dy=p.y-local.ry,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy),forward=dx*ux+dy*uy;if(d>range||forward<=0)continue;
  if(melee?forward/Math.max(d,.001)>=Math.cos(half):Math.abs(dx*uy-dy*ux)<=.45)return true;
 }return false;
}
function autoAttackTick(){
 const now=performance.now(),focus=document.activeElement,registry=window.__t7Registry__,local=window.__t7LocalEntity__;
 const session=automationSessionReady(registry,local,now);
 if(!CFG.autoAttack||disposed||!session||document.hidden||!document.hasFocus()||GAME.be.paused||(!GEAR.tx&&gearCursor()!==0)||GAME.c7('inventory')||trapBuildState()?.buildType>0||document.querySelector('dialog[open]')||focus?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(focus?.tagName||'')){autoAttackStop();return;}
 if(AUTO_ATTACK.session!==session){autoAttackStop();AUTO_ATTACK.nextAt=0;AUTO_ATTACK.session=session;}
 if(AUTO_ATTACK.conn!==session.conn){AUTO_ATTACK.off?.();AUTO_ATTACK.conn=session.conn;const off=session.conn.onAny?.(packet=>{
  if(packet.name!=='RPCPlayerAttack'||String(apValue(packet.payload?.id))!==String(GAME.d4.localEntityId))return;
  const pre=Number(apValue(packet.payload?.pre_timeout)),post=Number(apValue(packet.payload?.post_timeout));
  if(Number.isFinite(pre)&&Number.isFinite(post))AUTO_ATTACK.nextAt=Math.max(AUTO_ATTACK.nextAt,performance.now()+Math.max(0,pre+post)*1000);
 });AUTO_ATTACK.off=typeof off==='function'?off:null;}
 AUTO_ATTACK.intent=false;
 if(GEAR.tx)autoAttackRelease();
 else if(AUTO_ATTACK.input&&now>=AUTO_ATTACK.releaseAt)autoAttackRelease();
 const input=followInputGetter?.();if(!input||input.touch||FAST_BOOSTS.held)return;
 const pointer=FAST_BUILD.pointer;if(!pointer||document.elementFromPoint(pointer.x,pointer.y)?.id!=='three-canvas')return;
 const config=hatsConfig(),items=config?.items?.items??config?.items,action=items?.[gearActiveWeapon().toLowerCase()]?.item_action;
 if(!action||!['meleeweapon','rangedweapon','throwingweapon'].includes(String(action.type).toLowerCase())||!(Number(action.damage)>0))return;
 if(Number.isFinite(Number(GAME.c9.stamina))&&Number(GAME.c9.stamina)<Number(action.stamina||0))return;
 const aim=CFG.aimbot&&AIMBOT.point?AIMBOT.point:fastBuildPoint(pointer,'Free');if(!aim||(!(CFG.aimbot&&AIMBOT.target)&&!autoAttackTarget(local,aim,action,registry)))return;
 AUTO_ATTACK.intent=true;
 // Prepare equipment before pressing attack; keep intent throughout confirmed swaps.
 if(AUTO.hats||AUTO.shield)autoGearTick();
 if(GEAR.tx||gearCursor()!==0||input.primary||now<AUTO_ATTACK.nextAt)return;
 const progress=Number(GAME.bS.activeProgress),speed=Number(GAME.bS.activeProgressSpeed),started=Number(GAME.bS.activeProgressStartedAt);
 if(speed>0&&Number.isFinite(progress)&&Number.isFinite(started)&&progress+Math.max(0,now-started)/1000*speed<1)return;
 const delay=(Math.max(0,Number(action.pre_timeout)||0)+Math.max(0,Number(action.post_timeout)||0))*1000;
 AUTO_ATTACK.nextAt=now+Math.max(50,delay);
 AUTO_ATTACK.input=input;AUTO_ATTACK.releaseAt=now+50;input.primary=true;
}
listen(window,'pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button===0&&e.isTrusted)AUTO_ATTACK.input=null;},true);
listen(window,'blur',autoAttackStop);cleanups.push(autoAttackStop);

const RANDOM_SKIN={connections:new WeakSet(),rewriting:new WeakSet(),last:null};
function randomSkinDecode(frame){
 if(!(frame instanceof Uint8Array)||frame[1]!==compatPacketTypes?.Spawn)return null;
 const names=['','nickname','skin_idx','primary_color_idx','secondary_color_idx','start_resources','ad_skin','hide_badges','session','spawn_code','item_indexes','item_skin_indexes','env','character_material_dna','weapon_material_dna','artifact_indexes'];
 let p=2;const result={},seen=new Set(),strings=new Set([1,8,9,12,13,14]),arrays=new Set([10,11,15]);
 function read(end=frame.length){let n=0;for(let shift=0;shift<=28;shift+=7){if(p>=end)throw Error('Truncated');const b=frame[p++];n+=(b&127)*2**shift;if(!(b&128)){if(n>4294967295)throw Error('Overflow');return n;}}throw Error('Varint');}
 try{while(p<frame.length){const tag=read(),field=tag>>>3,wire=tag&7,key=names[field];if(!key||seen.has(field)&&!arrays.has(field))return null;seen.add(field);
 if(strings.has(field)){if(wire!==2)return null;const length=read();if(p+length>frame.length)return null;result[key]=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(frame.subarray(p,p+length));p+=length;}
 else if(field===3||field===4){if(wire!==5||p+4>frame.length)return null;result[key]=new DataView(frame.buffer,frame.byteOffset+p,4).getFloat32(0,true);p+=4;if(!Number.isFinite(result[key]))return null;}
 else if(arrays.has(field)){const values=result[key]??=[];if(wire===0)values.push(read());else if(wire===2){const length=read(),end=p+length;if(end>frame.length)return null;while(p<end)values.push(read(end));}else return null;}
 else{if(wire!==0)return null;const value=read();if(field!==2&&value>1)return null;result[key]=field===2?value:!!value;}
 }return result;}catch{return null;}
}
function randomSkinAttach(conn){
 if(!compatEquipmentTransport||!Number.isInteger(compatPacketTypes?.Spawn)||!conn?.outbound||typeof conn.send!=='function'||RANDOM_SKIN.connections.has(conn))return;
 const queue=conn.outbound,hooks=[];
 for(const key of ['enqueueReliable','enqueueLatest']){const original=queue[key],d=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(queue,key);if(typeof original!=='function'||d&&d.writable===false&&!d.set)return;hooks.push({key,original});}
 for(const hook of hooks){hook.patched=function(...args){
  if(CFG.randomSkin&&!disposed&&!RANDOM_SKIN.rewriting.has(conn)){
   const payload=randomSkinDecode(args[hook.key==='enqueueLatest'?1:0]);
   if(payload){const pool=[0,27,45].filter(n=>n!==RANDOM_SKIN.last),skin=pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)];payload.skin_idx=skin;payload.ad_skin=true;
    RANDOM_SKIN.rewriting.add(conn);try{const result=conn.send(compatPacketTypes.Spawn,payload);RANDOM_SKIN.last=skin;return result;}finally{RANDOM_SKIN.rewriting.delete(conn);}
   }
  }return hook.original.apply(this,args);
 };queue[hook.key]=hook.patched;}
 RANDOM_SKIN.connections.add(conn);cleanups.push(()=>{for(const h of hooks)if(queue[h.key]===h.patched)queue[h.key]=h.original;RANDOM_SKIN.connections.delete(conn);});
}

const BLOCK_ROTATION={connections:new WeakMap(),rewriting:new WeakSet()};
function rotationBuildPayload(frame){
 if(!(frame instanceof Uint8Array)||frame.length<2||frame[1]!==compatPacketTypes?.InputBuildRequest)return null;
 let p=2;const result={id:0,build_type:0,x:0,y:0,angle:0,test_pass:false},seen=new Set();
 const readVar=()=>{let n=0;for(let shift=0;shift<=28;shift+=7){if(p>=frame.length)throw Error('Truncated');const b=frame[p++];n+=(b&127)*2**shift;if(!(b&128))return n;}throw Error('Varint');};
 try{while(p<frame.length){const tag=readVar(),field=tag>>>3,wire=tag&7;if(field<1||field>6||seen.has(field))return null;seen.add(field);
  if([1,2,6].includes(field)){if(wire!==0)return null;const n=readVar();if(field===1)result.id=n;else if(field===2)result.build_type=n;else {if(n>1)return null;result.test_pass=!!n;}}
  else{if(wire!==5||p+4>frame.length)return null;const n=new DataView(frame.buffer,frame.byteOffset+p,4).getFloat32(0,true);p+=4;if(!Number.isFinite(n))return null;result[{3:'x',4:'y',5:'angle'}[field]]=n;}
 }return result.id===0&&result.build_type>0?result:null;}catch{return null;}
}
function rotationAttach(conn){
 if(!compatEquipmentTransport||!Number.isInteger(compatPacketTypes?.InputBuildRequest)||!conn?.outbound||typeof conn.send!=='function'||BLOCK_ROTATION.connections.has(conn))return;
 const queue=conn.outbound,hooks=[];
 for(const key of ['enqueueReliable','enqueueLatest']){const original=queue[key],descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(queue,key);if(typeof original!=='function'||descriptor&&descriptor.writable===false&&!descriptor.set)return;hooks.push({key,original});}
 for(const hook of hooks){hook.patched=function(...args){
  if(CFG.blockRotation&&!disposed&&GAME.be.scene==='gameplay'&&!BLOCK_ROTATION.rewriting.has(conn)){
   const payload=rotationBuildPayload(args[hook.key==='enqueueLatest'?1:0]);
   if(payload){const degrees=Number(CFG.blockAngle);if(Number.isFinite(degrees)){
    // Re-encode through the game's sender, preserving its checksum and queue policy.
    payload.angle=((degrees%360)+360)%360*Math.PI/180;BLOCK_ROTATION.rewriting.add(conn);
    try{return conn.send(compatPacketTypes.InputBuildRequest,payload);}finally{BLOCK_ROTATION.rewriting.delete(conn);}
   }}
  }return hook.original.apply(this,args);
 };queue[hook.key]=hook.patched;}
 BLOCK_ROTATION.connections.set(conn,hooks);
 cleanups.push(()=>{for(const hook of hooks)if(queue[hook.key]===hook.patched)queue[hook.key]=hook.original;BLOCK_ROTATION.connections.delete(conn);});
}

const bindOriginal=GAME.eC?.prototype?.bind;
function bindWithDropGuard(conn){
    if(!conn||typeof conn.send!=='function')return bindOriginal.apply(this,arguments);
    const session=observeAutomationSession(this,conn);
    rotationAttach(conn);
    randomSkinAttach(conn);
    let unbind;
    try{unbind=bindOriginal.apply(this,arguments);}catch(error){session.close();throw error;}
    return function(...args){session.close();return unbind?.apply(this,args);};
}
if(typeof bindOriginal==='function')GAME.eC.prototype.bind=bindWithDropGuard;
cleanups.push(()=>{AUTOMATION.active?.close();if(GAME.eC?.prototype?.bind===bindWithDropGuard)GAME.eC.prototype.bind=bindOriginal;});
const automationClearOriginal=GAME.eC?.prototype?.clear;
if(typeof automationClearOriginal==='function'){
    const patched=function(...args){
        const session=AUTOMATION.sessions.get(this);
        if(session&&!session.closed)resetAutomationSession(session);
        return automationClearOriginal.apply(this,args);
    };
    GAME.eC.prototype.clear=patched;
    cleanups.push(()=>{if(GAME.eC.prototype.clear===patched)GAME.eC.prototype.clear=automationClearOriginal;});
}

// Current transport binds send as a non-writable own property. Guard its queue instead.
function readGearInputFrame(frame){
    if(!(frame instanceof Uint8Array)||frame[1]!==85)return null;
    let p=2;
    const readVar=()=>{let n=0,shift=0,b;do{if(p>=frame.length||shift>28)throw Error('varint');b=frame[p++];n+=(b&127)*2**shift;shift+=7;}while(b&128);return n;};
    const input={primary_action_changed:false,primary_action:false,look:{x:0,y:0},move:{x:0,y:0}};
    try{
        while(p<frame.length){
            const tag=readVar(),field=tag>>>3,wire=tag&7;
            if(wire===0){const value=readVar();if(field===1)input.primary_action_changed=!!value;else if(field===2)input.primary_action=!!value;}
            else if(wire===2){const len=readVar(),end=p+len;if(end>frame.length)return null;
                if(field===3||field===4){const vector=field===3?input.look:input.move;
                    while(p<end){const t=readVar();if((t&7)!==5||p+4>end)return null;const value=new DataView(frame.buffer,frame.byteOffset+p,4).getFloat32(0,true);p+=4;if((t>>>3)===1)vector.x=value;else if((t>>>3)===2)vector.y=value;}
                }else p=end;
            }else return null;
        }
        return input;
    }catch{return null;}
}
function installQueueDropGuard(conn){
    const queue=conn.outbound;
    if(!queue||!['enqueueReliable','enqueueLatest'].every(k=>typeof queue[k]==='function'))return false;
    const hooks=[];
    for(const key of ['enqueueReliable','enqueueLatest']){
        const original=queue[key],own=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(queue,key);
        if(own&&!own.writable&&!own.set)return false;
        hooks.push({key,original,own});
    }
    const guard={conn,hatCode:127,shieldCode:113,isValid:()=>hooks.every(h=>queue[h.key]===h.patched)};
    for(const hook of hooks){
        hook.patched=function(...args){
            const frame=args[hook.key==='enqueueLatest'?1:0];
            if(frame instanceof Uint8Array){
                if(frame[1]===173&&(GEAR.tx||DROP_GUARD.owned)){
                    DROP_GUARD.blocked++;if(GEAR.held)ATTACK_SYNC.pending=true;return;
                }
                const input=readGearInputFrame(frame);
                if(input){
                    rememberAttackInput(conn,85,input);
                }
            }
            return hook.original.apply(this,args);
        };
        queue[hook.key]=hook.patched;
    }
    DROP_GUARD.active=guard;DROP_GUARD.owned=false;
    Object.assign(ATTACK_SYNC,{conn:null,type:null,payload:null,pending:false,restored:false});
    guard.restore=()=>{for(const h of hooks)if(queue[h.key]===h.patched){if(h.own)Object.defineProperty(queue,h.key,h.own);else delete queue[h.key];}};
    cleanups.push(guard.restore);
    return guard.isValid();
}
function ensureDropGuard(registry){
    const conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry);
    if(!conn)return false;
    if(DROP_GUARD.active?.conn===conn)return DROP_GUARD.active.isValid?DROP_GUARD.active.isValid():conn.send===DROP_GUARD.active.patched;
    const sendDescriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(conn,'send');
    if(sendDescriptor&&sendDescriptor.writable===false){
        if(!compatEquipmentTransport)return false;
        return installQueueDropGuard(conn);
    }
    if(GEAR.held||gearCursor()!==0||!['c5','c3','c4','c7','d0','d4'].every(key=>compatAvailable.has(key)))return false;
    const original=conn.send,own=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(conn,'send');
    const codes=[];
    // Discover the three commands with a sink; no equipment or drop is sent.
    // UI is touched once while attack is released, never during actual swaps.
    const sink=function(type){codes.push(type);};
    const wasOpen=GAME.c7('inventory');
    conn.send=sink;
    try{
        client.c5();
        if(!wasOpen)GAME.d3('inventory');
        if(!GAME.c7('inventory'))return false;
        GAME.c4();GAME.c3();
    }finally{
        try{if(!wasOpen)GAME.c_('inventory');}
        finally{if(conn.send===sink){if(own)Object.defineProperty(conn,'send',own);else delete conn.send;}}
    }
    if(codes.length!==3||!codes.every(Number.isInteger)||new Set(codes).size!==3)return false;
    const [code,hatCode,shieldCode]=codes;
    const patched=function(type,...args){
        if(type===code&&(GEAR.tx||DROP_GUARD.owned)){
            DROP_GUARD.blocked++;
            if(GEAR.held)ATTACK_SYNC.pending=true;
            return;
        }
        rememberAttackInput(this,type,args[0]);
        return original.call(this,type,...args);
    };
    conn.send=patched;
    DROP_GUARD.active={conn,patched,hatCode,shieldCode};DROP_GUARD.owned=false;
    Object.assign(ATTACK_SYNC,{conn:null,type:null,payload:null,pending:false,restored:false});
    DROP_GUARD.active.restore=()=>{if(conn.send===patched){if(own)Object.defineProperty(conn,'send',own);else delete conn.send;}};
    cleanups.push(DROP_GUARD.active.restore);
    return true;
}
// Hide only the game's floating inventory item during automatic equipment swaps.
// Replicated cursor state and ordinary manual inventory dragging are unchanged.
const gearCursorStyle=document.createElement('style');
gearCursorStyle.textContent='html[data-t7-auto-equipment] [data-ui-node="CursorItem"],html[data-t7-auto-equipment] .inv-cursor{visibility:hidden!important;pointer-events:none!important}';
(document.head||document.documentElement).appendChild(gearCursorStyle);
cleanups.push(()=>gearCursorStyle.remove());
let gearTransaction=GEAR.tx;
Object.defineProperty(GEAR,'tx',{
    enumerable:true,configurable:true,
    get(){return gearTransaction;},
    set(value){
        const previous=gearTransaction;
        gearTransaction=value;
        if(value)DROP_GUARD.owned=true;
        if(!value&&previous)GEAR.nextSwapAt=performance.now()+AUTO.delay;
        if(!value&&gearCursor()===0)DROP_GUARD.owned=false;
        document.documentElement.toggleAttribute('data-t7-auto-equipment',!!value);
    }
});

function gearHasHat(name){
    const type=gearType(name);
    if(type==null)return false;
    return gearCurrent('hat')===type||gearCursor()===type||
        GAME.bS.slots.some(slot=>slot.type===type&&slot.count>0);
}
// Port of auto_hats.txt. Decisions use the current server's item/entity settings.
const HATS={percent:20,lastActive:0,wasActive:false,first:true,elapsed:0,timerAt:null,entity:null,hud:null};
const HAT_MELEE=['VampireHat','BoneHat','SpikeHat','NightmareHat','CactusHat','BearHat','JasonHat'];
const HAT_COMBAT=[...HAT_MELEE,'PinkShooterHat','SniperHat','ScoreHat','MonsterHunterHat','IllusionHat','GoldenHelmet','MedicineHat','HalloweenHat'];
function illusionValue(value){return value&&typeof value==='object'&&'value'in value?value.value:value;}
function hatsReset(){Object.assign(HATS,{lastActive:performance.now(),wasActive:false,entity:null});ILLUSION_CLOCK.wearing=false;}
function hatsAvailableType(type){
 return Number.isInteger(type)&&type>0&&(gearCurrent('hat')===type||GAME.bS.slots.some(s=>s.type===type&&s.count>0&&Number.isInteger(s.index)&&s.index>=0));
}
function hatsFind(names){return names.find(name=>hatsAvailableType(gearType(name)))??null;}
const ILLUSION_CLOCK={server:null,readyAt:0,wearing:false};
try{const saved=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('kiro-illusion-clock-v1')||'null');if(saved&&typeof saved.server==='string'&&Number.isFinite(saved.readyAt)&&saved.readyAt<=Date.now()+300000){ILLUSION_CLOCK.server=saved.server;ILLUSION_CLOCK.readyAt=saved.readyAt;}}catch{}
function saveIllusionClock(){try{sessionStorage.setItem('kiro-illusion-clock-v1',JSON.stringify({server:ILLUSION_CLOCK.server,readyAt:ILLUSION_CLOCK.readyAt}));}catch{}}
function illusionReady(){return Date.now()>=ILLUSION_CLOCK.readyAt;}
function updateIllusion(registry){
 const url=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry)?.ws?.url;
 // The ticket changes on reconnect; the server endpoint does not.
 let server=null;try{if(url){const parsed=new URL(url);server=parsed.origin+parsed.pathname.split('/ws/')[0];}}catch{}
 if(server&&server!==ILLUSION_CLOCK.server){
  if(ILLUSION_CLOCK.server!==null)ILLUSION_CLOCK.readyAt=0;
  ILLUSION_CLOCK.server=server;ILLUSION_CLOCK.wearing=false;saveIllusionClock();
 }
 const active=GAME.be.scene==='gameplay'&&GAME.c9.health>0&&String(registry?.localId)===String(GAME.d4.localEntityId);
 const type=gearType('IllusionHat'),wearing=active&&Number.isInteger(type)&&type>0&&gearCurrent('hat')===type;
 if(wearing&&!ILLUSION_CLOCK.wearing&&illusionReady()){
  ILLUSION_CLOCK.readyAt=Date.now()+300000;saveIllusionClock();
 }
 ILLUSION_CLOCK.wearing=wearing;
 HATS.entity=registry?.entities?.get(String(registry.localId));hatsHUD();
}

function hatsHUD(){
 const available=gearHasHat('IllusionHat');
 if(!available){if(HATS.hud){HATS.hud.hidden=true;HATS.hud.textContent='';}return;}
 if(!HATS.hud){
  const box=document.createElement('div');box.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:20px;top:80%;z-index:99999;background:#111c;border-radius:6px;padding:8px 12px;font:13px Arial;pointer-events:none';
  document.body.appendChild(box);HATS.hud=box;cleanups.push(()=>box.remove());
 }
 HATS.hud.hidden=!AUTO.hats||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay';
 const seconds=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((ILLUSION_CLOCK.readyAt-Date.now())/1000));
 HATS.hud.style.color=illusionReady()?'#36db78':'#ff6464';
 HATS.hud.textContent=(uiLanguage==='en'?'Illusionist: ':'Иллюзионист: ')+(illusionReady()?(uiLanguage==='en'?'Ready':'Готов'):Math.floor(seconds/60)+':'+String(seconds%60).padStart(2,'0'));
}
function hatsConfig(){return Object.values(clientModule).find(v=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&v.items&&v.entities&&v.build&&v.craft);}
function hatsDefault(hp,max){
 const current=gearName(gearCurrent('hat'));
 if(!HAT_COMBAT.includes(current)&&current!=='SonicHat'&&current!=='RocketHat')return null;
 return hatsFind(hp/max<0.5?['SonicHat','RocketHat']:['RocketHat','SonicHat']);
}
function hatsDecision(hp,weapon,attacking,registry,local,now){
 const max=Number(GAME.c9.maxHealth);if(!(max>0&&hp>0)||!local)return null;
 const percent=hp/max*100;
 if(percent<HATS.percent){
  if(gearCurrent('hat')===gearType('IllusionHat'))return 'IllusionHat';
  if(illusionReady()&&hatsFind(['IllusionHat']))return 'IllusionHat';
  return hatsFind(['GoldenHelmet']);
 }
 const config=hatsConfig(),items=config?.items?.items??config?.items,defs=config?.entities?.entities??config?.entities;
 const item=items?.[weapon.toLowerCase()],action=item?.item_action??item?.itemAction;
 if(!action)return null;
 const tag=String(action.tag??'').toLowerCase(),actionType=String(action.type??action.action_type??'').toLowerCase();
 const distance=Number(action.distance)||0,damage=Number(action.damage)||0;
 if(actionType==='aoehealweapon'&&percent<90){const hat=hatsFind(['MedicineHat']);if(hat)return hat;}
 if(actionType==='aoehealweapon'&&percent>80&&gearCurrent('hat')===gearType('MedicineHat'))return hatsDefault(hp,max);
 if(['ElectricityKatana','BoostPad','VoidSpear'].includes(weapon))return hatsDefault(hp,max);
 const targets=[];let nearPlayer=false,meleePlayer=false,gather=false;
 for(const entity of registry.entities.values()){
  if(String(entity.id)===String(local.id)||!Number.isFinite(entity.rx)||!Number.isFinite(entity.ry))continue;
  const d=Math.hypot(entity.rx-local.rx,entity.ry-local.ry);
  const type=entity.typeName??GAME.at?.[entity.type]??entity.prefab?.type;
  const tags=defs?.[String(type??'').toLowerCase()]?.tags??[];
  if(type==='Player'&&!entity.isFriend?.()){if(d<=12)nearPlayer=true;if(d<=distance+1.3)meleePlayer=true;}
  if(d<=distance*2.5&&((Number(action.gathering?.pickaxeable)>=1&&tags.includes('pickaxeable'))||(Number(action.gathering?.axeable)>=1&&tags.includes('axeable'))))gather=true;
  if(tags.includes('enemies')&&d<=distance*(tag==='melee'?3:1.3))targets.push(entity);
 }
 let wanted=gather?hatsFind(['HalloweenHat']):null;
 if(!nearPlayer&&['melee','range','ranged'].includes(tag)){
  const mob=targets[0],health=Number(illusionValue(mob?.state?.get?.('HealthComponentRepl')?.health)??mob?.get?.('health')?.health);
  if(mob){
   const hit=damage*(1+0.013*49)*(tag==='melee'?1.25:1.4)*2;
   if(Number.isFinite(health)&&health-hit<1)wanted=hatsFind(['ScoreHat'])??wanted;
   else if(tag==='melee')wanted=hatsFind(['MonsterHunterHat'])??wanted;
   else if(damage>2&&attacking)wanted=hatsFind(['PinkShooterHat','SniperHat'])??wanted;
  }
 }
 if(tag==='melee'&&weapon!=='Hand'&&damage>0&&meleePlayer)wanted=hatsFind(HAT_MELEE)??wanted;
 if(['range','ranged'].includes(tag)&&damage>2&&actionType!=='aoehealweapon'&&attacking)wanted=hatsFind(['PinkShooterHat','SniperHat'])??wanted;
 if(wanted){HATS.wasActive=true;HATS.lastActive=now;return wanted;}
 if(HATS.wasActive){HATS.wasActive=false;HATS.lastActive=now;}
 return now-HATS.lastActive>2000?hatsDefault(hp,max):null;
}
function gearHatRule(hp,weapon,attacking,enemy,now){
 return AUTO.hats?hatsDecision(hp,weapon,attacking,window.__t7Registry__,window.__t7LocalEntity__,now):null;
}
function gearObserveHat(){return CFG.itemSwaps&&ITEM_SWAP.session===AUTOMATION.active&&ITEM_SWAP.hatHold!=null&&gearCurrent('hat')===ITEM_SWAP.hatHold;}

function gearCursor(){const cursor=GAME.bW?.cursor;return cursor&&cursor.count>0?cursor.type:0;}
function gearSlot(index){return GAME.bS.slots.find(s=>s.index===index);}
function gearObserveShield(threatened){
 const current=gearCurrent('shield');
 const automatic=GEAR.tx?.kind==='shield'||current===GEAR.autoShieldExpected;
 const changed=GEAR.observedShield!==undefined&&current!==GEAR.observedShield;
 const manual=changed&&!automatic;
 if(manual)GEAR.baseShield=current;
 if(current===GEAR.autoShieldExpected)GEAR.autoShieldExpected=undefined;
 GEAR.observedShield=current;
 return manual;
}
function gearCurrent(kind){return Number(GAME.bW[kind==='hat'?'hatType':'shieldType'])||0;}
function gearSwapEquipment(kind){
    const guard=DROP_GUARD.active;
    if(!guard||(guard.isValid?!guard.isValid():guard.conn.send!==guard.patched))throw Error('Защита обмена недоступна');
    const code=kind==='hat'?guard.hatCode:kind==='shield'?guard.shieldCode:null;
    if(!Number.isInteger(code))throw Error('Команда экипировки не распознана');
    // Same client command, without opening/closing inventory and resetting attack.
    gearCheckpoint(GEAR.tx);
    guard.conn.send(code,{});
}
const gearHomes=new Map();
let gearHomeRegistry=null;
function gearRememberSlots(){
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
 if(gearHomeRegistry!==registry){gearHomes.clear();gearHomeRegistry=registry;}
 if(GEAR.tx)return;
 for(const slot of GAME.bS.slots){
  if(slot.count>0&&Number.isInteger(slot.index)){
   const previous=gearHomes.get(slot.type),known=previous==null?null:gearSlot(previous);
   if(!known||known.type!==slot.type||known.count<=0)gearHomes.set(slot.type,slot.index);
  }
 }
}
function gearReturnSlot(type,fallback){return gearHomes.get(type)??fallback;}
// InventoryWindow renders indices 0..44, including entries omitted by replication.
function gearEmptySlot(exclude=null){
 for(let index=10;index<45;index++)if(index!==exclude&&!(gearSlot(index)?.count>0))return index;
 for(let index=0;index<10;index++)if(index!==exclude&&!(gearSlot(index)?.count>0))return index;
 return null;
}

function gearNearestEmpty(home,vacated=null){
 if(!Number.isInteger(home))return gearEmptySlot()??vacated;
 const indices=Array.from({length:45},(_,i)=>i).sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a-home)-Math.abs(b-home)||a-b);
 return indices.find(i=>i===vacated||!(gearSlot(i)?.count>0))??null;
}
function gearPutBack(tx,now){
 const index=gearNearestEmpty(tx.returnSlot??tx.slot);
 if(index==null){GEAR.status='Нет свободного места — ожидание';return;}
 tx.returnSlot=index;gearHomes.set(tx.old,index);
 tx.returningType=gearCursor();tx.stage=3;
 gearSendSlot(index);
}
function gearStart(kind,type,now){
    if(kind==='hat'&&type!==0&&!hatsAvailableType(type))return;
    gearRememberSlots();
    if(GEAR.halted||GEAR.tx||gearCurrent(kind)===type)return;
    if(now<(GEAR.nextSwapAt||0)&&!(kind==='shield'&&type===gearType('ReflectShield')))return;
    if(!ensureDropGuard(window.__t7Registry__)){GEAR.status='Защита: выйдите из сервера и зайдите обратно после установки скрипта';return;}
    if(gearCursor()!==0){GEAR.status='Курсор занят — ожидание';return;}
    // Removing equipment needs an empty slot in the confirmed inventory capacity.
    if(type===0){
        const oldType=gearCurrent(kind),home=gearHomes.get(oldType);
        const index=gearNearestEmpty(home);
        if(index!=null)gearHomes.set(oldType,index);
        if(index==null){GEAR.status='Снятие: нужен свободный слот инвентаря';return;}
        GEAR.lastSlots[kind]=index;
        GEAR.tx={kind,type:0,slot:index,old:gearCurrent(kind),stage:4,since:now,last:now};
        try{if(kind==='hat')GEAR.autoHatExpected=0;else GEAR.autoShieldExpected=0;gearSwapEquipment(kind);}catch(e){GEAR.tx=null;GEAR.halted=true;GEAR.status=e.message;}return;
    }
    const slot=GAME.bS.slots.find(s=>s.type===type&&s.count>0);
    if(!slot){GEAR.status='Нет предмета: '+gearName(type);return;}
    GEAR.lastSlots[kind]=slot.index;
    const old=gearCurrent(kind),home=gearReturnSlot(old,gearEmptySlot(slot.index)??slot.index);
    const returnSlot=gearNearestEmpty(home,slot.index);
    gearHomes.set(type,slot.index);
    if(old&&!gearHomes.has(old))gearHomes.set(old,returnSlot);
    GEAR.tx={kind,type,slot:slot.index,returnSlot,old,stage:1,since:now,last:now};
    try{gearSendSlot(slot.index);GEAR.status='Взять '+gearName(type);}
    catch(e){GEAR.tx=null;GEAR.halted=true;GEAR.status=e.message;}
}
const SMART_TRAPS={registry:null,samples:new Map(),sent:new Map(),next:0,traps:new Map(),released:new Map(),destroyed:new Map(),conn:null,off:null};
function smartTrapReset(){for(const sample of SMART_TRAPS.samples.values())sample.off?.();SMART_TRAPS.samples.clear();SMART_TRAPS.sent.clear();SMART_TRAPS.next=0;
 SMART_TRAPS.off?.();SMART_TRAPS.off=null;SMART_TRAPS.conn=null;
 for(const trap of SMART_TRAPS.traps.values())trap.off?.();
 SMART_TRAPS.traps.clear();SMART_TRAPS.released.clear();SMART_TRAPS.destroyed.clear();
}
function smartTrapObserve(registry,now){
 const state=SMART_TRAPS,conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry);
 if(state.conn!==conn){
  state.off?.();state.conn=conn;
  state.off=conn?.onAny?.(packet=>{
   if(!autoTrapsEnabled()||state.registry!==registry||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||packet.name!=='RPCObjectDeath')return;
   const id=idOf(packet.payload?.id),trap=state.traps.get(id);
   if(!trap||state.destroyed.has(id))return;
   const time=performance.now();state.destroyed.set(id,time);
   if(trap.captive&&time-trap.captiveSeen<1000){
    state.released.set(trap.captive,time+750);
    for(const key of state.sent.keys())if(key.startsWith(trap.captive+'|'))state.sent.delete(key);
    const epoch=state.session;
    queueMicrotask(()=>{if(state.registry===registry&&state.session===epoch)smartTrapTick();});
   }
  })||null;
 }
 for(const e of registry.entities.values()){
  if(e.prefab?.type!=='Trap')continue;
  const id=String(e.id);let trap=state.traps.get(id);
  if(!trap||trap.entity!==e){
   trap?.off?.();trap={entity:e,captive:null,captiveSeen:0,seen:now,off:null};
   const tracked=trap;
   const update=message=>{
    if(!message||!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(message,'trapped_id'))return;
    const raw=illusionValue(message.trapped_id);
    const captive=raw!=null&&typeof raw!=='object'&&String(raw)!=='0'?String(raw):null;
    tracked.current=captive;
    if(captive){tracked.captive=captive;tracked.captiveSeen=performance.now();}
   };
   update(e.state?.get?.('TrapComponentRepl'));
   trap.off=e.state?.subscribe?.('TrapComponentRepl',update);state.traps.set(id,trap);
  }
  trap.seen=now;if(trap.current)trap.captiveSeen=now;
 }
 for(const [id,trap] of state.traps)if(now-trap.seen>1000){trap.off?.();state.traps.delete(id);}
 for(const [id,time] of state.destroyed)if(now-time>2000)state.destroyed.delete(id);
 for(const [id,until] of state.released)if(now>until)state.released.delete(id);
}
function smartTrapReleasePoint(local,motion,angle){
 const speed=Math.hypot(motion.vx,motion.vy);
 if(speed>0.2){
  const lead=Math.max(0.8,speed*motion.horizon);
  return smartTrapPoint(local,{rx:motion.x,ry:motion.y},motion.vx/speed,motion.vy/speed,8,lead);
 }
 if(!Number.isFinite(angle))return null;
 return smartTrapPoint(local,{rx:motion.x,ry:motion.y},Math.cos(angle),Math.sin(angle),8,0.8);
}
function smartTrapPosition(message){
 const p=illusionValue(message?.position);
 if(!p)return null;
 const x=Number(illusionValue(Array.isArray(p)?p[0]:p.x));
 const y=Number(illusionValue(Array.isArray(p)?p[1]:p.y));
 return Number.isFinite(x)&&Number.isFinite(y)?{x,y}:null;
}
function smartTrapMotion(sample,point,now,source='server'){
 if(!point)return;
 if(sample.source!==source){sample.history=[];sample.vx=sample.vy=0;sample.source=source;}
 const history=sample.history,last=history[history.length-1];
 sample.x=point.x;sample.y=point.y;sample.fresh=now;
 if(last){
  const elapsed=(now-last.time)/1000,distance=Math.hypot(point.x-last.x,point.y-last.y);
  // A long gap or teleport must not become a giant velocity estimate.
  if(elapsed>0.5||distance>Math.max(16,Math.hypot(sample.vx,sample.vy)*Math.max(0,elapsed)*4+2)){
   history.length=0;sample.vx=sample.vy=0;
  }else if(now-last.time>=10){sample.cadence=sample.cadence?sample.cadence*0.7+(now-last.time)*0.3:now-last.time;}
 }
 // Coalesce packets received in the same burst rather than dividing by a near-zero interval.
 if(history.length&&now-history[history.length-1].time<10)history[history.length-1]={...point,time:now};
 else history.push({...point,time:now});
 while(history.length>2&&now-history[0].time>300)history.shift();
 const base=[...history].reverse().find(p=>now-p.time>=60&&now-p.time<=300);
 if(!base)return;
 const dt=(now-base.time)/1000,vx=(point.x-base.x)/dt,vy=(point.y-base.y)/dt;
 const oldSpeed=Math.hypot(sample.vx,sample.vy),speed=Math.hypot(vx,vy);
 const turn=oldSpeed>0.1&&speed>0.1&&(vx*sample.vx+vy*sample.vy)/(oldSpeed*speed)<0.7;
 const change=Math.abs(speed-oldSpeed)>Math.max(0.5,oldSpeed*0.25);
 const alpha=turn||change?0.9:0.6;
 if(speed<0.05){sample.vx=sample.vy=0;}
 else {sample.vx+=(vx-sample.vx)*alpha;sample.vy+=(vy-sample.vy)*alpha;}
}
function smartTrapChangedDirection(sample,motion){
 const speed=Math.hypot(motion.vx,motion.vy);if(speed<=.2)return false;
 const direction={x:motion.vx/speed,y:motion.vy/speed},old=sample.trapDirection;
 const changed=!!old&&old.x*direction.x+old.y*direction.y<.7;
 if(!old||changed)sample.trapDirection=direction;
 return changed;
}
function smartTrapPrediction(sample,now){
 const age=Math.max(0,now-sample.fresh);
 const stale=age>Math.max(250,Math.min(500,(sample.cadence||100)*3));
 const ping=Math.max(0,Math.min(500,Number(GAME.c9.ping)||0));
 // Raw server position is already one network trip behind the next build request.
 const horizon=Math.min(0.65,0.12+ping/1000+Math.min(age,150)/1000+Math.min(sample.cadence||100,200)/2000);
 return {x:sample.x,y:sample.y,vx:stale?0:sample.vx,vy:stale?0:sample.vy,horizon};
}
function smartTrapPoint(local,enemy,vx,vy,radius=8,horizon=0.2){
 if(![local.rx,local.ry,enemy.rx,enemy.ry,vx,vy,horizon].every(Number.isFinite))return null;
 const x=enemy.rx+vx*horizon,y=enemy.ry+vy*horizon,limit=radius-0.3;
 // Do not clamp sideways off the enemy's path, or build at a distant approach too early.
 if(Math.hypot(x-local.rx,y-local.ry)>limit)return null;
 return {x,y};
}
function autoTrapsEnabled(){return featureAvailable('t7autotraps')&&(AUTO.traps||(CFG.mode063&&CFG.mode036Traps));}
function smartTrapTick(){
 if(!autoTrapsEnabled()||disposed||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return;
 const registry=window.__t7Registry__,local=window.__t7LocalEntity__,now=performance.now();
 const session=automationSessionReady(registry,local,now);
 if(!session)return;
 if(SMART_TRAPS.registry!==registry||SMART_TRAPS.session!==session.epoch){smartTrapReset();SMART_TRAPS.registry=registry;SMART_TRAPS.session=session.epoch;}
 smartTrapObserve(registry,now);
 const enemies=new Map();
 for(const e of registry.entities.values()){
  if(e.prefab?.type!=='Player'||String(e.id)===String(local.id)||e.isFriend?.())continue;
  enemies.set(String(e.id),e);
 }
 const candidates=[];
 for(const [id,e] of enemies){
  if(!Number.isFinite(e.rx)||!Number.isFinite(e.ry))continue;
  let sample=SMART_TRAPS.samples.get(id);
  if(!sample||sample.entity!==e){
   sample?.off?.();
   sample={entity:e,x:e.rx,y:e.ry,fresh:now,vx:0,vy:0,hat:null,off:null,history:[],source:null,cadence:0};
   const tracked=sample;
   const updateHat=message=>{
    if(!message||!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(message,'active_hat_type'))return;
    const raw=illusionValue(message.active_hat_type);
    const value=raw&&typeof raw==='object'&&Object.keys(raw).length===0?0:Number(raw);
    if(Number.isFinite(value))tracked.hat=value;
   };
   updateHat(e.state?.get?.('InventoryComponentRepl'));
   const hatOff=e.state?.subscribe?.('InventoryComponentRepl',updateHat);
   const updateMotion=message=>{
    const rotation=illusionValue(message?.rotation);
    if(typeof rotation==='number'&&Number.isFinite(rotation))tracked.angle=rotation;
    if(message===undefined){tracked.history=[];tracked.vx=tracked.vy=0;tracked.source=null;return;}
    const point=smartTrapPosition(message);
    if(point)smartTrapMotion(tracked,point,performance.now());
   };
   // A cached frame has no receive timestamp: seed position, then measure fresh packets.
   tracked.angle=illusionValue(e.state?.get?.('TransformComponentRepl')?.rotation);
   const initial=smartTrapPosition(e.state?.get?.('TransformComponentRepl'));
   if(initial){tracked.x=initial.x;tracked.y=initial.y;tracked.source='server';}
   const motionOff=e.state?.subscribe?.('TransformComponentRepl',updateMotion);
   tracked.off=()=>{hatOff?.();motionOff?.();};
   SMART_TRAPS.samples.set(id,sample);
  }
  if(sample.source!=='server')smartTrapMotion(sample,{x:e.rx,y:e.ry},now,'render');
  const hats=Object.values(clientModule).find(value=>value?.hats?.ShooterHat)?.hats;
  const asset=e.get?.('hat')?.asset;
  if(sample.hat===gearType('ShooterHat')||(asset&&hats&&asset===hats.ShooterHat))continue;
  const motion=smartTrapPrediction(sample,now);
  const moving=Math.hypot(motion.vx,motion.vy)>0.2;
  const turned=smartTrapChangedDirection(sample,motion);
  const resumed=(moving&&sample.moving===false)||turned;
  sample.moving=moving;
  if(resumed){
   sample.resumePending=true;
   for(const key of SMART_TRAPS.sent.keys())if(key.startsWith(id+'|'))SMART_TRAPS.sent.delete(key);
  }
  const released=SMART_TRAPS.released.has(id);
  const held=[...SMART_TRAPS.traps].some(([trapId,trap])=>trap.current===id&&now-trap.seen<250&&!SMART_TRAPS.destroyed.has(trapId));
  if(held)continue;
  const angle=Number.isFinite(sample.angle)?sample.angle:e.root?.rotation?.z;
  const point=released?smartTrapReleasePoint(local,motion,angle):smartTrapPoint(local,{rx:motion.x,ry:motion.y},motion.vx,motion.vy,8,motion.horizon);
  if(point)candidates.push({...point,id,moving,released,direction:moving?{x:motion.vx/Math.hypot(motion.vx,motion.vy),y:motion.vy/Math.hypot(motion.vx,motion.vy)}:released&&Number.isFinite(angle)?{x:Math.cos(angle),y:Math.sin(angle)}:null,resumed:released||!!sample.resumePending,distance:Math.hypot(e.rx-local.rx,e.ry-local.ry)});
 }
 for(const [id,sample] of SMART_TRAPS.samples)if(!enemies.has(id)){sample.off?.();SMART_TRAPS.samples.delete(id);}
 for(const [key,time] of SMART_TRAPS.sent)if(now-time>=1000)SMART_TRAPS.sent.delete(key);
 // Build requests do not use the active item or inventory cursor.
 if(now<SMART_TRAPS.next&&!candidates.some(point=>point.resumed))return;
 const conn=DROP_GUARD.connections.get(registry),type=GAME.at?.Trap;
 if(!conn||conn.connected===false||typeof conn.send!=='function'||!Number.isInteger(type))return;
 for(const point of candidates.sort((a,b)=>Number(b.released)-Number(a.released)||a.distance-b.distance)){
  if(now<SMART_TRAPS.next&&!point.resumed)continue;
  const key=point.id+'|'+Math.round(point.x*2)+':'+Math.round(point.y*2);
  if(SMART_TRAPS.sent.has(key))continue;
  const occupied=[...registry.entities.values()].some(e=>{
   if(SMART_TRAPS.destroyed.has(String(e.id))||e.prefab?.type!=='Trap'||Math.hypot(e.rx-point.x,e.ry-point.y)>=0.8)return false;
   const captive=illusionValue(e.state?.get?.('TrapComponentRepl')?.trapped_id);
   // A stale captive ID must not prevent the next placement once that player moves.
   return !(point.moving&&captive!=null&&String(captive)===point.id);
  });
  if(occupied)continue;
  SMART_TRAPS.next=now+150;SMART_TRAPS.sent.set(key,now);
  SMART_TRAPS.released.delete(point.id);
  const sample=SMART_TRAPS.samples.get(point.id);if(sample){sample.resumePending=false;if(point.direction)sample.trapDirection=point.direction;}
  if(Number.isInteger(compatPacketTypes?.InputBuildRequest))conn.send(compatPacketTypes.InputBuildRequest,{id:0,build_type:type,x:point.x,y:point.y,angle:0,test_pass:false});
  break;
 }
}
cleanups.push(smartTrapReset);
function refillConsumable(type){
 const name=gearName(type);if(name==='BoostPad')return true;
 const config=hatsConfig(),items=config?.items?.items??config?.items;
 const def=items?.[name.toLowerCase()];
 // Consumable thrown stacks only: exclude drinkable potions and reusable boomerangs.
 return def?.item_action?.type==='throwingweapon'&&Number(def.stack)>1;
}
function refillCapacity(type){const config=hatsConfig(),items=config?.items?.items??config?.items;return Number(items?.[gearName(type).toLowerCase()]?.stack)||1;}
function refillTargetReady(index,type){const slot=gearSlot(index);return !(slot?.count>0)||(slot.type===type&&slot.count<=5&&slot.count<refillCapacity(type));}
function boostObserve(registry){
 if(BOOST.registry!==registry){BOOST.registry=registry;BOOST.previous.clear();BOOST.pending.clear();}
 for(let index=0;index<10;index++){
  const slot=gearSlot(index),previous=BOOST.previous.get(index),count=Number(slot?.count)||0;
  const touched=GEAR.tx&&[GEAR.tx.slot,GEAR.tx.returnSlot,GEAR.tx.target].includes(index);
  if(AUTO.boosts&&previous?.count>0&&count<=0&&refillConsumable(previous.type)&&!touched&&gearCursor()!==previous.type)BOOST.pending.set(index,previous.type);
  if(count>0){
   if(AUTO.boosts&&!touched&&gearCursor()!==slot.type&&refillConsumable(slot.type)&&refillTargetReady(index,slot.type))BOOST.pending.set(index,slot.type);
   else if(!touched)BOOST.pending.delete(index);
  }
  BOOST.previous.set(index,{type:slot?.type??0,count});
 }
}
const BOOST={registry:null,previous:new Map(),pending:new Map()};
function boostStart(now){
 if(!AUTO.boosts||GEAR.tx||GEAR.halted||gearCursor()!==0)return;
 for(const [target,type]of BOOST.pending){
  if(!refillTargetReady(target,type)){BOOST.pending.delete(target);continue;}
  if(!refillConsumable(type))continue;
  const source=GAME.bS.slots.find(s=>Number.isInteger(s.index)&&s.index>=10&&s.type===type&&s.count>0);if(!source)continue;
  if(!ensureDropGuard(window.__t7Registry__))return;
  GEAR.tx={kind:'boost',type,slot:source.index,target,stage:1,since:now,last:now};
  try{gearSendSlot(source.index);}catch(error){GEAR.tx=null;GEAR.halted=true;GEAR.status=error.message;}
  return;
 }
}
function gearCheckpoint(tx){
    const session=AUTOMATION.active;
    if(!session||session.closed||GEAR.session!==session.epoch)throw Error('Сессия изменилась');
    tx.cursorRevision=session.cursorRevision;
    tx.equipmentRevision=session[tx.kind==='hat'?'hatRevision':'shieldRevision'];
    tx.last=performance.now();
}
function gearSendSlot(index){
    const tx=GEAR.tx;if(!tx)throw Error('Нет активного обмена');
    gearCheckpoint(tx);
    AUTOMATION.active.conn.send(automationPackets.InputSwapItemAndCursor,{index,half:false});
}
function gearCursorConfirmed(tx){return AUTOMATION.active.cursorRevision>tx.cursorRevision;}
function gearEquipmentConfirmed(tx){return AUTOMATION.active[tx.kind==='hat'?'hatRevision':'shieldRevision']>tx.equipmentRevision;}
function gearFinish(status){
 const tx=GEAR.tx,swap=tx?.itemSwapEquip;
 if(swap&&gearCurrent(swap.kind)===tx.type&&gearCursor()===0){
  if(swap.returning)delete ITEM_SWAP.equipment[swap.kind];else ITEM_SWAP.equipment[swap.kind]={type:swap.type,previous:swap.previous};
  if(swap.kind==='hat')ITEM_SWAP.hatHold=swap.returning?null:tx.type;
  else{GEAR.baseShield=tx.type;GEAR.observedShield=tx.type;GEAR.autoShieldExpected=undefined;}
 }

    GEAR.tx=null;DROP_GUARD.owned=false;GEAR.halted=false;GEAR.status=status;
    if(tx&&GEAR.held&&gearCursor()===0)ATTACK_SYNC.pending=true;
}
function gearRecover(tx,now){
    const cursor=gearCursor();
    if(!cursor){gearFinish('Обмен завершён');return;}
    // Never move a manually substituted item, or repeat an unconfirmed swap.
    if(cursor!==tx.type&&cursor!==tx.old){
        gearFinish('Курсор изменён вручную');GEAR.halted=true;return;
    }
    if(tx.recoverySent){
        GEAR.tx=null;DROP_GUARD.owned=true;GEAR.halted=true;
        GEAR.status='Сервер не подтвердил возврат — предмет доступен на курсоре';return;
    }
    const index=gearNearestEmpty(tx.slot);
    if(index==null){
        GEAR.tx=null;DROP_GUARD.owned=true;GEAR.halted=true;
        GEAR.status='Освободи слот и положи предмет с курсора';return;
    }
    tx.recoverySent=true;tx.returningType=cursor;tx.returnSlot=index;tx.stage=3;
    gearSendSlot(index);
    GEAR.status='Возвращаю предмет в инвентарь';
}
function gearAdvance(now){
    const tx=GEAR.tx;if(!tx)return;
    const timeout=Math.max(2000,Math.min(10000,(Number(GAME.c9.ping)||0)*6+1000));
    try{
        if(now-tx.last>timeout){gearRecover(tx,now);return;}
        if(!gearCursorConfirmed(tx))return;

        if(tx.kind==='boost'&&tx.stage===5){
            const current=gearSlot(tx.target);
            if(current?.type!==tx.type||!(current.count>tx.targetCount))return;
            BOOST.pending.delete(tx.target);
            if(gearCursor()===0){gearFinish('Стак пополнен');return;}
            if(gearCursor()!==tx.type)return;
            const back=gearNearestEmpty(tx.slot);if(back==null)return;
            tx.returnSlot=back;tx.returningType=tx.type;tx.stage=3;gearSendSlot(back);return;
        }
        if(tx.stage===3){
            if(gearCursor()===0&&gearSlot(tx.returnSlot)?.type===tx.returningType&&gearSlot(tx.returnSlot)?.count>0){
                if(tx.kind==='boost')BOOST.pending.delete(tx.target);
                gearFinish('Предмет возвращён');
            }
            return;
        }
        if(tx.kind==='boost'){
            if(gearCursor()!==tx.type)return;
            let destination=tx.target;
            if(!AUTO.boosts||!refillTargetReady(destination,tx.type))destination=gearNearestEmpty(tx.slot);
            if(destination==null)return;
            tx.returnSlot=destination;tx.returningType=tx.type;
            tx.targetCount=Number(gearSlot(destination)?.count)||0;
            tx.stage=destination===tx.target?5:3;
            gearSendSlot(destination);return;
        }
        if(tx.stage===4){
            if(!gearEquipmentConfirmed(tx)||gearCurrent(tx.kind)!==0||gearCursor()!==tx.old)return;
            gearPutBack(tx,now);
        }else if(tx.stage===1){
            if(gearCursor()!==tx.type)return;
            if(tx.kind==='hat')GEAR.autoHatExpected=tx.type;else GEAR.autoShieldExpected=tx.type;
            tx.stage=2;gearSwapEquipment(tx.kind);
        }else if(tx.stage===2){
            if(!gearEquipmentConfirmed(tx)||gearCurrent(tx.kind)!==tx.type)return;
            // Cursor and equipment are separate packets; wait for both expected values.
            if(gearCursor()!==tx.old)return;
            const elapsed=now-tx.since;
            if(elapsed>0&&elapsed<10000)GEAR.equipLatencyMs=Math.max(elapsed,(GEAR.equipLatencyMs||0)*0.8);
            if(!tx.old){gearFinish('Надето: '+gearName(tx.type));return;}
            gearPutBack(tx,now);
        }
    }catch(error){
        // Keep ownership only until a bounded recovery or manual handover.
        tx.last=now-timeout-1;GEAR.status=error.message;
    }
}

function gearEnemyNear(registry,local){
    for(const e of registry.entities.values()){
        if(String(e.id)===String(local.id)||e.prefab?.type!=='Player'||typeof e.isFriend!=='function'||e.isFriend())continue;
        if(Math.hypot(e.rx-local.rx,e.ry-local.ry)<=AUTO.enemyRadius)return true;
    }return false;
}
function gearSantaProjectile(projectile,registry){
    const seen=new Set();let current=projectile;
    for(let depth=0;current&&depth<12;depth++){
        const type=current.prefab?.type??current.type;
        if(type==='Santa'||(typeof GAME.at?.Santa==='number'&&type===GAME.at.Santa))return true;
        const id=idOf(current.parentId)||parentFromEntity(current).id;
        if(!id||seen.has(id))return false;
        seen.add(id);current=registry.entities.get(id);
    }
    return false;
}
function gearProjectileOwner(projectile,registry){
    const seen=new Set();let current=projectile;
    for(let depth=0;depth<12;depth++){
        const id=idOf(current.parentId)||parentFromEntity(current).id;
        if(!id||seen.has(id))return null;seen.add(id);
        const owner=registry.entities.get(id);if(!owner)return null;
        if(owner.prefab?.type==='Player')return owner;
        // Only projectile-to-projectile chains may lead to a player shot.
        // Buildings, towers, pets and unknown intermediates are not shooters.
        if(owner.prefab?.category!=='projectile')return null;
        current=owner;
    }
    return null;
}
const reflectProjectileTypes=new Set([
    'Fireball',
    "ThrowingKnife","DarkThrowingKnife","CopperThrowingKnife","BronzeThrowingKnife","DaemonKnife","Kunai","Lollipop",
    'Boomerang','DarkBoomerang',
    'Arrow','TimeriteArrow','PhantomArrow',
    'PolarBombPotion',
    'StickyPotion','ImprovedStickyPotion','ChristmasPotion',
    'GrenadeToxic','GrenadeFire','GrenadeSmoke','GrenadeSticky','GrenadeIce'
]);
function gearReflectProjectile(entity){
    return entity.prefab?.category==='projectile'&&reflectProjectileTypes.has(entity.prefab.type);
}
function gearReflectLeadMs(){
 const value=Number(GAME.c9?.ping),ping=Number.isFinite(value)?Math.max(0,value):150;
 const measured=Number(GEAR.equipLatencyMs)||0;
 // Two acknowledged exchanges equip the shield; allow one full transaction already in progress.
 const equip=Math.max(300,ping*2,measured*1.2);
 return Math.max(1000,equip+ping+350+(GEAR.tx?Math.max(equip,ping*3):0));
}
function gearIncoming(registry,local,now){
    let danger=false;const live=new Set();GEAR.closeThreatThisFrame=false;
    const boomNames=new Set(['Boomerang','DarkBoomerang']);
    const closeBoom=new Map();
    const preemptWeapons=new Set(['Boomerang','DarkBoomerang','Bow','ChitinBow','PhantomBow','PolarBow','PremiumBow','GoldenBow']);
    for(const player of registry.entities.values()){
        gearAttachAttackRpc(player);
        if(player.prefab?.type!=='Player'||String(player.id)===String(local.id)||player.isFriend?.())continue;
        const weapon=gearName(player.get?.('equipment')?.activeItemType);
        const distance=Math.hypot(player.rx-local.rx,player.ry-local.ry);
        // At close range the projectile can arrive before an equipment swap completes.
        // Proximity alone is not evidence of a shot.
        if(boomNames.has(weapon)&&distance<=AUTO.closeBoomRadius)
            closeBoom.set(String(player.id),player);
    }
    for(const id of GEAR.closeBoomPlayers)if(!closeBoom.has(id))GEAR.closeBoomPlayers.delete(id);
    // The RPC arrives at attack start, so it protects the short pre-projectile window.
    // Evaluate windups after projectiles so a confirmed incoming shot consumes its distant windup.
    for(const e of registry.entities.values()){
        if(!gearReflectProjectile(e))continue;
        if(gearSantaProjectile(e,registry)){GEAR.shots.delete(String(e.id));continue;}
        const id=String(e.id);live.add(id);const previous=GEAR.shots.get(id);
        if(!Number.isFinite(e.rx)||!Number.isFinite(e.ry))continue;
        const moved=!previous||e.rx!==previous.x||e.ry!==previous.y;
        // Retain a real movement sample across frames with no network position update.
        if(moved)GEAR.shots.set(id,{x:e.rx,y:e.ry,time:now,hit:false,closeOrigin:previous?.closeOrigin});
        if(String(e.prefab.type||'').toLowerCase().includes('snow'))continue;
        const owner=gearProjectileOwner(e,registry);
        if(!owner||String(owner.id)===String(local.id)||owner.isFriend?.())continue;
        const ownerWeapon=gearName(owner.get?.('equipment')?.activeItemType);
        if(reflectBlockedWeapons.has(ownerWeapon)){GEAR.shots.delete(id);continue;}
        if((GEAR.ignoredRpcOwners.get(String(owner.id))||0)>now){GEAR.shots.delete(id);continue;}
        const ownerId=String(owner.id);
        if(closeBoom.has(ownerId)&&/boomerang/i.test(String(e.prefab.type)))GEAR.closeBoomPlayers.add(ownerId);
        const dx=local.rx-e.rx,dy=local.ry-e.ry,distance=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
        if(!Number.isFinite(distance)||distance>10){const sample=GEAR.shots.get(id);if(sample)sample.hit=false;continue;}
        // Scan every received allowed projectile; arrival time determines urgency.
        let hit=false;
        // Require observed incoming movement, not proximity alone.
        if(!previous){hit=false;}
        else if(!moved){hit=previous.hit&&now-previous.time<=300;}
        else if(now>previous.time && now-previous.time<=300){
            const vx=(e.rx-previous.x)/(now-previous.time),vy=(e.ry-previous.y)/(now-previous.time);
            const speed2=vx*vx+vy*vy;
            if(Number.isFinite(speed2)&&speed2>=1e-8){
                const eta=(dx*vx+dy*vy)/speed2;
                hit=eta>=0&&eta<=gearReflectLeadMs()&&Math.hypot(dx-vx*eta,dy-vy*eta)<=2.2;
                if(distance<1.4&&dx*vx+dy*vy>=0)hit=true;
            }
        }
        if(moved)GEAR.shots.get(id).hit=hit;
        if(hit){
            const sample=GEAR.shots.get(id);
            if(sample.closeOrigin===undefined)sample.closeOrigin=Math.hypot(owner.rx-local.rx,owner.ry-local.ry)<=4;
            if(sample.closeOrigin)GEAR.closeThreatThisFrame=true;
            GEAR.rpcThreats.delete(ownerId);
            danger=true;GEAR.lastThreatId=id;
        }
    }
    for(const id of GEAR.shots.keys())if(!live.has(id))GEAR.shots.delete(id);
    if(gearEarlyRpcThreat(registry,local,now))danger=true;
    if(danger)GEAR.lastThreat=now;
    // Return the original shield 100 ms after the last threat, at every distance.
    return danger || (GEAR.wasThreatened && now-GEAR.lastThreat<100);
}
function gearToggle(kind,value){
    AUTO[kind]=value;GEAR.halted=false;
    if(kind==='shield'&&!value){GEAR.rpcThreats.clear();GEAR.ignoredRpcOwners.clear();GEAR.lastThreat=0;GEAR.closeBoomPlayers.clear();GEAR.shots.clear();GEAR.wasThreatened=false;GEAR.shieldHoldUntil=0;}
    if(kind==='hats'){HATS.lastActive=performance.now();HATS.wasActive=false;if(!value)HATS.timerAt=null;hatsHUD();}
    if(kind==='shield'){GEAR.baseShield=gearCurrent('shield');GEAR.observedShield=GEAR.baseShield;GEAR.autoShieldExpected=undefined;}
}
function autoGearTick(){
    const now=performance.now(),registry=window.__t7Registry__,local=window.__t7LocalEntity__;
    const session=automationSessionReady(registry,local,now);
    if(!session)return;
    gearRememberSlots();
    if(GEAR.registry!==registry||GEAR.session!==session.epoch){
        GEAR.session=session.epoch;
        GEAR.registry=registry;GEAR.chasedUntil=0;GEAR.combat=null;GEAR.equipLatencyMs=0;GEAR.bobUntil=0;GEAR.bobWeapon=null;GEAR.tx=null;GEAR.halted=false;
        GEAR.baseHat=gearCurrent('hat');GEAR.observedHat=GEAR.baseHat;GEAR.manualHatRule=null;GEAR.baseShield=gearCurrent('shield');GEAR.observedShield=GEAR.baseShield;GEAR.autoShieldExpected=undefined;
        gearDetachAttackRpcs();GEAR.shots.clear();GEAR.closeBoomPlayers.clear();GEAR.lastThreat=0;GEAR.wasThreatened=false;GEAR.shieldHoldUntil=0;
    }
    boostObserve(registry);
    if((AUTO.hats||AUTO.shield||AUTO.boosts||GEAR.tx)&&!ensureDropGuard(registry)){GEAR.status='Защита: выйдите из сервера и зайдите обратно после установки скрипта';return;}
    if(!GEAR.tx&&gearCursor()===0){DROP_GUARD.owned=false;GEAR.halted=false;}
    // Continue tracking trajectories throughout all equipment transactions.
    const threatened=AUTO.shield?gearIncoming(registry,local,now):false;
    const manualShield=gearObserveShield(threatened);
    GEAR.wasThreatened=threatened;
    const active=GAME.bS.slots.find(s=>s.index===GAME.bS.activeIndex);
    const name=gearActiveWeapon(),hp=Number(GAME.c9.health);
    updateIllusion(registry);
    GEAR.combat={close:0};
    const rule=gearHatRule(hp,name,GEAR.held||(CFG.autoAttack&&AUTO_ATTACK.intent),GEAR.combat.close>0,now);
    // Observe before advancing: confirmed automatic swaps must not become manual choices.
    const manualHat=gearObserveHat(rule || 'idle');
    gearAdvance(now);
    resumeConsumedAttack();
    if(GEAR.tx||GEAR.halted)return;
    if(rule)GEAR.lastHatRuleAt=now;
    const returning=!rule&&now-(GEAR.lastHatRuleAt||0)<180;
    const wanted=AUTO.hats&&!manualHat?rule:null;
    const hatType=typeof wanted==='number'?wanted:wanted?gearType(wanted):null;
    const critical=AUTO.hats&&GAME.c9.maxHealth>0&&hp/GAME.c9.maxHealth*100<HATS.percent;
    if(critical&&hatType!=null&&gearCurrent('hat')!==hatType){gearStart('hat',hatType,now);if(GEAR.tx)return;}
    if(AUTO.shield&&!manualShield){
        const type=threatened?gearType('ReflectShield'):GEAR.baseShield;
        if(type!=null&&gearCurrent('shield')!==type){gearStart('shield',type,now);if(GEAR.tx)return;}
    }
    if(!critical&&AUTO.hats&&hatType!=null&&gearCurrent('hat')!==hatType)gearStart('hat',hatType,now);
    if(AUTO.hats&&!GEAR.tx){
        const current=gearCurrent('hat');
        if(manualHat)GEAR.status='Шапка: ручной выбор';
        else if(hatType===current)GEAR.status=rule==='PinkShooterHat'
            ?'Боб: '+(GEAR.held?'кнопка атаки удерживается':Math.max(0,Math.ceil(GEAR.bobUntil-now))+' мс до возврата')
            :rule?'Шапка: условие '+rule+' активно':'Шапка возвращена';
    }
    if(!GEAR.tx)boostStart(now);
    if(!AUTO.hats&&!AUTO.shield&&!AUTO.boosts)GEAR.status='Выключено';
}
// Respond to received projectile updates and confirmed equipment changes immediately.
let gearUrgentQueued=false;
function queueUrgentGearTick(){
 if(disposed||(!AUTO.shield&&!AUTO.hats&&!AUTO.boosts)||gearUrgentQueued)return;
 gearUrgentQueued=true;
 queueMicrotask(()=>{
  gearUrgentQueued=false;
  if(disposed||(!AUTO.shield&&!AUTO.hats&&!AUTO.boosts)||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')return;
  try{syncLocalPlayer();autoGearTick();}catch(error){GEAR.status=error.message;GEAR.halted=true;}
 });
}
for(const method of ['onAdd','onUpdate']){
 const original=GAME.eC?.prototype?.[method];if(typeof original!=='function')continue;
 const patched=function(...args){
  const result=original.apply(this,args);
  if(this===window.__t7Registry__)queueUrgentGearTick();
  return result;
 };
 GAME.eC.prototype[method]=patched;
 cleanups.push(()=>{if(GAME.eC.prototype[method]===patched)GAME.eC.prototype[method]=original;});
}
if(typeof GAME.w==='function')cleanups.push(GAME.w(()=>[GAME.bW.cursor?.type,GAME.bW.cursor?.count,GAME.bW.hatType,GAME.bW.shieldType,
 ...GAME.bS.slots.flatMap(slot=>[slot.index,slot.type,slot.count])],queueUrgentGearTick,{flush:'sync'}));

// Repeat native primary-button edges; the game computes aim, movement and packets.
const FAST_BOOSTS={held:false,down:true,dispatching:false,next:0,x:0,y:0,pointerId:1,canvas:null,session:null,epoch:null};
function fastBoostsStop(){if(FAST_BOOSTS.input){FAST_BOOSTS.input.primary=false;FAST_BOOSTS.input=null;}FAST_BOOSTS.held=false;FAST_BOOSTS.next=0;FAST_BOOSTS.session=null;FAST_BOOSTS.canvas=null;}
function fastBoostsTick(){
 const state=FAST_BOOSTS;if(!state.held)return;
 const now=performance.now(),registry=window.__t7Registry__,session=automationSessionReady(registry,window.__t7LocalEntity__,now);
 const focused=document.activeElement;
 if(!AUTO.fastBoosts||document.hidden||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||GAME.be.paused||!session||session!==state.session||session.epoch!==state.epoch||
    (!GEAR.tx&&(gearActiveWeapon()!=='BoostPad'||gearCursor()!==0||GAME.c7('inventory')))||trapBuildState()?.buildType>0||
    focused?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(focused?.tagName||'')||!state.canvas?.isConnected||document.elementFromPoint(state.x,state.y)!==state.canvas){fastBoostsStop();return;}
 if(now<state.next)return;
 const input=followInputGetter?.();if(!input||input.touch){fastBoostsStop();return;}
 // The native setter queues primary edges without invoking cursor-drop DOM handlers.
 state.next=now+50;state.down=!state.down;input.primary=state.down;state.input=input;
}
listen(window,'pointerdown',e=>{
 if(FAST_BOOSTS.dispatching)return;
 if(e.button!==0||e.pointerType!=='mouse'||e.target?.id!=='three-canvas'||!AUTO.fastBoosts||gearActiveWeapon()!=='BoostPad')return;
 const session=automationSessionReady(window.__t7Registry__,window.__t7LocalEntity__);if(!session)return;
 Object.assign(FAST_BOOSTS,{held:true,down:true,next:performance.now()+50,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,pointerId:e.pointerId,canvas:e.target,session,epoch:session.epoch});
},true);
listen(window,'pointermove',e=>{if(FAST_BOOSTS.dispatching||!FAST_BOOSTS.held)return;FAST_BOOSTS.x=e.clientX;FAST_BOOSTS.y=e.clientY;if(!(e.buttons&1))fastBoostsStop();},true);
for(const event of ['pointerup','mouseup','pointercancel','blur'])listen(window,event,()=>{if(!FAST_BOOSTS.dispatching)fastBoostsStop();},true);
listen(document,'visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)fastBoostsStop();});
cleanups.push(fastBoostsStop);

// Capture input before game handlers can stop propagation.
function gearReleaseAttack(){if(FAST_BOOSTS.dispatching)return;GEAR.held=false;releaseRestoredAttack();}
listen(window,'pointerdown',e=>{
    if(FAST_BOOSTS.dispatching||e.button!==0||e.target?.id!=='three-canvas')return;
    GEAR.held=true;
    // Latch even a click that begins and ends between animation frames.
    const active=GAME.bS.slots.find(slot=>slot.index===GAME.bS.activeIndex);
    const weapon=gearActiveWeapon();
    if(AUTO.hats&&gearRanged(weapon)){
        GEAR.bobWeapon=weapon;
        GEAR.bobUntil=performance.now()+AUTO.bobReleaseMs;
    }
    // Observe only. The game receives this event before the next equipment tick.
},true);
listen(window,'pointerup',e=>{if(e.button===0)gearReleaseAttack();},true);
listen(window,'mouseup',e=>{if(e.button===0)gearReleaseAttack();},true);
listen(window,'pointermove',e=>{if((e.buttons&1)===0)gearReleaseAttack();},true);
listen(window,'pointercancel',gearReleaseAttack,true);
listen(window,'blur',gearReleaseAttack,true);
listen(document,'visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)gearReleaseAttack();},true);

// Export only the current client's map source and replicated configuration.
function privateExportName(name){
 const parts=String(name).replace(/\\/g,'/').split('/');
 if(parts.some(p=>p==='..')||String(name).startsWith('/')||/^[a-z]+:/i.test(name))throw Error('Unsafe file path');
 return parts.filter(p=>p&&p!=='.').join('/');
}
function privateExportZip(files){
 const enc=new TextEncoder(),local=[],central=[];let offset=0;
 const crc=bytes=>{let c=0xffffffff;for(const b of bytes){c^=b;for(let i=0;i<8;i++)c=(c>>>1)^((c&1)?0xedb88320:0);}return(c^0xffffffff)>>>0;};
 for(const [path,data] of files){
  const name=enc.encode(privateExportName(path)),bytes=typeof data==='string'?enc.encode(data):data,sum=crc(bytes);
  const h=new Uint8Array(30+name.length),v=new DataView(h.buffer);
  v.setUint32(0,0x04034b50,true);v.setUint16(4,20,true);v.setUint16(6,0x800,true);v.setUint16(12,33,true);v.setUint32(14,sum,true);v.setUint32(18,bytes.length,true);v.setUint32(22,bytes.length,true);v.setUint16(26,name.length,true);h.set(name,30);
  const c=new Uint8Array(46+name.length),w=new DataView(c.buffer);w.setUint32(0,0x02014b50,true);w.setUint16(4,20,true);w.setUint16(6,20,true);w.setUint16(8,0x800,true);w.setUint16(14,33,true);w.setUint32(16,sum,true);w.setUint32(20,bytes.length,true);w.setUint32(24,bytes.length,true);w.setUint16(28,name.length,true);w.setUint32(42,offset,true);c.set(name,46);
  local.push(h,bytes);central.push(c);offset+=h.length+bytes.length;
 }
 const end=new Uint8Array(22),v=new DataView(end.buffer);v.setUint32(0,0x06054b50,true);v.setUint16(8,files.size,true);v.setUint16(10,files.size,true);v.setUint32(12,central.reduce((n,c)=>n+c.length,0),true);v.setUint32(16,offset,true);
 return new Blob([...local,...central,end],{type:'application/zip'});
}
async function collectPrivateFiles(registry,config){
 const files=new Map(),missing=[],source=registry.services?.tilemap?.source;
 let total=0;
 const add=(name,data)=>{name=privateExportName(name);const size=typeof data==='string'?new TextEncoder().encode(data).length:data.byteLength;if(total+size>128*1024*1024)throw Error('Export exceeds 128 MB');total+=size;files.set(name,data);};
 const read=async url=>{const response=await fetch(url,{signal:AbortSignal.any([lifecycle.signal,AbortSignal.timeout(20000)])});if(!response.ok)throw Error('HTTP '+response.status);const blob=await response.blob();if(blob.size>64*1024*1024)throw Error('File exceeds 64 MB');return new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());};
 for(const key of ['build','craft','items','unlocked','shop','skin','localkit','weapon_skin','entities']){
  if(config?.loaded?.[key])add('settings/'+key+'.json',JSON.stringify(config[key],null,2));
 }
 if(source?.index instanceof Map&&source.files){
  for(const name of source.index.values()){
   try{
    if(source.files[name])add('map/'+name,new Uint8Array(source.files[name]));
    else if(source.texts?.has(name))add('map/'+name,source.texts.get(name));
    else if(source.urls?.has(name))add('map/'+name,await read(source.urls.get(name)));
    else missing.push(name);
   }catch(error){missing.push(name+': '+error.message);}
  }
 }else if(source?.base&&typeof source.text==='function'){
  const queue=['map.tmx'],seen=new Set();
  while(queue.length&&seen.size<512){
   const name=queue.shift();if(seen.has(name))continue;seen.add(name);
   try{
    privateExportName(name);
    if(/\.(tmx|tsx)$/i.test(name)){
     const xml=await source.text(name);add('map/'+name,xml);
     const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(xml,'application/xml');if(doc.querySelector('parsererror'))throw Error('Invalid map XML');
     for(const node of doc.querySelectorAll('tileset[source],image[source]')){
      const ref=node.getAttribute('source');if(!ref||/^[a-z]+:|^\/|^\\/i.test(ref))continue;
      const root=new URL('https://export.invalid/'),resolved=new URL(ref,new URL(name,root));
      if(resolved.origin===root.origin)queue.push(decodeURIComponent(resolved.pathname.slice(1)));
     }
    }else add('map/'+name,await read(source.imageUrl(name)));
   }catch(error){missing.push(name+': '+error.message);}
  }
  if(queue.length)missing.push('File limit reached');
 }else missing.push('Map source is not available in this session');
 if(window.__t7Registry__!==registry||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay')throw Error(uiText('Сервер изменился. Повтори выгрузку.'));
 const count=files.size;
 if(!count)throw Error(uiText('Нет загруженных файлов. Войди на сервер и повтори.'));
 add('export-info.json',JSON.stringify({createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),scope:'Client-received map assets and configuration only; not a server backup',files:[...files.keys()],missing},null,2));
 return{files,count,missing};
}
let privateExportDialog=null,privateExportBusy=false;
function openPrivateExport(){
 if(!privateExportDialog){
  const d=document.createElement('dialog');privateExportDialog=d;d.id='t7privateexportdialog';
  d.style.cssText='margin:auto;width:min(410px,90vw);background:#191d22;color:#e4e6e9;border:1px solid #48554e;border-radius:12px;padding:18px;font:13px Arial';
  const title=document.createElement('strong');title.textContent=uiText('Выгрузка файлов приватки');
  const status=document.createElement('p');status.textContent=uiText('Карта и настройки текущего сервера');status.setAttribute('role','status');
  const save=document.createElement('button'),close=document.createElement('button');save.textContent=uiText('Скачать ZIP');close.textContent=uiText('Закрыть');
  for(const b of [save,close])b.style.cssText='padding:9px 14px;margin-right:8px;border:1px solid #47564e;border-radius:7px;background:#303a36;color:#d6e8df;cursor:pointer';
  close.onclick=()=>d.close();save.onclick=async()=>{
   if(privateExportBusy)return;
   const registry=window.__t7Registry__;
   if(!registry||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'){status.textContent=uiText('Сначала войди на сервер');return;}
   privateExportBusy=true;save.disabled=true;status.textContent=uiText('Собираю файлы…');
   try{
    const config=Object.values(clientModule).find(v=>v&&typeof v==='object'&&v.loaded&&v.items&&v.build&&v.craft&&v.weapon_skin);
    const result=await collectPrivateFiles(registry,config),url=URL.createObjectURL(privateExportZip(result.files));
    const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='KiroHack-server-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.zip';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),60000);
    status.textContent=uiText('Файлов: ')+result.count+(result.missing.length?uiText('. Не удалось выгрузить: ')+result.missing.length:'');
   }catch(error){status.textContent=error.message;}finally{privateExportBusy=false;save.disabled=false;}
  };
  d.append(title,status,save,close);document.body.appendChild(d);cleanups.push(()=>d.remove());
 }
 if(!privateExportDialog.open)privateExportDialog.showModal();
}
