${card('config','Объекты и подключение','',`<div class="t7stack"><span id="t7targets">Ожидание объектов</span><span id="t7cam">Ожидание клиента</span></div>`)}
${card('config','Управление меню','','Delete — скрыть или показать меню. Перетаскивайте меню за полосу «T7 / CONTROL». Поиск работает по всем разделам.')}
</div><div class="t7empty" hidden>Ничего не найдено</div><footer class="t7footer"><input id="t7search" type="text" placeholder="Поиск функций…" aria-label="Поиск функций"><button type="button" class="t7hint" id="t7menubind">Delete</button></footer></div>`;
    document.body.appendChild(mn);
    let selected='visual';
    const filter=()=>{
        const query=mn.querySelector('#t7search').value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ru');let count=0;
        for(const card of mn.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]')){
            const show=query?card.textContent.toLocaleLowerCase('ru').includes(query):card.dataset.tab===selected;
            card.hidden=!show;if(show)count++;
        }
        mn.querySelector('.t7empty').hidden=count>0;
        mn.querySelector('#t7sectionname').textContent=query?'ПОИСК':{pvp:'БОЙ',visual:'ВИЗУАЛ',helpers:'ПОМОЩНИКИ',config:'СЕССИЯ',profiles:'КОНФИГИ'}[selected];
    };
    for(const tab of mn.querySelectorAll('[data-page]'))tab.onclick=()=>{
        selected=tab.dataset.page;mn.querySelector('#t7search').value='';
        for(const other of mn.querySelectorAll('[data-page]'))other.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(other===tab));filter();
    };
    for(const button of mn.querySelectorAll('.t7settings'))button.onclick=()=>{
        const fields=button.closest('.t7card').querySelector(':scope > .t7fields');fields.hidden=!fields.hidden;
        button.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!fields.hidden));
    };
    mn.querySelector('#t7search').oninput=filter;
    mn.querySelector('.t7close').onclick=()=>mn.hidden=true;
    const handle=mn.querySelector('.t7drag');
    let drag=null;
    const place=(x,y)=>{
        const rect=mn.getBoundingClientRect();
        mn.style.left=Math.max(0,Math.min(x,Math.max(0,innerWidth-rect.width)))+'px';
        mn.style.top=Math.max(0,Math.min(y,Math.max(0,innerHeight-rect.height)))+'px';
        mn.style.right='auto';
    };
    listen(handle,'pointerdown',e=>{
        if(e.button!==0 || drag)return;
        const rect=mn.getBoundingClientRect();
        drag={id:e.pointerId,x:e.clientX-rect.left,y:e.clientY-rect.top};
        handle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    });
    listen(handle,'pointermove',e=>{
        if(drag?.id!==e.pointerId)return;
        place(e.clientX-drag.x,e.clientY-drag.y);e.preventDefault();
    });
    const finish=e=>{if(drag?.id!==e.pointerId)return;drag=null;if(handle.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId))handle.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);};
    listen(handle,'pointerup',finish);
    listen(handle,'pointercancel',finish);
    listen(handle,'lostpointercapture',()=>drag=null);
    listen(window,'resize',()=>{const r=mn.getBoundingClientRect();if(!mn.hidden)place(r.left,r.top);});
    mn.placeAt=place;filter();return mn;
}

// Dependencies use verified canonical exports and individually checked packet schemas.
const featureRules={

function featureAvailable(id){
 if(id==='t7autoleave'&&(typeof nativeClient.bO!=='function'||typeof nativeClient.b?.logoutEverywhere!=='function'))return false;
 if(id==='t7packetlimit'&&!nativeBuildKnown)return false;
 if(id==='t7clearpacket'&&!nativeBuildKnown)return false;
 if(id==='t7reconnect'&&(!['bO','bQ','bh','bR'].every(k=>typeof nativeClient[k]==='function')||!nativeClient.b?.createGameAdmission))return false;
 if(id==='t7chattranslate'&&(!window.Translator||!window.LanguageDetector))return false;
 const rule=featureRules[id];if(!rule)return true;
 if(id==='t7visualbadge'&&!badgeNative)return false;
 if(id==='t7visualskin'&&typeof nativeClient.aa!=='function')return false;
 if(['t7fastbuild','t7mode063'].includes(id)&&!fastBuildNative)return false;
 if(['t7follow','t7autoattack','t7aimbot'].includes(id)&&!followInputGetter)return false;
 if(id==='t7freefps'&&(!fpsCallbackSource||!fpsSettings||!fpsBridgeSupported||fpsBridgeFailed))return false;
 if(id==='t7zoom'&&!Camera)return false;
 // Equipment also depends on the verified outbound queue framing.
 if(['t7autohats','t7autoshield','t7autoboosts','t7fastboosts','t7blockrotation','t7randomskin'].includes(id)&&!compatEquipmentTransport)return false;
 if((rule.exports||[]).some(key=>!compatAvailable.has(key)))return false;
 if((rule.exports||[]).includes('eC')&&!registryCaptureAvailable)return false;
 return (rule.packets||[]).every(name=>Number.isInteger(compatPacketTypes?.[name]));
}
function enforceFeatureAvailability(){
 if(!compatAvailable.has('dT')||!registryCaptureAvailable)CFG.allChunks=false;
 for(const [id,rule]of Object.entries(featureRules)){
  if(featureAvailable(id))continue;
  if(rule.cfg)CFG[rule.cfg]=false;if(rule.auto)AUTO[rule.auto]=false;
  if(id==='t7chatrepeat')CHAT_REPEAT.enabled=false;
  const input=document.getElementById(id);if(input?.type==='checkbox')input.checked=false;
 }
}

function safeMenuAsset(asset){
 try{if(typeof client.f==='function'){const url=client.f(asset);if(typeof url==='string'&&url)return url;}}catch{}
 return 'data:image/svg+xml,'+encodeURIComponent('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="48" height="48" viewBox="0 0 48 48"><path d="M24 8 40 24 24 40 8 24Z" fill="none" stroke="#777f8b" stroke-width="2"/></svg>');
}

    function restoreRainbowNickname(){
      rainbowNick.element?.classList.remove('kiro-rainbow-name');rainbowNick.element=null;
    }
    function findLocalNickname(localId,name){
      const candidates=[...document.querySelectorAll('.identity-name')].filter(el=>el.textContent===name);
      for(const el of candidates){
        let parent=el;
        for(let depth=0;parent&&depth<4;depth++,parent=parent.parentElement){
          const key=parent.__vnode?.key;
          if(key!=null&&String(key)===String(localId))return el;
        }
      }
      // Never recolor multiple players sharing the same nickname.
      return candidates.length===1?candidates[0]:null;
    }
    function updateRainbowNickname(){
      const localId=GAME.d4.localEntityId,player=localId==null?null:GAME.cO.players[String(localId)];
      if(!CFG.rainbowNickname||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!player?.name){restoreRainbowNickname();return;}
      const element=findLocalNickname(localId,player.name);
      if(element!==rainbowNick.element){restoreRainbowNickname();rainbowNick.element=element;}
      element?.classList.add('kiro-rainbow-name');
    }

    function clearPickupColors(){
      for(const span of pickupMarked){if(span.parentNode){while(span.firstChild)span.parentNode.insertBefore(span.firstChild,span);span.remove();}}
      pickupMarked.clear();
    }
    function updatePickupColors(){
      const registry=window.__t7Registry__,id=GAME.cP?.targetId;
      const target=id==null?null:registry?.entities.get(String(id));
      if(!CFG.pickupColors||GAME.be.scene!=='gameplay'||!target?.state?.get?.('DropComponentRepl')){clearPickupColors();return;}
      for(const span of pickupMarked)if(!span.isConnected)pickupMarked.delete(span);
      for(const root of document.querySelectorAll('.interact-label__text')){
        const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);const texts=[];let node;
        while(node=walker.nextNode())if(node.nodeValue.trim()&&!node.parentElement.closest('.interact-label__key,.kiro-pickup-letters'))texts.push(node);
        for(const text of texts){const span=document.createElement('span');span.className='kiro-pickup-letters';text.parentNode.insertBefore(span,text);span.append(text);pickupMarked.add(span);}
      }
      for(const span of pickupMarked)for(let i=0;i<3;i++)span.style.setProperty('--kiro-pickup-'+(i+1),pickupPalette[i]);
    }
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const input=document.getElementById('t7pickupcolor'+(i+1));input.value=pickupPalette[i];input.oninput=()=>{pickupPalette[i]=input.value;try{localStorage.setItem('kiro-pickup-colors',JSON.stringify(pickupPalette));}catch{}updatePickupColors();};}

    const MOB_PREVIEW={runtime:null,cache:new Map(),queue:Promise.resolve()};
    function mobPreviewRuntime(){
      if(MOB_PREVIEW.runtime)return MOB_PREVIEW.runtime;
      const values=Object.values(three);
      const Renderer=values.find(v=>typeof v==='function'&&/this\.isWebGLRenderer\s*=/.test(Function.prototype.toString.call(v)));
      const Scene=values.find(v=>typeof v==='function'&&/this\.isScene\s*=/.test(Function.prototype.toString.call(v)));
      if(!Renderer||!Scene)throw Error('Preview renderer unavailable');
      const renderer=new Renderer({alpha:true,antialias:true,preserveDrawingBuffer:true});
      try{
        renderer.setPixelRatio(1);renderer.setSize(160,160,false);
        const savedCamera=window.__camera__;let camera;
        try{camera=new Camera(-1,1,1,-1,-1000,1000);}finally{window.__camera__=savedCamera;if(camera)cameraStates.delete(camera);}
        MOB_PREVIEW.runtime={renderer,camera,scene:new Scene()};return MOB_PREVIEW.runtime;
      }catch(error){renderer.dispose();throw error;}
    }
    async function renderMobPreview(type,previewDefinition=null){
      if(disposed)return null;
      if(MOB_PREVIEW.cache.has(type))return MOB_PREVIEW.cache.get(type);
      const def=previewDefinition||TRACE_MODELS[type];if(!def||!client.eL?.createSkeleton)throw Error('Models unavailable');
      const model=await client.eL.createSkeleton(def.spine,def.skin);
      if(!model)throw Error('Model unavailable');
      try{
        if(disposed)return null;
        const {renderer,camera,scene}=mobPreviewRuntime();
        model.update(0);
        const offset={x:0,y:0,set(x,y){this.x=x;this.y=y;return this;}},size={...offset};
        model.skeleton.getBounds(offset,size,[]);
        if(![offset.x,offset.y,size.x,size.y].every(Number.isFinite)||Math.max(size.x,size.y)<=0)throw Error('Empty model');
        const half=Math.max(size.x,size.y)*0.59;
        camera.left=-half;camera.right=half;camera.top=half;camera.bottom=-half;projectionOriginal.call(camera);
        model.mesh.position.set(-(offset.x+size.x/2),-(offset.y+size.y/2),0);
        for(const layer of model.batchLayers||[])layer.mesh.traverse(obj=>obj.renderOrder=layer.order);
        scene.add(model.mesh);
        try{
          renderer.setClearColor(0,0);renderer.clear();renderer.render(scene,camera);
          const url=renderer.domElement.toDataURL('image/png');MOB_PREVIEW.cache.set(type,url);return url;
        }finally{scene.remove(model.mesh);}
      }finally{model.dispose();}
    }
    function queueMobPreview(type,frame){
      MOB_PREVIEW.queue=MOB_PREVIEW.queue.then(async()=>{
        if(disposed||!frame.isConnected)return;
        try{
          const url=await renderMobPreview(type);if(disposed||!frame.isConnected||!url)return;
          const img=document.createElement('img');img.alt=type;img.src=url;img.style.cssText='width:64px;height:64px;object-fit:contain';frame.replaceChildren(img);
        }catch(error){if(!disposed&&frame.isConnected){frame.textContent='Нет превью';}console.debug('[T7] Mob preview:',type,error.message);}
      });
    }

    function filterFastBuild(){

    }
    function loadFastBuildImages(){

        try{url=client.f(asset);}catch{record.box.textContent=uiLanguage==='en'?'Waiting for game assets':'Ожидание картинок игры';continue;}

        img.onerror=()=>{record.pending=false;record.box.textContent=uiLanguage==='en'?'No image':'Нет картинки';};img.src=url;
      }
    }

    function bindingKey(e){
      if(bindingCapture){fastBuildStop();e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(e.repeat)return;if(e.code==='Escape'){bindingCapture=null;showHotkeys();return;}if(['ControlLeft','ControlRight','ShiftLeft','ShiftRight','AltLeft','AltRight','MetaLeft','MetaRight'].includes(e.code))return;
        const id=bindingCapture;
        if(e.ctrlKey||e.altKey||e.metaKey)return;
        if(id!=='menuToggle'&&e.code===hotkeys.menuToggle)return;
        if(e.code==='Backspace'&&id!=='menuToggle')delete hotkeys[id];
        else{const code=e.code==='Backspace'?'Delete':e.code;for(const k of Object.keys(hotkeys))if(hotkeys[k]===code)delete hotkeys[k];hotkeys[id]=code;}
        bindingCapture=null;saveHotkeys();showHotkeys();return;
      }
      if(e.repeat||e.ctrlKey||e.altKey||e.metaKey||e.target?.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(e.target?.tagName||'')||traceDialog.open||fastBuildDialog.open||skinDialog.open||badgeDialog.open||itemSwapDialog.open)return;
      const id=Object.keys(hotkeys).find(k=>hotkeys[k]===e.code&&bindActions.has(k));if(!id)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(id.startsWith('fastbuild:'))fastBuildHold(id.slice(10),e.code);else bindActions.get(id)();
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown',bindingKey,true);cleanups.push(()=>window.removeEventListener('keydown',bindingKey,true));

    function placeActiveBinds(){const r=activePanel.getBoundingClientRect();activePosition.x=Math.max(8,Math.min(activePosition.x,Math.max(8,innerWidth-r.width-8)));activePosition.y=Math.max(8,Math.min(activePosition.y,Math.max(8,innerHeight-r.height-8)));activePanel.style.left=activePosition.x+'px';activePanel.style.top=activePosition.y+'px';}
    function activeBindingRows(){
      const rows=[];
      for(const [id,code] of Object.entries(hotkeys)){
        if(!code||!bindActions.has(id))continue;
        if(id.startsWith('fastbuild:')){if(CFG.fastBuild){const item=BUILDING_ALERT_CATALOG.find(x=>x.type===id.slice(10));rows.push({title:uiText(item?.label||id.slice(10)),key:code.replace(/^Key/,'').replace(/^Digit/,'')});}continue;}
        const input=document.getElementById(id);
        const enabled=id==='zoomToggle'?CFG.zoomEnabled:id==='t7autotraps'?autoTrapsEnabled():input?.type==='checkbox'&&input.checked;
        if(!enabled)continue;
        const title=id==='zoomToggle'?'Зум':input.closest('.t7card')?.querySelector('.t7title')?.textContent.trim();
        if(title)rows.push({title,key:code.replace(/^Key/,'').replace(/^Digit/,'').replace(/^Numpad/,'Num ').replace('Space','Пробел').replace('Arrow','')});
      }
      return rows;
    }
    function updateActiveBinds(){
      activePanel.hidden=!activeBindsBox.checked;if(activePanel.hidden)return;
      const rows=activeBindingRows(),snapshot=JSON.stringify(rows);
      if(snapshot!==activeSnapshot){activeSnapshot=snapshot;const list=activePanel.querySelector('.ab-list');list.replaceChildren();
        for(const row of rows){const line=document.createElement('div');line.className='ab-row';const name=document.createElement('span'),key=document.createElement('kbd');name.textContent=row.title;key.textContent=row.key;line.append(name,key);list.append(line);}
        if(!rows.length){const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='ab-empty';empty.textContent='Нет активных биндов';list.append(empty);}
      }
      if(!activeDrag)placeActiveBinds();
    }
    activeBindsBox.onchange=()=>{updateActiveBinds();saveActiveBinds();};
    const activeHandle=activePanel.querySelector('.ab-head');
    function endActiveDrag(e){if(!activeDrag||e.pointerId!==activeDrag.id)return;activeDrag=null;if(activeHandle.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId))activeHandle.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);saveActiveBinds();}
    listen(activeHandle,'pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button!==0||activeDrag)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const r=activePanel.getBoundingClientRect();activeDrag={id:e.pointerId,dx:e.clientX-r.left,dy:e.clientY-r.top};activeHandle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);});
    listen(activeHandle,'pointermove',e=>{if(!activeDrag||e.pointerId!==activeDrag.id)return;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();activePosition={x:e.clientX-activeDrag.dx,y:e.clientY-activeDrag.dy};placeActiveBinds();});
    listen(activeHandle,'pointerup',endActiveDrag);listen(activeHandle,'pointercancel',endActiveDrag);listen(activeHandle,'lostpointercapture',e=>{if(activeDrag?.id===e.pointerId){activeDrag=null;saveActiveBinds();}});
    listen(window,'resize',()=>{if(!activePanel.hidden){placeActiveBinds();saveActiveBinds();}});
    const activeBindsInterval=setInterval(updateActiveBinds,100);updateActiveBinds();

    const languageNodes=new WeakMap();
    const languageSelector='#t7menu,#t7trace,#t7buildingchoices,#t7activebindspanel,#t7-night-timer,#t7fastbuilddialog,#t7skindialog,#t7badgedialog,#t7itemswapdialog';
    function localizeNode(node){
      if(node.nodeType===3){
        if(!node.parentElement?.closest(languageSelector)||node.parentElement.closest('[data-language],[data-profile-name]'))return;
        let saved=languageNodes.get(node);if(!saved||node.nodeValue!==saved.last)saved={source:node.nodeValue,last:node.nodeValue};
        const next=uiText(saved.source);if(node.nodeValue!==next)node.nodeValue=next;saved.last=next;languageNodes.set(node,saved);return;
      }
      if(node.nodeType!==1)return;
      if(node.closest(languageSelector))for(const attr of ['title','placeholder','aria-label','alt']){
        if(!node.hasAttribute(attr))continue;
        const key='data-t7-original-'+attr,current=node.getAttribute(attr),previous=node.getAttribute(key);
        if(previous===null)node.setAttribute(key,current);
        const next=uiText(previous??current);if(next!==current)node.setAttribute(attr,next);
      }
      for(const child of node.childNodes)localizeNode(child);
    }
    function applyLanguage(){
      for(const root of document.querySelectorAll(languageSelector))localizeNode(root);
      for(const b of document.querySelectorAll('[data-language]'))b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(b.dataset.language===uiLanguage));
    }
    for(const b of document.querySelectorAll('[data-language]')){
      listen(b,'pointerdown',e=>e.stopPropagation());
      b.onclick=()=>{uiLanguage=b.dataset.language;try{localStorage.setItem('t7-language',uiLanguage);}catch{}applyLanguage();};
    }
    const languageObserver=new MutationObserver(records=>{
      for(const r of records){
        if(r.type==='characterData')localizeNode(r.target);
        else if(r.type==='attributes'&&r.target.closest(languageSelector))localizeNode(r.target);
        else for(const node of r.addedNodes)if(node.nodeType===1?(node.matches(languageSelector)||node.closest(languageSelector)):node.parentElement?.closest(languageSelector))localizeNode(node);
      }
    });
    languageObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['title','placeholder','aria-label','alt']});
    cleanups.push(()=>languageObserver.disconnect());applyLanguage();

    // User profiles contain settings only; equipment transactions and cooldowns stay live.
    const profilesKey='kiro-profiles-v1';let profiles=[],selectedProfile=null;

    const profileStyle=document.createElement('style');profileStyle.textContent=`

#t7menu .kp-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:16px}#t7menu .kp-head strong{font-size:17px;color:#d9efe3}#t7menu .kp-caption{font-size:11px;color:#8d9d97;margin-top:4px}#t7menu .kp-count{border:1px solid #40554b;border-radius:20px;padding:4px 9px;color:#a9dbc3;font-size:11px}

    document.head.append(profileStyle);mn.querySelector('.t7grid').append(profilePanel);cleanups.push(()=>{profilePanel.remove();profileStyle.remove();});
    const profileControls=()=>[...mn.querySelectorAll('input[id],select[id]')].filter(el=>!profilePanel.contains(el)&&!['t7search'].includes(el.id)&&!['search','file','button','submit'].includes(el.type));
    const primitiveSettings=obj=>Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(obj).filter(([,v])=>['boolean','number','string'].includes(typeof v)));
    const profileStatus=text=>{profilePanel.querySelector('.kp-status').textContent=uiText(text);};
    const profileData=()=>({format:'KiroHack-config',version:1,cfg:primitiveSettings(CFG),auto:primitiveSettings(AUTO),hatPercent:HATS.percent,
      controls:Object.fromEntries(profileControls().map(el=>[el.id,el.type==='checkbox'?el.checked:el.value])),
      trace:{...TRACE_SELECT,types:[...TRACE_SELECT.types]},buildings:[...selectedBuildings],palette:[...pickupPalette],hotkeys:{...hotkeys},language:uiLanguage,
      activePosition:{...activePosition},menuPosition:{left:mn.style.left,top:mn.style.top},mapPosition:{left:resourcePanel.style.left,top:resourcePanel.style.top,right:resourcePanel.style.right},chat:{text:CHAT_REPEAT.text,channel:CHAT_REPEAT.channel,effect:CHAT_REPEAT.effect,enabled:CHAT_REPEAT.enabled}});
    function validateProfile(data){
      if(!data||data.format!=='KiroHack-config'||data.version!==1||!data.cfg||!data.auto||!data.controls||!data.trace||!Array.isArray(data.trace.types)||!Array.isArray(data.buildings)||!Array.isArray(data.palette)||!data.hotkeys)throw Error('Неверный формат конфига');
      for(const [target,values]of [[CFG,data.cfg],[AUTO,data.auto]])for(const [key,value]of Object.entries(values)){
        if(!Object.hasOwn(target,key))continue;
        if(typeof value!==typeof target[key]||typeof value==='number'&&(!Number.isFinite(value)||Math.abs(value)>1000000)||typeof value==='string'&&value.length>500)throw Error('Неверные настройки');
      }
      for(const values of [data.trace.types,data.buildings])if(values.length>1000||values.some(v=>typeof v!=='string'||v.length>80))throw Error('Неверный выбор объектов');
      if(data.palette.length!==3||data.palette.some(v=>typeof v!=='string'||!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(v)))throw Error('Неверные цвета');
      if(!Number.isFinite(data.hatPercent)||data.hatPercent<1||data.hatPercent>40)throw Error('Неверный порог ХП');
      const elements=new Map(profileControls().map(el=>[el.id,el]));
      for(const [id,value]of Object.entries(data.controls)){
        const el=elements.get(id);if(!el)continue;
        if(el.type==='checkbox'){if(typeof value!=='boolean')throw Error('Неверный переключатель');continue;}
        if(typeof value!=='string'||value.length>500)throw Error('Неверное значение');
        if(el.tagName==='SELECT'&&![...el.options].some(o=>o.value===value))throw Error('Неизвестный режим');
        if(el.type==='color'&&!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(value))throw Error('Неверный цвет');
        if(['number','range'].includes(el.type)&&(!Number.isFinite(Number(value))||el.min!==''&&Number(value)<Number(el.min)||el.max!==''&&Number(value)>Number(el.max)))throw Error('Настройка вне диапазона');
      }
      for(const [key,min,max]of [['meatCustom',1,4096],['visualBadgeId',0,4096],['visualSkinId',0,4096],['followDistance',1,6],['blockAngle',0,360],['zoom',1.2,5],['apPackets',1,30],['apDelay',0,400],['apRadius',0,10]])if(data.cfg[key]!=null&&(data.cfg[key]<min||data.cfg[key]>max))throw Error('Настройка вне диапазона');
      return data;
    }
    function applyProfile(data){
      validateProfile(data);if(GEAR.tx)throw Error('Дождись завершения смены экипировки');
      // Restore through the existing handlers, so observers and feature cleanup are retained.
      const controls=profileControls(),checks=controls.filter(el=>el.type==='checkbox'),previousChecks=new Map(checks.map(el=>[el.id,el.checked]));
      for(const el of checks)if(el.checked&&data.controls[el.id]===false){el.checked=false;el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}
      for(const [key,value]of Object.entries(data.cfg))if(Object.hasOwn(CFG,key)&&typeof value!=='boolean')CFG[key]=value;
      followRefresh();
      for(const [key,value]of Object.entries(data.auto))if(typeof value!=='boolean')AUTO[key]=value;
      for(const el of controls){if(el.disabled||el.type==='checkbox'||!Object.hasOwn(data.controls,el.id))continue;el.value=data.controls[el.id];el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}
      TRACE_SELECT.types=new Set(data.trace.types);for(const key of ['friends','enemies','scaffolds'])if(typeof data.trace[key]==='boolean')TRACE_SELECT[key]=data.trace[key];saveTraceSelection();
      selectedBuildings.clear();for(const type of data.buildings)selectedBuildings.add(type);renderBuildingChoices();renderTraceChoices();
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){pickupPalette[i]=data.palette[i];document.getElementById('t7pickupcolor'+(i+1)).value=pickupPalette[i];}localStorage.setItem('kiro-pickup-colors',JSON.stringify(pickupPalette));
      HATS.percent=data.hatPercent;document.getElementById('t7hatpercent').value=HATS.percent;document.getElementById('t7hatpercentvalue').textContent=HATS.percent+'%';
      hotkeys=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(data.hotkeys).filter(([key,value])=>(key==='menuToggle'||bindActions.has(key))&&typeof value==='string'&&/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]{0,30}$/.test(value)));if(!hotkeys.menuToggle)hotkeys.menuToggle='Delete';saveHotkeys();showHotkeys();
      if(['ru','en'].includes(data.language)){uiLanguage=data.language;localStorage.setItem('t7-language',uiLanguage);applyLanguage();}
      for(const [key,value]of Object.entries(data.cfg))if(typeof value==='boolean')CFG[key]=value;
      for(const [key,value]of Object.entries(data.auto))if(typeof value==='boolean')AUTO[key]=value;
      for(const el of checks)if(Object.hasOwn(data.controls,el.id)){el.checked=!el.disabled&&data.controls[el.id];if(el.checked!==previousChecks.get(el.id)&&el.checked)el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}
      if(data.activePosition&&Number.isFinite(data.activePosition.x)&&Number.isFinite(data.activePosition.y)){activePosition={x:data.activePosition.x,y:data.activePosition.y};placeActiveBinds();saveActiveBinds();}

    function saveNew(name,data){
      name=name.trim().slice(0,48);if(!name)throw Error('Введи название конфига');if(profiles.length>=100)throw Error('Достигнут лимит: 100 конфигов');if(profiles.some(p=>p.name.toLocaleLowerCase()===name.toLocaleLowerCase()))throw Error('Такое имя уже есть — выбери перезапись');

    }

        if(action==='save'){saveNew(name,profileData());profileStatus('Конфиг сохранён');}
        if(action==='load'&&item){applyProfile(item.data);profileStatus('Конфиг загружен');}
        if(action==='overwrite'&&item){const fresh=validateProfile(profileData());persistProfiles(profiles.map(p=>p.id===item.id?{...p,data:fresh,updated:Date.now()}:p));renderProfiles();profileStatus('Конфиг перезаписан');}
        if(action==='delete'&&item){if(deleteArmed!==item.id){deleteArmed=item.id;profileStatus('Нажми «Удалить» ещё раз для подтверждения');return;}persistProfiles(profiles.filter(p=>p.id!==item.id));selectedProfile=null;deleteArmed=null;renderProfiles();profileStatus('Конфиг удалён');}

        if(action==='import')profilePanel.querySelector('.kp-file').click();

      }catch(error){profileStatus(error.message);}

            const origin=localTracerOrigin();
            if(!origin){
                document.getElementById('t7l').textContent='Игрок: ожидание позиции';
                return;
            }
            const W=cv.width,H=cv.height,CX=origin.sx,CY=origin.sy;
            for(const[id,p]of Object.entries(pl||{})){
                 if(!p?.nameAnchor||!p.name||id===lid||!(TRACE_SELECT.friends&&TRACE_SELECT.enemies))continue;
                const entity=window.__t7Registry__?.entities.get(String(id));
                if(entity&&tracerActualKind(entity)!=='Player')continue;
                const ax=p.nameAnchor.x,ay=p.nameAnchor.y;
                if(ax===0&&ay===0)continue;
                const sc=w2s(ax,ay-1.2);
                if(!Number.isFinite(sc.sx)||!Number.isFinite(sc.sy))continue;
                if(sc.sx<-500||sc.sx>W+500||sc.sy<-500||sc.sy>H+500)continue;
                 const col=CFG.ec;
                ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(CX,CY);ctx.lineTo(sc.sx,sc.sy);
                ctx.strokeStyle=col;ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.globalAlpha=.75;ctx.stroke();ctx.globalAlpha=1;
                ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(sc.sx,sc.sy,4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.fill();
                ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.font='bold 11px Arial';ctx.textAlign='center';
                ctx.shadowColor='#000';ctx.shadowBlur=3;
                ctx.fillText(p.name,sc.sx,sc.sy-7);ctx.shadowBlur=0;
            }
            const targets=collectTracerTargets();
            const styles={
                Heartstone:['#ff44ff','Камень душ'],
                ForeignHeartStone:['#ff44ff','Камень душ'],
                PrivateChest:['#ffee44','Личный сундук'],
                GoldenCow:['#ffaa00','Золотая корова']
            };
            const counts={heart:0,chest:0,cow:0,building:0};
            for(const target of targets){
                const[color,label]=styles[target.kind]||['#66ddff',tracerLabel(target.kind)];
                if(target.building)counts.building++;
                else counts[target.kind==='GoldenCow'?'cow':target.kind==='PrivateChest'?'chest':'heart']++;
                const point=w2s(target.x,target.y);
                if(!Number.isFinite(point.sx)||!Number.isFinite(point.sy))continue;
                const dx=point.sx-CX,dy=point.sy-CY;
                let factor=1;
                if(dx>0)factor=Math.min(factor,(W-12-CX)/dx);
                if(dx<0)factor=Math.min(factor,(12-CX)/dx);
                if(dy>0)factor=Math.min(factor,(H-12-CY)/dy);
                if(dy<0)factor=Math.min(factor,(12-CY)/dy);
                factor=Math.max(0,factor);
                const sx=CX+dx*factor,sy=CY+dy*factor;

            }
            document.getElementById('t7targets').textContent=
                'Целей: '+targets.length+' | Строятся: '+counts.building+
                (window.__t7Registry__?'':' | Ожидание списка существ');
        } catch(e) { console.error('[T7] draw failed',e); }
    }
    _r(drawTracers);
    console.log('[T7] init: full code with building owners');
}
if(document.body)init();else listen(document,'DOMContentLoaded',init);
console.log('[T7] v7.251 shared outbound packet limiter');
})().catch(error=>{window.__T7_LOCAL_API__?.dispose();console.error('[T7] installation failed',error);
const notice=document.createElement('div');notice.textContent='KiroHack: ошибка запуска. '+error.message;
notice.style.cssText='position:fixed;top:16px;right:16px;max-width:420px;padding:14px;background:#24262e;color:#ffb5b5;border:1px solid #755;border-radius:8px;z-index:2147483647;font:13px Arial';
(document.body||document.documentElement).append(notice);notice.addEventListener('click',()=>notice.remove());});
